# Fantasy Realm 3D — notes

## log
- v2.39.96 (2026-10-04, fannar22 12:25-12:27 "drag it over or trade into another inventory slot", "same with putting item into backpack", "swapping places within backpack", "rename the bag section to Inventory"): dragEnd gains a bag->bag branch (any #slots/#packSlots li) calling bagSwap(a,b): empty = move, other item = trade places, same stackable = join up to it.stack (rest stays, full stack says so); a backpack/packw never goes into a pocket (i>=INV_SLOTS). Character window h3 "Bag" -> "Inventory"; help line mentions the drag. Probe hm/pfr96.js drives real PointerEvents on the slot buttons (move, swap, stack, full stack, into/within/out of the pack, backpack refused).
- v2.39.95 (2026-10-04, fannar22 12:06-12:21: the "flying blue face" is Falsity, "vanish completely" on Brutal): engine DIFF gains quiet (diffApply copies d.quiet); falsityTick returns early with o.on=0 under DIFF.quiet, so Brutal never meets it and an already-met Falsity hides while on Brutal and returns on the other difficulties (S.quests.falsity kept). Brutal hint mentions it. Probe hm/pfr95.js (keep F.step loops short: ~1.5 s per step headless).

- v2.39.91 (2026-10-04, fannar22 12:02 "option window half the size and be scrollable and be resizeable"): .optwin height calc(50% - 2.1rem) (phones 50vh), overflow hidden + resize:both (min 15rem x 13rem), the visible .optpage scrolls (min-height:0) between the tabs and the button row. The dragged size is kept in localStorage fr3d-optsize (ResizeObserver, only when style width/height are set) and cleared by "put windows back". optTabGo no longer pins a growing min-height (it blocked shrinking). Probe hm/pfr91.js.
- v2.39.90 (2026-10-04, fannar22): wolves hunt alone. A pack no longer follows its lead after a deer; each wolf that sees one within 16 m goes after a deer no other wolf is chasing (engine huntStep). Tested in a separate E.makeRun with S.prey set by hand: lead and a packmate took two different deer.
- v2.39.89 (2026-10-04, fannar22): a hero sheet remembers the base its stats were counted from (prog.bs, set by progState and every progNorm). When a race or class base changes, saved heroes follow it and keep every point put in (sta = new base + (saved - old base), clamped). Sheets from before take today's base. The old goblin rules (>=50, ==46) only run on sheets without bs. Health and mana already come from the class tables on load. So: change RACES.*.base freely now, no migration needed.
- v2.39.88 (2026-10-04, fannar22): start over resets hunger and thirst (hgClear: the slot's record in PKEY-hunger is keyed by slot + name/race/class, which start over keeps, so the old needs came back).
- v2.39.87 (2026-10-04, fannar22): fixed a level-1 goblin warrior showing 54 max health instead of 30. A goblin loaded under v2.39.83 got clamped from 50+ to the new cap 46 and saved there, which v2.39.84's ">= 50" rule missed (4 hp per Stamina over base). Now a one-time fix per sheet: a goblin at 46 without the gs40 flag goes to 40 (past level 1 the 6 come back as points). New goblins start with gs40, so a legit 46 later stays. Lesson: when lowering a race base, the clamp to base+6 runs and saves before any migration sees the old value.
- v2.39.86 (2026-10-04, fannar22): every new hero (any race, class or difficulty; the hero maker, a scratch hero and start over) starts with 20 Bread and 20 Bottles of Milk, like Monsters & Memories. Bread and Milk now stack to 20 so the kit takes 2 of the 8 slots. Wrapper on E.heroState placed before the roster load; heroNorm uses the engine's own heroState, so loaded heroes get nothing extra.
- v2.39.85 (2026-10-04, fannar22): the difficulty dial now sets the helping hands when you pick it (Options or the World Overlord): Gentle and Hardcore turn on "camera turns to who you fight or talk to" and "a strike starts auto-attack"; Brutal keeps the camera turn, switches auto-attack off, and hides your corpse (no map pin, no arrow or distance in the corpse hint, just "somewhere you fell" until you are within 3 m). The two checkboxes still change afterwards; loading a save never touches them (diffAids runs only on a pick).
- v2.39.84 (2026-10-04, fannar22 04:23 "Its say it at 56, so make sure it 40"): progNorm stores stats on the sheet and only clamps them to [base, base+6], so made goblins kept 56 (made at 50 +6 points). After the clamp: a goblin sheet with sta >= 50 goes to the base (40) and refunds sta minus the base it was made with (60 if >= 60, else 50) as points. Idempotent (result < 50). Probe hm/pfr84.js: 56/0 -> 40/6, 50/2 -> 40/2, 60/0 -> 40/0, 66/1 -> 40/7, 43 and humans untouched, twice = once.
- v2.39.83 (2026-10-04, fannar22 04:22 "can we make playerable goblin have 40 sta"): RACES.goblin.base.sta 50 -> 40 (the race card reads 'Stamina 40'). WRONG claim at first: made heroes keep their stored sta (see v2.39.84).
- v2.39.82 (2026-10-04, fannar22 04:20 "can we add 10 more save slots to the game"): HERO_SLOTS 8 -> 18. rosterNorm loops HERO_SLOTS and heroNorm(undefined) is null, so an 8-slot roster loads into the first 8. The select overlay already scrolls (overflow auto); probe hm/pfr82.js at 390x844: 18 slots, '0 of 18 heroes', last slot reachable, no errors.

- v2.39.74 (2026-10-01, fannar22): MUSIC VOLUME (fannar 19:44 "the music should at least start at 15 percent volume every time someone new enter the game"). OPTS.musVol (number, default 15; saved players without it get 15 too) and a range slider #optMusVol under the Music switch in the Settings tab, readout #optMusVolV in tabular figures with a fixed min-width so nothing shifts as it changes; musVolApply() sets MUS.vol=.55*v/100 (100 % = the old level) and eases the live bus there unless hushed (musInit and the hush release already read MUS.vol). Probe mv1a: readout 15 % -> 40 %, saved, readout/slider boxes identical at 40 and 100, no errors.
- v2.39.73 (2026-10-01, fannar22 + termiimret): OPTIONS TABS + GAME DIFFICULTY (fannar 19:26 "2 taps 1 says keys and 1 say Game Difficulty"; termiimret 19:09 a difficulty dial, 19:10 "controllable suffering"; fannar 19:12 hardcore is the realm's own way). Options window: role=tablist .opttabs with otKeys/otSet/otDiff over pages opKeys (keys + windows help), opSet (all the switches), opDiff (three radios name=optDiff); arrow keys move between tabs; the last tab is remembered while the page is open; optTabGo pins the window's min-height to the tallest page seen since opening so the tabs and buttons never jump (reset on open). Engine: DIFF={hurt,regen,corpse} next to REGEN, exported as E.DIFF; hurt() multiplies the blow by DIFF.hurt before the AC math, fxTick DoTs too (min 1), regen interval REGEN.every/DIFF.regen, and corpseDrop only when DIFF.corpse. Page: OPTS.diff ('hard' default; OPTS now loads any field whose type matches the default, so strings work), DIFFS gentle {.5, 2x regen, keep your things on death} / hard {1,1,corpse} / brutal {1.5, .5x regen, corpse}; diffApply() on load and on change, with a state-line toast. t305 = difficulty suite (blows 3/2/5 from a goblin, regen 5/10/2 in 10 s, corpse vs none). t289-t303 now fail only their "engine untouched" assert (by design: this one changes the engine) + t292's old failure; the 14 latest makeRun suites pass. NEXT: the World Overlord NPC (termiimret) can sit on top of this, e.g. talking to him opens the Game Difficulty tab or offers a harsher pact.
- v2.39.72 (2026-10-01, pushedbutton, fannar agreed): CAMERA FOCUS (pushedbutton 18:28 "It might just flow a little if the camera followed the focus, say if you were talking to someone or engaging in battle", fannar "yup"). CFOC + cfocYaw(h) before camFollow: with OPTS.camFocus on (default; Options checkbox optFocus "Camera turns to who you fight or talk to"), a target that is a foe within 14 m while auto-attack is on or you struck/were hit in the last 4 s, or a friendly NPC within 6.5 m while you stand still, gives a goal yaw = bearing to it + side*(.42 if d<4 else .3), side kept from where the camera already was so it never swings across; camFollow eases camYaw to it at k 2.2 (x.6 while moving) and skips the normal lazy follow. No turn when moving away from the target (cos<.2), in first person, rowing, dead, keyboard tank steering (camTight), or self/pet targets. A drag of your own (cam.hold>0) sets CFOC.skip for that target key: the camera leaves you alone until you pick another target. NPC position from gvPos (the 3D object), else E.targetInfo. Probe (cf1a): talk offset .42, after-drag 2.0 unchanged, fight -.36 at 4 m, option off = no turn, no errors. PROBE NOTE: in headless SwiftShader, real-time frames after a scenario at Enzo/the forest crawl (follow-up evals time out, Debugger.enable gets no reply = main thread waiting on the GPU process); same on v2.39.70, so not this change: freeze + draw inside the same eval, then a 3 s wait still yields the shot (cf8a). t292 still fails as on c177.

TRADES=['throw','smith','tailor','cook'] next to twTeaches: every class trainer (Enzo, Bob, Grudge, the Goblin, Necromancer and Shaman Trainers) lists them under a 'trades' heading after Dual Wield, for skill points like any skill (same level cap, still rise with use). trTx(k) gives the current value line (burn %, stone save %, stitch %, dagger damage) since engine skillText has no entry for them; trainSk zero-fills a missing P.sk[k]; each trainer says one line (TRQ) the first time you train a trade per session. - v2.39.67 (2026-10-01, fannar22): TWO FIXES. Smith's shop sign flickered when the camera turned (z-fighting: the front-wall boards alternate z offsets of .012 and the sign plane sat at hd+.012): the sign now hangs at hd+.05. Doug's hall floor had a seam from the door to the counter and boards that didn't line up: the floor west of the flagstones and the strip east of them were two loops on different z grids (east one started at .8) with a .02 gap at local x .9; now one loop on a .42 grid, each row either a short board beside the flagstones (z<.78) or a full-width board in front of them.

SEA.m moved to (-76,0), 8 m off the pier end where the seabed's second slope starts (-70 -> -85). seaCulBuild + the .cul stock circles are gone; seaTunBuild: mossy boulder outcrop, a 9-voussoir arch facing the pier (+x) with a black inside (fogged), a grate open on one hinge, 7 weed cones (SEA.weed, swayed in diveView), an anchor at (-69.6,2.0) and a torus chain along the bottom to the mouth. Way in: armO (d>3) then d<1.3 and feet (h.y-DIVE.d, seaPost runs before divePost) within 1.05 of W.height. Way out (chamber arch) puts you at mouth+1.7 east, yaw pi/2, DIVE.pend = depth .55 above the bottom, applied by divePost when not 'tp'. Hint shows when swimming within 11 m. Port map label 'the sea tunnel'. Probe bp9.js.
- v2.39.65 (2026-10-01, fannar22): DIVING: DIVE module before seaPost (divePost called at the end of pswPost, so it runs after the engine step every frame). While S.swim, a left press held still 250 ms on the canvas (a drag >10 px before that stays a camera drag) sinks you 1.5 m/s; release keeps DIVE.d; Space held (or the touch JUMP button held) rises 2.4 m/s. h.y -= DIVE.d each step, clamped a hand above W.height (PSW pools/pond: surface-1.85). Head under past .45 m: breath 30 s (+30 s at max Swimming), #brRow ten bubbles under the health bar (grid-column 1/-1), refills in 2.5 s at the surface. Out of air: S.fx.drown (E.AFFLICT.drown, dmg = 10 % maxHp every 1 s, t kept at 1 while drowning, deleted on surfacing) so the engine's fxTick does the fall + corpse. diveView in drawFrame after chunkTick: camera under the surface (sea, pools, pond) -> teal fog near .4 far 6-15, sky hidden, #uwFx vignette over the canvas, breath bubbles (Points, dropTex) rise to the surface. Third-person hy -= DIVE.d*.5 so the camera follows you down; FP eye clamp is h.y+1.6-1.5*DIVE.d. __F3.DIVE getter for probes (bp8.js).

- v2.39.22 (2026-09-30, fannar22 10:05-10:07 "translate text within Fantasy Realm to anyone regardless what language they prefer"; arianmartiz writes Spanish): options -> Language (18 languages, own key 'fr3d-lang'). TR module translates npc/sys/net chat lines (the last text node of the li; original in li.dataset.o + title, class 'tr') and the quest line, via POST text.pollinations.ai/openai {model:'openai', reasoning_effort:'low'} — without 'low' the GPT-OSS reasoning model took ~70 s for 8 lines, with it ~5 s. 8 lines per request, one in flight, 2.5 s gap, 60 s abort, backoff 6 s x fails (max 60 s), 3 tries then the line rests in English; answers of the wrong length are thrown away; quick repeats give {} or http 402 (anonymous rate limit) -> backoff. Cache per language in localStorage 'fr3d-tr-<lang>' (1200 newest). Switching language restores/re-asks the last 40 lines. Non-English browser gets a one-time hint. The prompt lists names that must stay (A King was "Un Rey" before). Typed words to NPCs are still read in English. TR.err keeps the last error. Test t284. Snapshot pre128.html.

- v2.39.15 (2026-09-30, fannar22 07:40 "same with slot within the bank", 07:41 "it only fit in player inventory and bank slots"): BACKPACKS IN THE BANK VAULT (stage 2 of the backpack ask). A Backpack in one of the vault's own 8 slots (0..7) gives it a shelf of 8 more (blocks after E.BANK_SLOTS, same positional model as the pockets), up to 8 bags = 72 slots. packWrap's bankStore routes 'backpack' and 'packw' to vaultStore: a worn one needs an EMPTY pocket block (that block is spliced, the pack save dropped if it was the last, like selling); a bag goes only into a free main vault slot ("never on a shelf"; "Eight bags in the vault already" at 8). A new bankTake wrap routes a vault 'backpack' to vaultTake: needs an EMPTY shelf when bags <= shelves (the last empty one is spliced, nothing else moves) and a free main bag slot (packFree). The engine's bankNorm cuts a loaded vault to 8, so the shelves have their own save PKEY+'-vault' per hero (same identity/level guards as the pack save): vaultApply (from packApply, once per run via r.vtOn, only while the vault is still 8) restores min(8, ceil(len/8)) shelves dropping class kit/bags/unknown ids and capping stacks, then gives every vault bag its shelf; vaultSave (from packSave) writes the slices past 8 or drops the record. Vault window: headings "The vault eight slots" and "Backpack N a shelf of eight" (vaultHead, li.pkh). bankDo stays quiet after our own lines (why 'said'; the old packw refusal used to add "there is nothing there"), the full-vault line hints at bags. CSS: .bankch wraps and its h3 small no longer squeezes "Coins in the vault" into a column. Headless: 2 bags stored -> 24 slots, milk/flask on shelf 1, fish on shelf 2, take refused while both shelves hold something, ok after emptying one. t278. DEPLOY CHECKLIST: t195..t278 all pass, snapshot pre120, next stamp v2.39.16.
- v2.39.14 (2026-09-30, fannar22 07:40 "you should be able to buy backpack for each slot within inventory, same with slot within the bank, so give player no limit on buying a backpack", 07:41 "I don't want backpack fit in backpack it only fit in player inventory and bank slots"): AS MANY BACKPACKS AS YOUR BAG HAS SLOTS (stage 1: the bag; the bank vault is stage 2). A Merchant sells one whenever the bag's own 8 slots have a free one (packFree; bought backpacks go only there, never a pocket), so up to 8 ("a tortoise made of tortoises" at 8). Pockets are blocks of 8 after the bag: block k = slots 8+8k..15+8k; packBlocks/packWorn/packCount. Worn backpacks stay ONE id ('packw'), so a block is not tied to a particular bag: strapping one on appends a block (packGrow), or takes over pockets a bag on your corpse left behind (after death corpseStrip keeps the slots, empty) when worn < blocks; clicking a worn one with worn > blocks gives it a block ("back on"), otherwise toggles the window. Selling one needs an EMPTY block (any, the last empty goes; "Empty one of them first") and splices only that block, so nothing else moves; worn > blocks sells without a splice. packApply restores 1..8 blocks from the record (ceil(len/8)), heals bags lost to an old update (blocks - worn in bag/record - 'packw' on corpses) into free bag slots, returns false when every bag is on a corpse (as before). packFix (every frame, after corpseTw) moves a backpack/packw found in a pocket back into the bag, swapping with the first non-backpack there ("your Fish takes its pocket"); a pocket backpack refuses packUse. Pack window: li.pkh headings "Backpack 1..N" when N>1 (a block without a bag says "its bag is on your corpse"/"no bag holds these"), title "Backpacks N × eight pockets" (packTitle). Item texts: no more "one per hero". Headless: 3 bought for 18g, 32 slots, three headings; Mira refuses while all three hold something, sells the emptied middle one: 24 slots, bread in bags 1 and 3 untouched; phone 390 scrolls. t277; t256 (two old one-per-hero asserts rewritten), t274 (frame regex). DEPLOY CHECKLIST: t195..t277 all pass, snapshot pre119, next stamp v2.39.15. TODO stage 2: backpacks in the bank vault add vault slots (engine bank arrays + a page save like PACK).
- v2.39.13 (2026-09-30, fannar22 07:25 "when you drag coins into bank or from the bank ... choose how much", 07:26 "click the amount you want to buy at a time", 07:33 "if you don't have enough money the Merchant will tell ya so", 07:35 "a scroll line instead to choose between 1 to 20 item at a time at merchant", 07:36 "same with banker as well when it comes to coins", 07:38 "remove purse since we don't need it anymore we have coins slot"): HOW MANY. Shop: a range slider #shopAmtR 1..20 (shopAmt, label "buy N at a time") in a .bankch row beside the For sale heading, shown only when the keeper's stock has something that stacks; a stackable's button reads "buy N · total" and calls shopBuyN(id,n). shopBuyN is ALL OR NOTHING: purse < price*n -> the merchant says 'N × Bread is X. your purse has Y. count again, friend.' and nothing is bought; the bag can't take n (invStack on a JSON copy) -> 'your bag has room for K more'; otherwise it calls run.buy n times (the page amWrap/rsWrap dispatch, every keeper's own rules), swaps the page say() out during the loop and splices the engine's S.events from the batch, then keeps ONE line (the last) + " · N bought for X". Returns why 'said' for the refusals, and trade() now stays quiet on 'said'. Non-stackables always buy 1. Bank: the 1/10/all chips became a slider #bankAmtR 1..100 (label "move N") + the all button, which toggles between all and the slider's number; bankUI syncs the slider/label/'off' class. bankAmt is what bankCoins, the drag ghost and the change buttons already read. The HUD purse chip (.hmeta .bar.w) is hidden (hidden attr + CSS); the #purse span still updates because the frame's save-on-coins-change hook compares it. Headless: 5 bread for 1g in one chat line, 20 refused with 2g, bank slider 37 -> 37s in the vault, all toggles, phone 390 fits. t276. DEPLOY CHECKLIST: t195..t276 all pass, snapshot pre118, next stamp v2.39.14.

Not a regression: the engine's lootOpen takes a body's coins the moment you kneel and sinks a body with nothing else, and goblins only carry a rusty sword 25 % of the time, so 3 goblins in 4 never showed a window (wolves almost always had meat/pelt). Page-only: lootGo resolves the body itself (corpseNear/bodyNear for L and the pill); a mob body with coins>0, hero alive and within E.LOOT.r gets S.looting=i + S.lootAt set by the page and the window opens WITHOUT calling run.lootOpen, so the coins stay on the body (the engine's step keeps a body while coins>0). lootUI prepends a full-width gold pocket li.lootcoins > button.lootcoin ("1 silver 2 copper", CSS coin dot, no emoji: headless showed tofu for U+1FA99); clicking it (lootCoinTake) or loot all calls run.lootOpen(i), which takes the coins with the usual chat line and sinks the body if nothing else is left (lootSync then closes the window). lootSig includes the pending coins. Your own corpse and coin-less bodies go through run.lootOpen as before. Wolves: FG.more wolf meat .5, ruined pelt .5 (t259 updated). t273 = the coin pocket (vm stand-in for lootOpen). Re-checked in r128 source: the only uncaught undefined.matrixWorld paths are Object3D.updateMatrixWorld/updateWorldMatrix with parent undefined (page never sets .parent), light.target (only the sun, default target) and SkinnedMesh.boneTransform on a missing bone (pickAt's raycast is in try/catch since v1.89.2; corpseAt/ovenAt/fgAt only hit plain meshes); a headless scan found 248 skinned meshes, all skinIndex < bones.length, no undefined parents. No page change for it; don't chase it again unless the count grows. The one new error, "unhandled rejection: Failed to start the audio device" (a machine with no working sound device), fixed: AudioContext resume()/suspend() promises go through acQuiet (a no-op .catch) in audioWake and the visibilitychange hush; the sound object is kept so it can try again. t270 (vm with a fake AudioContext that rejects). - v2.39.7 (2026-09-30, fannar22 01:51 "water and milk, and bread bring mana back if you go to long without food", "bread, water and Milk to help bring health back as well", "if you go without food"): HUNGER AND THIRST. Page-only HG module before LK. Two needs per hero, food and drink (0..100), drain while you play and are alive: food 100 in 40 min (fT 2400 s), drink in 30 (dT 1800). <=25 Hungry/Thirsty (half), 0 Starving/Parched (none). Hunger slows health regen; thirst slows mana regen (a hero without mana: health; health factor = min(food, noMana?drink:1)). Done in hgPre (ocStep, before run.step) by subtracting dt*(1-k) from the engine's S.regenT / S.manaT (engine REGEN and MANA_REGEN, both every 2 s; dt clamped to .1 like step). EverQuest-style auto-consume: at <=25 you eat from the main bag (bread, cfish, fish, rawfish, wsand last) or drink (flask, milk; never ale), so carrying food means you never go hungry. Fills: bread 40, wsand 60, fish 30, cfish 35, rawfish 15, milk 10 food+35 drink, flask 50, ale 20. Eating by hand fills too (amUse hgFill; run.eat/run.drink wrapped per run in hgWrap), and bread/milk/water are no longer refused at full health while hgWants (a need below 90). Lines on getting worse (the first names A Merchant). Effects window: 'hunger'/'thirst' entries (loaf/flask glyphs added to GLYPH) with a countdown to the next stage; Starving/Parched have t:null, so fxHud now shows no clock (and no 'low' blink) when t is null. Saved per hero in PKEY-hunger {n,r,c,f,d} via saveP (hgSave after wsSave) and every 20 s; hgLoad after wsLoad in start(). Backpack slots are not searched by auto-eat. t269 (vm, with a copy of the engine's regen loop); t255 ctx stubs, t256/t259/t261 wiring regexes, t262 stub. DEPLOY CHECKLIST now t195..t269, snapshot pre112, next stamp v2.39.7 -> v2.39.8.

Standing: W.standY is wrapped for -470<x<-440 by lkY(x,z,hero.y) = the highest of the deck (not over the stairwell hole) and the ramp turns, each no more than .55 above your feet; once you are above y0+.6 the surfaces reach 1.1 m past the rails so long frames cannot tip you off; lkPost (after run.step) clamps you inside the rails (deck rp-.35, steps rout-.15). Walking round the deck over the stairwell drops you ~1 m onto the steps (intended). The forest loop skips trees on the tree/paths via lkClear, which still calls rnd() once so the rest of the forest is unchanged. Lines: at the foot (tree + notches) and at the top (the view + the footprints go on), each once per 10 min. OC.solid has the trunk. - v2.39.3 (2026-09-30, the stream's story: the wreck is the Enterprise's lost sister and somebody carved a warning into her ribs): THE WRECK ON TEARDROP ISLE. Page-only WRK module before SEAM. Walk within 5.2 m of the wreck (-206.8,-21.6): two say() lines, the carving in Elder Futhark runes (wrkRunes, plain letter-for-rune: SHE WEEPS FOR WHAT SHE LOST. DO NOT ANSWER HER CALL; C/K/Q share a rune, V/W, J/Y, X/Z) and her split name board '...ANCE'; re-arms after walking 7.2 m away, at most once per 10 min. wrkBuild (after seamBuild) lays half a name board in the sand beside her (first free beach spot of five offsets, clear of OC.solid), with a weathered CanvasTexture '...ANCE'. Google Fonts link gains Noto Sans Runic (unicode-range, only fetched where runes show); --display/--fell/--body stacks list it before serif. The answer is the stream's: don't spoil it in-game; fannar may name her (Endurance?) and decide what the warning means. __F3 gains WRK/wrkRunes. t265 new; t262 ctx stub wrkStep. DEPLOY CHECKLIST now: t195..t265, snapshot pre108, next stamp v2.39.3 -> v2.39.4.
- v2.39.2 (2026-09-30, Tatum 04:10 "there should be a sea monster, at least as an easter egg in the distance"): SOMETHING IN THE CRYING OCEAN. Page-only SEAM module before SAIL. seamBuild(g) (after ocBuild) makes one hidden group 'seamonster': head+neck (glowing pale eyes with tear streaks, horns, a fin frill), four torus-arc humps with fins, a tail with flukes (six wBaker parts, S.body, head first), ten foam rings (one shared transparent material). seamPick(h,fx,fz,rnd): centre 38-48 m ahead of the camera (+-0.55 rad), heading across the view, every footprint point x<-106, |z|<150 and ocLand<sea-4 (ring of 14 m <sea-3); starts 18 m before the centre. seamStep (in ocStep after sailStep): only when alive and x<-110; the first check waits 8 s (aboard under way) or 25 s; rolls every 40 s: the first time aboard under way (sailAboard, SAIL.x<-120, ramp w 0) it always comes, then 40% aboard / 18% elsewhere; 150 s cooldown after a sighting. It shows 18 s (dt-based): parts rise head first (3 s, .45 s stagger), bob, swim 2.6 m/s, sink from 13.8 s. At 1.8 s one say() line (the first per session is the long 'sounds like crying' one) and seamMoan (sfxTone moan, panned by side). Unnamed on purpose: fannar gets to name it. __F3 gains SEAM/seamPick/seamRise. t264 new; t262 ctx stub seamStep. DEPLOY CHECKLIST now: t195..t264, snapshot pre107, next stamp v2.39.2 -> v2.39.3.
- v2.39.1 (2026-09-30, fannar22 02:30 "the Enterprise does round trips: 2 minutes at the island, 2 minutes at the New World, 2 minutes back at West Dominos"): THE ENTERPRISE SAILS, phase 2 of 3. Page-only SAIL module before the OC module. One timetable for everybody off Date.now() (SAIL.off shifts it for probes): dock port 120 s, go to Teardrop Isle jetty (x -200.4) 45 s, dock 120, go to t1 -335 40 s, turn pi 12 s, back in to the NW pier (x -350) 12 s, dock 120, go to t2 -90 70 s, turn 12, back in to her berth 14 s = 565 s; smootherstep legs, z stays 3.5. sailStep (from ocStep, each frame) moves E.PORT.boat (x; beam=len and deck=len/2 while turning so the whole hull is deck), the ramp (x+.4 docked, x 1e4 w 0 at sea), the pivot group 'enterprise' (the ship's meshes + name plate, baked into her own wBaker SB at the home berth, rotated/rolled), 'gangplank' (GB, translated only, shown when docked) and 'mooring' (DB, shown only docked at port). The v1.51.0 IIFE now adds to TB (SB/DB/GB) instead of PB and calls sailModel. sailAboard: within len/2+.2 x max(beam)/2+.3, y > deck-.35, not in the Pickle; aboard heroes are carried by translation + rotation (hero yaw and camYaw turn with her). sailTalk within 40 m or aboard: tie-up line + bell, cast-off line when aboard, bell and 'sails in a minute' at 60 s, last call at 10 s; on the empty port pier 'back in about N minutes' once per half voyage. The current's line now says take the Enterprise. t263 new; t262 ctx stub. DEPLOY CHECKLIST now: t195..t263, snapshot pre106, next stamp v2.39.1 -> v2.39.2. NEXT (phase 3): content on the isle and the New World (Tatum 04:10: a sea monster glimpsed in the distance; ask fannar).
- v2.39.0 (2026-09-30, fannar22 02:25 the Crying Ocean): THE CRYING OCEAN, phase 1 of 3. Page-only OC module before buildWorld. E.PORT.west = -560 at load (collide clamp, rowWet); buildWorld paints the old ground/sea at OC.pw -92 (try/finally) then ocBuild widens the sea plane to x -690, adds a flat seabed plane (-5.9, visible only west of -95), Teardrop Isle (centre -200,-14, R(a)=12+7cos^3 tail east + wobble; tiles 32 m at .8 m), the New World coast (x_c(z)=-372+6sin(.08z+1)+3sin(.21z), land behind it, hills; tiles 24 m at 1.2 m, z +-72) - all ground tiles in WX.chunks. ocWrap(W) = rsFlat pattern: W.height/standY = max(engine, ocGround) for x<-140; ocGround includes the two piers (island jetty x -200.9..-199.1 z -6.5..0.9; NW pier x -372..-346 z +-0.9; y .3). Palms, rocks, the wreck (SW beach -206.8,-21.6) and New World trees (wTree) go into WX.pb; their circles are OC.solid, pushed page-side in ocStep (engine never sees them). Current: ocPush(x,z) east, 7 m/s full past x -125, calm within ~6 m of the island shore and x < -352; ocStep after rowStep pushes swimmers and the rowboat, says once per 45 s. Sea shader gets OC_SEA (shallows + foam at both shores). Zones via ocZone in zoneAt: The Crying Ocean (x<-110), Teardrop Isle (e<1.8), The New World (x<-310). __F3 gains OC, ocYaw. t262 new; t250/t252 loosened. Unreachable until phase 2 (the Enterprise sails: v2.39.1). DEPLOY CHECKLIST now: t195..t262, snapshot pre105, next stamp v2.39.0 -> v2.39.1.
- v2.38.1 (2026-09-30, fannar22 01:35 "when you add wolf meat and bread in the oven, it make wolf sandwich"): THE WOLF SANDWICH (wsand, food {str:1,dur:300}, price 30 -> 15c, stack 10, flag ws; EARLY block). Page-only WS module before fgWrap: wsWrap(run) wraps run.ovenPut(k,'ws')/ovenTake(k) (idempotent, called from ovenUI and wsLoad); a sandwich shelf is {t:OVEN.bake, ws:1}, the engine counts it down and rings, wsEv rewrites the ring line. Burn = E.cookBurn(P) like fish; Cooking skill-up page-side (canSkillUp/skillUpChance, then run.retrain(P)). Eating goes through run.eat (the engine's px() only ever adds ITEMS.fish.food.str = +1, so str:2 would be a lie in the buff list); wsAte rewrites the fried-fish line and heals a quarter of max health. Oven window: recipe row (fish/sandwich, WS.mode; shown once you carry wolf meat or a sandwich shelf exists), empty shelves say 'put meat + bread in'. Opening the oven closes the bag, so there is no click-meat-in-bag path. Sandwich shelves saved per hero in PKEY-oven (wsSave in saveP, wsLoad at start; ovenNorm drops extra keys). Smith hints once when you walk in with wolf meat, and has a line every third sandwich. __F3 gains WS/toggleOven/ovenUI/wear. t261 new; t238/t253/t255/t256/t258/t259 loosened. Stats are mine, tunable. DEPLOY CHECKLIST now: t195..t261, snapshot pre104, next stamp v2.38.1 -> v2.38.2.

FG.at entry flags: dusk:1 (dForge DK: violet coals [1.1,.5,1.9]/[1.5,.9,2.5], violet coal light and embers) and sink:.1 (dForge lowers the forge by sink, the anvil by half, so it sits into the slope). - v2.37.0 (2026-09-30, fannar22 02:40-03:04: "orc pawns drop rusty sword 25%", "Vesh sells Sharpening stone", "Forge to Duskhollow", "I don't want to have no issue with forge", "Rusty sword be sold at Doug for 1 silver, to A Merchant be 5 copper", "I only want 1 sharpening stone and Rusty Sword at a time within the forge", "rusty sword should have 5 damage and delay 38 but Tarnished Sword be 5 damage and 32 delay"): THE FORGE. Goblins (goblin, goblin_s, goblin_g) and orc pawns drop a Rusty Sword 25% (fgTick rolls once per body, flag body.fgR). Sharpening Stone (whet, 5c, stacks 20) sold by Doug and Vesh (E.stockOf wrapped .fg). Doug's forge in East Dominos (FG.at, E.VILLAGE.forge) is clickable (invisible pick box, fgAt raycast within 9 paces) and opens #forgeWin (8 slots, beside the bag like the bank). Click a stone and a rusty sword in the bag to put them in (one of each; nothing else), Forge: the stone is used up, chance min(.9,.25+smith*.013) to turn the Rusty Sword (5 dmg, delay 38) into a Tarnished Sword (5 dmg, delay 32); fail keeps the sword. New skill Blacksmithing (smith, SKILL_KEYS, rises by doing, cap 5+5*level). Doug buys rusty 1s / tarnished 5s (engine sellValue = price/2), A Merchant buys rusty 5c / tarnished 3s (FG.amBuys via keeperRefuses/sellValue/r.sell wraps). Forge contents saved per hero in PKEY-forge (fgSave/fgLoad, identity + level check). Sparks + anvil clang. RELOAD BUG FIXED: the roster loads before the page's item/skill registrations, so invNorm dropped page items (backpack, packw, AM goods) and progNorm dropped page skills on every reload; the EARLY block before `var KNIGHT=` now registers flask/milk/bread/backpack/packw/whet/swords/smith first. packApply heals a lost strapped backpack when its pack record exists (not if it lies on a corpse). t259 new; t238/t255/t256/t257/t258 loosened. DEPLOY CHECKLIST now: t195..t259, snapshot pre100, next stamp v2.37.0 -> v2.37.1. NEXT: forges in West Dominos and Duskhollow (add to FG.at with a model).
- v2.36.0 (2026-09-30, fannar22 01:57-02:32: "a New merchant in East Dominos near Ranger guild but in a shop", "the shop be called Range Shop", "the new Merchant be called A Merchant 2", "A Merchant 2 sell Arrows and Bow", "remove Arrows and Bow from Mira Inventory" once he is open, "welcome to my Range shop feel free to brows around", "A Merchant to say welcome are you interested in a new backpack?", "why not even out the land for the lodge?"): THE RANGE SHOP. A log-walled hunter's lodge with an open front, a counter, a moss-green gable roof running out as an awning, bows on the back wall, arrow barrels, quivers, antlers, a painted target on the east wall, a lantern and a RANGE SHOP sign on the ridge, at RS (-17.3,-10.8) in the pines north of Bob's target, front facing +z. A Merchant 2 (RIG_LOOKS.amerch2, green feathered hat, he/him) sells the Bow (1 gold) and Arrows (4c, one at a time) via rsBuy; he buys nothing (Mira does). E.stockOf wrapped (.rs): his stock, and Mira's list minus bow/arrows; E.keeperRefuses true for him; SHOP_HI/NOT_STOCKED lines; E.ENZO.nobow/bow and the engine "out of arrows" event (rsEv at the chatAdd(khEv) loop), CLASS_WHAT ranger and the invUI no-arrows hint now point to the Range Shop. rsClear drops 4 pines (|lx|<4.3, lz -3.4..4.4) and calls rsFlat: the hill climbs ~.35/m there, so W.height and W.standY are wrapped on the world (the engine reads both through W for jumps, the page for everything) to a flat pad at P=h(RS.x,RS.z+.8) over |lx|<2.9, lz -2.3..3.2, smoothstep back to the hill over 2.6 m. Solid circles tagged .rs. Map label, name tag, pickable, welcome once (rsTick), rsReply topics (name/the 2, brother, Bob, Mira, bow, arrows, sell, bag, wolves, hello). A Merchant's welcome now opens with the backpack. t258; t238/t252/t253/t255 regexes loosened for the new hooks.
- v2.35.1 (2026-09-30, fannar22 02:03 "when you left click on the backpack in inventory window, then it open it own backpack window", "backpack window be moveable"): the backpack's 8 pockets (bag slots 8..15) live in #packWin (class inv bankwin packwin, ol#packSlots), not at the bottom of the bag. invUI still builds every slot with the same handlers and appends i>=INV_SLOTS to #packSlots. togglePack(v) (packOpen): clicking the strapped packw toggles it, strapping it on opens it, selling it back closes it, invUI closes it when the bag is 8 again. Tick refresh `if(invOpen||packOpen)invUI()`. winDrag/winPlace lists include packWin (drag by title, reset with the others). dragEnd: worn gear dropped on a pocket goes to that slot (index from the button's data-i). Desktop: beside the character window (right: 3.85rem + 38rem + .6rem, clamped on screen). Phone: top sheet, help hidden, #inv:has(~ #packWin:not([hidden])) max-height calc(100vh - 14.5rem) so they don't overlap. t256 updated (window open/close), t257 new. - v2.35.0 (2026-09-30, fannar22 01:33 "A Merchant to sell a 8 slot backpack which sell for 6 gold", 01:53 "the backpack fill up 1 inventory slot", 01:55 "sell backpack back ... once it has no item in it"): A Merchant sells a Backpack (page item backpack, 600 copper, pack 8, not stackable, one per hero). Click it in the bag (wear -> packUse): it becomes packw 'Backpack (on)' in its own slot and the bag grows 8 -> 16 (slots 8..15 get li.pk, a leather tint). The engine's invNorm slices a loaded bag to INV_SLOTS=8, so the page keeps slots 8..15 in its own localStorage record PKEY+'-pack' keyed by hero slot HI {n,r,c,xp,lv,slots,cnt}: packSave() inside saveP (never wipes a record while the bag is still 8), packApply(run) right after E.makeRun in start() (needs matching name/race/class, level/xp not below the record, packw in the bag or the record; unknown ids dropped, stacks clamped). packWrap(run): Mira (engine sell) buys packw back only when slots 8..15 are empty, then the bag shrinks to 8 and the record is dropped; the vault refuses it; A Merchant refuses a second pack while one is in the bag or on your back. Known edges: after death the corpse holds packw but the bag keeps 16 until relog; relog before looting the corpse and clicking a packw re-extends. t256 new, t255 loosened. (old checklist) NEXT: A Merchant 2 at the Range Shop in East Dominos near the Ranger guild (fannar 01:57-02:01: sells arrows and bow; once open, arrows and bow come off Mira's stock), then the rest of the v2.34.0 queue.
- v2.34.0 (2026-09-30, fannar22 01:32-01:40 "a new merchant to Mira shop name A Merchant", "Flask of Water 1 silver, bottle of Milk for 3 silver, bread 2 silver", "Mira found a person to rent the empty spot"): A Merchant rents the empty west counter (HALL.counter2) of Mira's hall, at hall-local (2.45,-2.1) = world (-21.05,12.5), facing the door. Round grocer in a brown bowler, ginger beard, cream apron (RIG_LOOKS.amerch); his counter goods are a rigProp on his rig (loaves on a board, five milk bottles, three leather flasks, a chalk board). Page items flask/milk/bread (slot loot, stack 10, price 10/30/20 copper, `am` effect: water +10% hp +30% mana, milk +30% hp, bread +20% hp; S.eatT 4 s wait, refused at full). Used from the bag via wear() -> amUse. Shop: E.stockOf/E.keeperRefuses wrapped for 'A Merchant' (he sells only, Mira buys); per run amWrap(run) wraps run.shopKeep (pref 'A Merchant' -> amNear: hallIn and within VILLAGE.tradeR of his spot), run.buy (amBuy: price, coin, bag room via E.invStack) and run.sell (he points you to Mira). Every 5th sale a copper 'goes to Mira'. npcReply wrapper amReply (A King is his cousin, the rent, the cow, his name). engine invNorm checks ITEMS live, so the page items survive a save. Tests t238/t253 loosened for the new tick/pick lines; t255 new. (old checklist). NEXT (fannar's queue): backpack (6 gold, +8 bag slots; engine invNorm slices to INV_SLOTS=8 so page-side persistence), wolf meat 75% (sells 5 copper) + ruined wolf pelt 65%, rusty sword off goblins 25%, oven wolf meat + bread = wolf sandwich, sharpening stone at Doug 5 copper, forge in East and West Dominos with 8 slots (stone + rusty sword = Tarnished Sword), Blacksmithing and Cooking skills for Human/Dark elf/Wolfman (learned by doing, trainers can teach), mob stats (goblin 20/25/20, scout 23/25/20, wolf 25/25/23).
- v2.33.1 (2026-09-30, fannar22 01:24 "I want A King to be level 20, Health 800 and his Damage is 40 damage"): A KING FIGHTS BACK. He is not in the engine's NPCF/TARGET_NPCS (engine untouched), so the page hands the engine his fight record: khF(S) creates S.npcf['A King'] (lv 20, hp 800, atk 40, roam 0, a step in front of the throne, KH.up .55) every frame it is missing; E.provoke/npcAt/npcHit/the npcf tick then do everything as for Doug and Mira (XP, coins, 180 s down, 'A King is back.'). E.targetInfo is wrapped page-side (khInfo: lv, fight, hp from the record, hostile when mad); E.NPC_HP, NPCF_T.mad/shut get his lines. The rig has userData.fn/home so npcPose poses him (stands when mad, lies then vanishes when down); khTick moves him to the standing spot / floor height when up, drops him from S.folk while down, and skips his greetings mid-fight. West Dominos Port is not guard ground (zoneOf != good): no WANTED there, so a guard only shouts "For A King!" and stays (khEv now keeps 'a guard' in WANTED lines when the hero is within NPCF_T.hunt of his post). Rat-King eviction quest dropped: fannar doesn't want it (01:24/01:25, "hold off on a king mission while I think about it"). Tests t254 (+t253 ctx/khEv tweak).
- v2.33.0 (2026-09-30): A KING (king1.py; fannar22 01:00 "a house in west dominos where a person name a King", 01:01 "a level 20 guard named a guard which has 400 health attack for 30 damage and has Dominos guard as a faction", 01:02 "if there is 2 kings then both of them can fight over ownership of the land", 01:07 "name him A King"). KH (-47.5,23): a two-storey house on the south lawn of West Dominos Port, stone ground floor with quoins, plaster + timber upper floor, a steep red gable over the door (east, +x), a round turret on the front-north corner with a slate cone and a gold crown weathervane, royal banners, chimney; a porch with A King's throne (he sits on it, emo sit, y F+.44, looks at you), three steps, a hedged lawn with a gate at lx 12 and topiary pots. Solid: PORT.stock circles tagged kh (house perimeter, turret, porch edge, steps, hedges), so the lawn is entered by the gate and the porch is his (you stand ~1.5 m from him). a guard = a THIRD ENGINE GUARD: E.GUARD.posts.push at page load (before any makeRun) at (KH.x+7.8,KH.z+1.9) + E.FAC.guard.folk, so lv 20 / 400 hp / fights / target window / faction come from the engine; rigGuard(i>1) uses RIG_LOOKS.kguard (purple tabard). His hit is the engine's GUARD.atk 25, not fannar's 30 (per-guard atk would need an engine change). khEv drops him from the WANTED 'coming for you' list (he never leaves the lawn). A King: RIG_LOOKS.aking + new L.crown piece in rigHumPieces; talk via npcReply wrapper khReply (rat/sewer, castle claim, guard 'Lord Guard of the Lawn', name, throne, crown off his grandmother's cake, kingdom, kneel, 4 rotating hellos; S.kingDown>0 changes the rat line). khTick: S.folk target, see line <18 m, hi line <6.5 m + sys hint. Map: lawn, house, porch, turret + label. Next ideas: the feud (eviction notice quest down the manhole, the Rat King's reply, stomping heard in the sewer), villagers' opinions of A King, night-lit windows. Tests: t253 (VM) + t238/t246 anchors relaxed. 
- v2.32.0 (2026-09-30): THE OLD CASTLE (castle1.py; coldpresss 00:30 "Next up castles?"). A ruined castle in the north woods at CAS (93,-26), half 8 m, where the Good Forest turns dark: curtain walls (north broken west of x-4, east crumbling south of z3, a breach in the west wall z 0..3.2), four corner towers (only the NE one whole, 10.5 m, with a red rag banner), a gatehouse with a portcullis stuck half up (one gate leaf fallen outside, one hanging inside), a roofless hall along the north wall (gable, arched door, windows), a dry well, flagstones, rubble. casClear moves the engine's trees out of the footprint and the lane to the gate (x/z += 1e5, W.trees rebuilt in place, before worldPaint). Collision = PORT.stock circles tagged cas (214, every .35 m along walls, none poking into gaps), camera = casCam marches from the hero to the camera against sol + camera-only circles across the gate and hall door and pulls in, casCamY caps it under the arch and the lintel. Map: outline + 'the old castle'. Lines: seen through the trees (<30 m), stepping into the courtyard (+ sys hint to ask around). Rumours: npcReply wrapper for castle/ruin/fort words, 12 villagers with 12 contradicting stories (Smith's grandfather's unpaid hinges, Fergus's very tall farmer, Bobby's dragon, Lizzie's feasts via Old Pell, Enzo's skeletons, Bob's oak, Doug and rope, Chomp, Mira's tax, Rooster, Wick's light in the top window, Ollie, Greymane's lit stone den). Known: mobs ignore stock circles, so skeletons walk through walls. Next ideas: climb the tall tower, Wick's light at night, alqsara's dragon, clues backing each rumour (hinges, cold hearth, scratch marks, cut rope), fannar22's king's house out west + a level 20 royal guard + two kings feuding. Tests: t252 (castle VM) + t238 anchor relaxed for the casTick call.
- v2.31.2 (2026-09-30): FISHING FROM THE PICKLE (light12.py; coldpresss 00:00 "Out to the marigold to catch the rarer fish", 00:22 "the game should tell the player about the fishing chances"). The engine's fish() refuses while S.swim (always true in the boat) and fishTick cancels on swim, so the page runs the boat cast itself: fishGo() -> rowFish() when ROW.on. Needs a pole in either hand (rowPole via E.ITEMS[..].pole) and worms (E.invCount; rowUse replicates invUse). Cast: the boat stops (v,w,act 0), R.fish={x,z,hx,hz,t} 2.4 m to the rower's right (cos yaw,-sin yaw), wait E.FISH.waitMin..waitMax. The page's own bobber/line/rod-up code draws it: var fsh=S.fishing||(ROW.on&&ROW.fish)||null. The stow line spares pole/pole2 while fishing; pose emo k 'sit' while R.fish; oars rest shipped. Any stroke (|th| or |tu| > .15) reels in; drifting > 1.2 m from the cast spot reels in; stepping out clears it. rowFishTick (in rowTick): odds rowOdds = E.fishChance(P,rain,riseNear) x1.5 in the bay, x2 in deep water (bobber x < -72), x3 within 16 m of the Marigold when she is in (cap .95); pole snap E.poleSnap(P) (pole removed from its slot, S.snaps++), skill-up E.canSkillUp/E.skillUpChance -> P.sk.fish++ and run.retrain(P). Catch: S.fishEv catch/miss/snap for the ripple, S.catches++. Rare roll of catches: 10 % bay / 25 % deep / 50 % Marigold; pools mack/snap/moon(night)/bream(Marigold only); 8 % of rare rolls in deep or Marigold water are OLD GRUMPUS (seen, not caught: nothing in the bag). A rare fish = 2 Raw Fish (E.invStack(...,2)), tally in localStorage fr3d_rare ('(your rare catches: ...)'). Ollie shouts about rare catches by the Marigold (ROWF.wq, 2 s later, within 40 m); Wick or Ollie on Grumpus. Talk: Wick grumpus|grouper; Ollie grumpus|grouper|bream. The odds are told at every cast (boat) and on the shore too (fishGo adds '(your odds: X % a cast...)' after the engine's cast); rowPct keeps one decimal under 10 %. Button: 'Cast a line' when |v|<.6 with pole+worms away from land, 'Reel in' while a line is out, 'Step out' near land wins. t251.
- v2.31.1 (2026-09-30): the Pickle is smaller (coldpresss 00:17: the Enterprise should be much bigger than her; the Enterprise is the engine's walkable deck, so the dinghy shrinks instead): ROW.SC 1.5 -> 1.25, rowFits bow/stern probes ROW.BOW 1.1 / ROW.STERN 1, float origin sea-.24 so the inner floor stays above the sea plane (floor top local .23*SC). light11.py; t250 uses R.BOW.
- v2.31.0 (2026-09-30): THE ROWBOAT, the Pickle (light10.py, keeper block before keepSay; coldpresss asked for sailing 23:48 and picked Rowboat 23:53, wants to row out to the Marigold for rarer fish). ROW state: moored at (-61.8,-1.85) on the pier's south side, yaw -PI/2 (bow west), SC 1.5, VMAX 3.1, TURN 1.25. rowModel: wBaker hull (green band, dry inner floor at local y .2 so the sea plane never shows inside, bow wedge, stern, two thwarts, oarlocks) + 2 oar pivot groups at (+-.47,.5,.15) + a CanvasTexture 'PICKLE' plane on the stern. rowBuild in its own try after beachBuild; rowTick on the keeper hook (bob at sea-.28, oar sweep A=-.55cos(ph) / dip, shipped 1.3 when moored, rowBtnSync every .25 s, boat left on a beach is rowed home after 60 s with the hero >30 m away, see-line once). rowStep(dt) runs in tick() just before run.step: stick = turn toward the stick heading (d*1.6 clamped) with thrust mag*max(-.35,cos d); tank keys = turn/fwd (reverse x.45); then ZEROES inp dx/dz/fwd/turn/side/tank/jump/dodge/attack so the engine sees you floating still at her spot (S.swim stays true: swm is patched &&!ROW.on so no strokes/tread; Smith's far-bell patched; wasSw=false so no drip). rowFits checks centre, bow +1.3 and stern -1.2 with rowWet: not x<west+2.5, not height>sea-.28, not the pier rect (+.6/+.4), not the Enterprise ellipse, not within 2.8 of the lighthouse, not within 3.2 of the Marigold when she is in. Bumps: v*=-.2 + sfx thud (.9 s apart). camYaw eases behind her when |v|>.35 (camFollow only swings on h.moving); the camera's hy = sea+.05 while rowing; boarding pulls cam.dist to 6.8 and leaving restores it. rowSeat(H) after H.rotation.y: seated at local (0,.29,.36)*SC, facing the stern (yaw+PI). Pose emo {k:'row',sit:1,ph} ahead of EMO in animRig; weapons/shield/bow hidden while rowing. Button #rowBtn inside .arena .stage (position absolute, right .9rem, bottom 9.6rem: the full-screen arena has z-index 2147483000, a body-level fixed button hides behind it): 'Take the rowboat' within 3.4 m / 'Step out' when rowLand() finds the pier deck within 2.7 m or dry sand within 3.5 m (16 directions). /row /boat /rowboat toggle. Board refused far away (says where she is), in the air, looting, dead; leave refused in deep water. Pier exit re-moors her. __F3.ROW and __F3.row(v). t250; t227/t228 anchors accept a ROW emo prefix, t245 allows thud. Ideas next: fishing from the boat near the Marigold (check the engine fishing spot rules), Ollie heckling a rower, Wick's driftwood fire for cooking.
- v2.30.3 (2026-09-29): BEACHCOMBING (light9.py, keeper block before keepSay). BEACH: the tide leaves finds along the west beach waterline: 4 on the first tick after a world build, +3 each dawn (KEEP.night falling edge), at most 6 on the sand. beachSpawn: z in -26..27 avoiding the pier (|z|<2.6), the rowboat (-23.5..-18.3) and within 2.5 m of another find; scan x from -52 west until W.height<sea+.06, then .5-1.4 m landward. Kinds: shell 30 %, scallop 22 %, star 13 %, wood 13 %, glass 22 % (red 6 % of glass, white 30, green 32, blue 32). beachGeo per kind cached (wBaker, ink .01-.012), group scale 1.8, one twinkle sprite each (shared canvas texture, own SpriteMaterial, disposed on pickup). Walk within 1.3 m (hp>0): FIRST line (loot channel) the first time per kind, then 'You pick up <name>.'; '(your beach finds: ...)' on sys (beachSum); counts in localStorage fr3d_beach (beachGot keeps only positive numbers < 1e6, floored); sfx ting (.6 for red). Wick within 16 m: red glass always gets 'Red? Forty years on this point and I've found two. Keep that somewhere safe.' 2.2 s later (BEACH.wq), other first finds 50 %. keepReply topic shell|glass|starfish|driftwood|scallop|beachcombing (first after the go/back rule: red / glass / plain lines); ollieReply same topic before the ship|sea rule ('Brisk says half the green sea glass on this coast is his old bottles'). Hint once per session. t249; t245 allows ting; t241/t246/t248 anchors accept more [a-z]+Build/[a-z]+Tick insertions. coldpresss asked for cooking/sailing/castles 23:48 and picked Rowboat next.
- v2.30.2 (2026-09-29): CRABS on the west beach (light8.py, in the keeper block before keepSay). crabGeo (wBaker: orange flattened icosphere shell, three legs a side, arms and claws, eyes on stalks; ~.5 m, group scale 1.25) shared by 7 crabs (2 meshes each: toon + ink) in group three.crabs, built in buildWorld right after mariBuild in its own try. Homes [-54.8,-24] [-53.6,-12] [-55.6,-7] [-54.2,6] [-55.4,12] [-53.4,19] [-55,25] (clear of the pier and the rowboat). crabTick (end of the keepTick hook, after mariTick): group visible only when h.x<-15 && |z|<70; states pause -> walk (sideways: rotation.y=atan2(-vz,vx) (+PI when flipped), .55 m/s, target within 1.8 m of home, x clamped -56.6..-52.3) -> flee when the hero (hp>0) is within 3.2 m (2.6 m/s away, toward the water unless you stand seaward; x floor -57.8) -> dig (.45 s, sink .16) -> hide (7-14.5 s, and until you are 6 m from its home) -> rise at home. y = max(height, sea-.12). One 'Little crabs scuttle sideways across the sand.' per session. Wick: 'The crabs? They were here first. I just keep the light.' Ollie: "Cap'n says crabs are just spiders that went to sea. I don't like that." +14 draw calls (~7k tris) while on the west beach. t248; t241/t246 anchors relaxed for the crabBuild/crabTick insertions.

Once the parcel is given (MARI.st.given) the beam/glow are x1.2, the lamp-catches line mentions the new wick, and Wick's lit-lamp reply is 'Burns cleaner with the new wick. Don't tell Brisk.'
