- v2.30.0 (2026-09-29): THE MARIGOLD COMES IN (light6.py). MARI block (in the keeper block, before keepSay). Once keepNotes().length>=3, at each dawn (M.day flag, K.night from keepTick) she lies at anchor at (-71,-15) rot 1 (keepShip(T,1): furled sail on the yard + an anchor line; lantern off), a rowboat (mariBoat, wBaker: bottom, sides with a blue band, pointed bow, thwart, two oars; scale 1.35) at the waterline at z=-21 (scan like the bottle), and Ollie the ship's boy (humanoid knit cap 0x2f5f9a, white tunic, red scarf, scale .84) 2.9 m inland; always until the parcel is delivered, then 34% of mornings. Walk within 3.5 m (or tap him within 8 m): intro queue (M.q, timed lines) -> parcel; localStorage fr3d_marigold {met,parcel,given,laugh}, only plain 1s count. Walk within 3 m of Wick while he sits on his sail -> the unwrapping: 'A wick. For Wick... Every year I laugh. Don't tell him.' ollieReply topics (bottles: 'I wrote the scared one'), Wick's reply on ollie/brisk/parcel/marigold. Ollie: name tag (NAMES list), pickables, S.folk, npcOpen->mariOpen, E.npcReply wrap. mariBuild is called at the END of buildWorld (before namesRebind), NOT from keepBuild, so the keeper VM tests don't build him (their stub keepShip would be shared). At dusk he rows back out. Tests t216/t217/t239/t240/t241/t244 relaxed for v2.30; new t246.
- v2.29.4 (2026-09-29): THE BOTTLE (light5.py). When the night ship finishes her run (keepShipTick P.done) K.bq=1; the next morning (dl>.5) keepBottleTick puts a corked green bottle (keepBottle: glass cylinder + neck + cork + a paper roll, a pulsing additive glint sprite, scale 1.7) in the surf at the waterline: scan west from Wick's seat at z=sz+4 until W.height<sea+.06, then .25 m seaward (fallback lx+3.5,lz+5). Hint line once (west side, or within 9 m). Walk within 1.7 m: KNOTES (8 notes from the Marigold's crew: Hask the mate, Ollie the ship's boy, Captain Brisk, the cook) in order, unread first, kept in localStorage fr3d_bottles (validated ints), then random; '(you tuck it away with the others: N notes from the sea so far)'. Wick nearby reacts 2.5 s later (first time: 'They used to do that, the ships...'). Unpicked, the tide takes it at nightfall. Wick's ship reply now: Enterprise line (never lit) / 'Blinks at me. Don't know her name.' (lit, no notes) / 'The Marigold? ... the show-off.' (after a note). Nothing is rewarded, on purpose. Test t245.
- v2.29.3 (2026-09-29): Wick is a target (light4.py). pickables() pushes three.wick while visible+parented (tap / right-click him on phones and desktop); the S.folk entry for the target window is set AFTER the cubs line in the frame (folkTick resets S.folk each frame and keepTick runs before it, so it can't live in keepTick); npcOpen -> keepOpen(): keepReply('hail') ('Morning. Mind the net.' / 'Evening.' / lit: 'Nice night for it...') and at night before the lamp the one-time /sit hint. Inside the tower he is hidden, so not targetable (the folk entry is deleted). Test t244.
- v2.29.2 (2026-09-29): the ship that answers, and light on the water (light3.py). keepShip: a small sloop (hull + pointed bow, stern cabin, mast, yard, square sail, jib, pennant; x1.25) with a stern lantern (MeshBasic fog:false + additive glow sprite fog:false). Each night, 6 s after the lamp reaches lit > .8, it sails up the coast far out (x -80, z -38 -> 38 at 1.15 m/s, bobbing, lantern fading in/out over 4 m); abeam of the light (z > lz+4) its lantern blinks twice, chat hears it if you are on the west side (x < -36), it bears away (vx -.18), and if you sit beside Wick he says 'See that? That's why you light it.' 3.5 s later. One ship a night (K.ship reset each dusk). keepGlint: two additive shader strips on the sea (26 m, widening 1.6 -> 6 m) turning with the beams, shimmering, shown only while the beam points over the sea (cos(rot) < .1). t243.
- v2.29.1 (2026-09-29): the lighthouse is shared (light2.py). Your pos card carries lh:1 once YOU lit it (KEEP.mine); netPos reads p.lh (only a plain 1). While your lamp is dark, keepTick checks the peers once a second (keepPeerLit: lh 1, not gone, heard from in the last 15 s): if someone online lit theirs, yours is lit too (KEEP.by = their hero name) with one 'net' line (by day: 'Word from the west beach: X sat with Wick...', at night: 'Out on the west beach the lighthouse is lit...'), and it keeps burning after they log off. Only your own lighting is sent on (no endless relay). Wick's lamp answer names them ('X sat with me a while. So she burns tonight.'). If their light arrives while he is on the stairs, he still lights it and it counts as yours. Old clients ignore the field. t242.
- v2.29.0 (2026-09-29): THE LIGHTHOUSE KEEPER (light.py, page only). A red-and-white lighthouse stands with its feet in the surf at the north end of the west beach (-60.2,-33; keepModel: rocks, plinth, tapered tower, gallery, lantern glass, cone roof, stepping stones), solid via one extra circle in E.PORT.stock (tagged .lh, pushed once). Wick, its old keeper (he/him; humanoid bald, white beard, navy coat, red scarf), sits on a folded sail in front with a crate, a hand lantern, rope and a net (keepSpot). By day he mends his net and grumbles if you sit ('Lamp's dark till dusk. No point lighting it for nobody.'). After dark (daylight < .3, back at > .45) sit (/sit) within 4.5 m: at 4 s he glances at you, at 10 s 'Well. Seeing as somebody's here.', walks to the door, goes in (hidden, no tag), 5 s later the lamp catches ('lampup' sfx) and at 12 s he walks back and sits: 'Looks better with the light on'. Standing near him 8 s at night without sitting gives one sys hint. Once lit (KEEP.on, kept through a world rebuild) the lamp burns every night of the visit: a glow sprite, a lamp sphere and two additive cone beams (ShaderMaterial, fog:false) turning at .62 rad/s, eased in with a flicker. He talks (keepReply via an E.npcReply wrapper: lamp, sea/ships, name, gulls, nets, lonely, thanks, bye, hello) and is hailable/talkable/emotable first when close (keepNear in the three chains), NOT pickable (E.targetInfo has no entry). Nothing drops, no xp: the reward is the light. Also a real fix: name tags were built once and stayed on the FIRST world's people after a rebuild (a second play / new character), so a moving NPC's tag stayed behind; namesRebind(g) at the end of buildWorld moves stale tags onto the new world's objects by name. t241 (and t218..t238 build regexes accept v2.29+, t238 ignores the keepTick hook).
- v2.28.22 (2026-09-29): three more cloud shapes (clouds2.py, shapes from cloudshapes.js): a howling wolf (sitting, muzzle up), a sword (point up) and a little blob wearing headphones and a bow (the old Sloppy look, a stream in-joke); 12 shapes now. When a shape has formed and chat names it, a soft airy chime plays (sfxPlay 'cloudform': a faint lowpass breath and three slow sine notes D5 G5 D6, called with typeof guard so the vm tests need no stub); pinned probe shapes stay silent. t240.
- v2.28.21 (2026-09-29): CLOUD WATCHING (clouds.py), a quiet moment with nothing to fight or win (the producer asked for one). /sit under an open sky (not rainIn, not underground, rain < .25) and after 2.5 s you lean back: skyCam blends the camera (SKYW.cam, in ~2.5 s, out fast) to 3.2 m behind the hero, low, looking up at SKYW.lel (eases to the shape's el - .11). By day (elevation > .02) 3.5 s later a SKY_SH shape (sheep, fish, rabbit, hen, turtle, crown, dragon, Sloppy the tavern cat, whale: [x,y,r] puffs, max 24) gathers from loose wisps (6 s), holds (to 13 s, chat names it), drifts apart (to 20 s), 2.5 s gap, no repeat within four. One Mesh of billboard quads (aQ corner, aA alpha, aS softness) 62 m out, sharing the sky shader's uCl/uCs, fog:false, renderOrder -7. Placement: skyNew casts 9 rays per candidate (5 az within the view, el .47/.57) against treesNear crowns (3.6*s tall, 1.6*s wide) and takes the clearest; shapes are centred on their outline and flipped at random. At night with stars: one shooting star per sit (STAR.force={fall:0}) and 'A shooting star! You make a wish.' A hit (hp drop) stops it for 6 s; standing up ends it. Your own name tag hides while the camera is up. A hint once a visit, first two visits (fr3d_skyhint) after 30 s standing still under a clear day sky. __F3.sky({k,at,freeze,flip}) pins a shape for probes; __F3.SKYW. Headless runs ~0.3 fps, so probe by setting cur.t.
- v2.28.20 (2026-09-29): FERGUS UP THE HILL (hill.py). Pays off his promise: once anSt()===2, on moonlit nights (daylight hysteresis .3/.45, moonOf k!==3) Fergus stands by Greymane's fire at the Moon Circle (local spot [-.95,-.85], his own humanoid+fidRig built lazily into three.elder.parent; the village Fergus is hidden, f.o.visible=false, and his garden spot stays quiet). He only leaves the garden when it's out of view (FOLK.group hidden or camera > fog.far+10); if the circle is in view he walks in/out along local [-1.8,9] (road side). Arriving at the stones: a line, then hillPlay: the answer twice through at spb .6 via musNote on MUS.bus (gain follows your distance), Greymane howls the harmony on the last note (mcHowl + hillElder anim, she looks at him while he plays: hillLook), then Greymane's first-time line (fr3d-hill LS) or a rotating HILL.fin line; replays every 80 s while you stay. Sound off: a narrated line and he saws silently (HILL.mute). Play the answer on your whistle there: hillDuet, he plays it straight back. Dawn while you watch: 'goodnight, old wolf' and he walks off. Chomp explains the empty spot once a night. Test t238; old tests' build regexes loosened to 2x.
- v2.28.19 (2026-09-29): THE FIDDLER'S ANSWER (page only), Fergus's fourth tune: TUNES[3] 'the fiddler\'s answer' s [0,2,1,4,2,5,4,7] = the moon song reversed ('turned round and climbing'), m 'moon'. Lore: at twelve he climbed to the Moon Circle, Greymane taught him her song, a year on he played her this and ran off; he never played it again. Gate: lesCap() is 3 until the songbook's first three ticks (bookMask()&7===7), then 4; lesStart practises instead (with a hum aside), jamFergus's lesson offer and lesDone's next-tune line respect it; bookDone's three-audiences Fergus line now ends 'come and see me. there\'s one more I could teach you.' Played (lesHeard i===3 -> anPlay): at the circle (ring+4) Greymane goes still, one soft low howl (anHowl via mcHowl; anElder: sitting howl emo 1.2-3.0 s after), three lines at 2.6/7/11.5 s, then anSt(1) (localStorage fr3d-answer), bookDone(3) 'Greymane remembered', stateLine. By Fergus (7 m): st 0 he goes red ('not so loud!'), st 1 anTell: you tell him (or say anything about her/greymane/wolf/hum/stones to him: npcReply5), his lines, then anSolo: fidStop, the tune on 'fid' at .55 s, then again from beat 10 with a harmony a third up (hm(d+2)); ANS.P drives fidBow; then anSt(2), fidPlay('moon'), stateLine. Afterwards: Greymane hums along, 'Have you told him yet?' (st 1) / 'I heard a fiddle on the wind last night' (st 2); the book's margin note turns to 'she remembers.' ANS.busy stops repeats. t237 (48 checks); t229/t234/t235/t236 loosened for four tunes.
- v2.28.18 (2026-09-29): THE SONGBOOK (page only). A ♪ button on the whistle bar (bookInit, called from jamBar; before the ✕; on phones it sits top-left of the bar like the ✕) opens a panel above the bar: every tune you know (lesKnown) with its notes as button numbers (T.s+1), a "show me" that lights the bar buttons in order at LES.gap (jamHit(n,1), silent: you play along), Fergus's margin notes on where each is loved (BOOK.W, IM Fell English italic, signed — F.; a beer ring stain by the jig), and ??? for tunes still to learn. Ticks once a tune finds its audience: bookDone(i) from henJig (0), vaStart when someone dances (1), mcSong's chorus (2); kept in localStorage fr3d-tunes-done (bitmask); a ♪ Songbook line 900 ms later; all three -> Fergus 'a proper whistler' + stateLine. A dot on the ♪ when you know more tunes than fr3d-book-seen. Closes on jamOff and lesStart (the lesson cue sits in the same spot). The first whistle line says '♪ opens your songbook.' t236 (40 checks); t228/t229 fake DOM got insertBefore/querySelector/classList.toggle, t228 expects 10 bar buttons.
- v2.28.17 (2026-09-29): THE VILLAGE DANCE (page only). Play the village air (Fergus's second tune, lesHeard i===1 -> vaStart) within 14 m of FOLK.center by day: everyone out in the lane (not stay/inside/seated, within 20 m) dances a slow round about you: f.va spread evenly by their angle, ring turns .3 rad/s, r 1.85 (Bobby 1.45 inside). vaStep: folkSteer to the moving spot (sp >= 1.2, Bobby 1.8); no progress for 1.5 s -> f.vjx, dance where you are (Pell gets boxed in behind the house by (-15,-3.6) from the east); at the spot sidestep round (1.2 m/s cap), face you, folkPush; animRig emo dance ph = e*2.6 + a per-person offset, a hop (.045, Pell .02). vaTick (once a frame in folkTick): on while < VD.end (12 s after the tune; the tune again = 12 s more) or while you keep playing (a note in 2.5 s), a minute at most, not at night, not 18 m+ away; when it ends and Lizzie reached her spot, a knees line (3 rotate). First time: 'The village comes out...' + Lizzie (+ Bobby call if he's out), Pell at 2.6 s (grandmother / wife), Bobby 'grandpa can DANCE?' at 5.2 s, a toast; later a short line; nobody out = a hens line. Night: a shutter + Lizzie once per night. TUNES[1] tip; ask Lizzie about music/dance (npcReply4, vaAsk). ALSO: lesHeard says a recognised tune once per 20 s (LES.hrd) so looping a tune doesn't spam 'bravo, whistler!' (the hens/moon/village still hear every loop); t229 updated. Fixed moon.py's stamp bump, which had retagged the cat's comments v2.28.16 (now v2.28.15 again): bump only 'build v2.28.X'. t235 (40 checks).
- v2.28.16 (2026-09-29): THE MOON SONG (page only). Play the moon song (Fergus's third tune, lesHeard i===2, called after the 'You play...' line) within MOONC.ring+4 of the Moon Circle on a moonlit night: mcSong starts MSONG (t0 = S.t, 7 s, per run). mcStone lights the six glyphs one after another from tonight's (.3 s apart), the ring glows, all fade over the last 2 s. Greymane (mcElder) howls sitting from .6 to 3.4 s (animRig emo {k:'howl',sit:1}; gsit now also honours emo.sit). The cubs (mcCub, in denTick after petting/games) sit up (roll -> 0), face you, and howl from 1.1+.4*i for 2.2 s, back to bed at 5.6 s. Sound (mcChorus/mcHowl, the /howl synth with a pitch): Greymane on the key's fifth an octave down, the cubs on the octave, third and fifth (Wren .4 semitone sharp), volume by distance, then two farHowls at 3.7/4.7 s. Lines: first time the whole picture + Greymane at 3.2 s + a toast; later a short line; by day / new moon Greymane says why (once each). TUNES[2] tip; ask Greymane about music/song/whistle (npcReply3 wrapper, mcAsk). t234 (47 checks).
- v2.28.15 (2026-09-29): SLOPPY THE TAVERN CAT SINGS (page only). catHears: whistle out, a note in the last 3 s, hero < 6 m: she turns her head to you and her tail sways with the notes (K.jp eases toward JAM.n). catSing(i) from jamPlay on notes 7 and 8 (i >= 6) when she's visible and < 6 m, 1.2 s cooldown (K.mt): K.mw .7 s lifts her head and opens her up (head rot.x -.55*mu), catMeow = sawtooth + triangle octave, pitch .82->1.03->.99->.7 over .62 s through a bandpass 800->2300->1000 Q 2.2, in the jam scale an octave down, volume by distance, panned. First time: 'Sloppy the cat lifts her head and yowls along with your high notes.' + Chomp line if within 9 m. t233; t228/t229 get catSing stubs.
- v2.28.14 (2026-09-29): THE HENS DANCE (page only). henJamOn: by day, whistle out, a note in the last 2.5 s, hero < FOLK.R+3 of the village centre. Hens within 11 m run to henSpots (a half ring at yaw+[-1.05,-.35,.35,1.05], r 1.35/1.8/2.4, inside FOLK.R+.5, folkClear .22, >= .45 apart, cached by hero x/z/yaw), folkSteer with the hero as an obstacle, a cluck (henCluck: two triangle chirps + a bandpass puff, panned) as each arrives; no progress for 1.5 s (hn.jx) = dance where she is. henPose: hop .07 on each note (odd hens .18 behind), sway body.z, head bob, legs; FOLK.jig 2.5 s after lesHeard recognises the Sleepy Hen jig (henJig: flutter hops, a cluck chorus, Clucky spins 360, 'The hens go wild...' + Bobby line if within 14 m, a toast once). Bobby (id pip) stops chasing to watch while they dance; first time a line 'The hens come running to your whistle!' + Bobby 'grandpa, look!'. Fergus's jig-learned line adds T.tip (the hens hint); ask Bobby about music/whistle/jig (npcReply2 wrapper). t232 (38 checks); t229 gets a henJig stub and the tip line.
- v2.28.13 (2026-09-29): OTHER PLAYERS SEE AND HEAR YOUR WHISTLE (page only). The postcard gains j (whistle out), jn (a note counter &1023) and jq (the digits 1-8 played since the last card, at most 6); a card that only carries notes waits 160 ms (about 6 a second at most, 40 fast notes = 23 cards, none lost). Receiving (netPos): the first card only sets the scene; after that each jn change plays the last min(count, jq) notes through netJam: gold notes and finger lifts whenever the peer is drawn, sound only within NET_JAMR 22 m, v .8*(1-d/22)^1.4, in the LISTENER's jamScale (their own music's key), spread over the card's gap, on its own NET.jP fader into MUS.bus (jamBus wakes it). jamRig(H,sn) builds the whistle for any rig (a wolfman peer to the snout); netFrame hides the peer's weapons, poses 'jam' (an emote comes first) and runs fidNotes with p.jb. "<name> takes out a tin whistle." once a minute within 40 m (p.jlAt, and never gated by page age). Older pages ignore the new fields. t231 (34 checks); t230's page regex loosened.
- v2.28.12 (2026-09-29): THE CUBS DANCE TO YOUR WHISTLE (page only). By day, when you play inside the Moon Circle (hero < 5.2 m from its centre, a note in the last 2.5 s, whistle out), Tuft, Bramble and Wren run over and dance in a half-ring in front of you, facing you, a hop and a dance swing per note (c.jp eases to JAM.n; Wren a quarter pulse behind). jamSpots(MC,h) picks all three spots together: 1.35 m (then 1.85 m) from you toward the centre, angles fanned out, clamped inside r 3.3, >= 1.15 m from the fire, >= .8 m from Greymane, >= .6 m from each other; null (only just outside the stones behind the fire) = dance where they are. Not during moon-chase or hide and seek; 2.5 s of silence or the whistle away and they go back to their games. Greymane says "look at them go. play on, little whistler." once per session (U.jdSaid). At night the cubs sleep on and your playing within 7 m calms Wren's shiver (c.calm). JAM.lt = time of your last note. NOTE: MOONC lives in the engine, page code must use E.MOONC. Tests: t230. Probe: all three reach their spots and dance (desktop, day).
- v2.28.11 (2026-09-29): WHISTLE LESSONS (page only). Say teach / lesson / learn / practice to Fergus (fidReply, before the tune keywords): the whistle comes out, he stops his tune (fidStop; lesTick keeps MUS.next 4 s ahead so the garden tune waits), plays a phrase on his 'fid' voice through LES.G into MUS.bus (the buttons flash blue, .demo), then "your turn" (cue pill .jam-cue above the bar). Rounds of 3, 5, 8 notes of the tune; notes are compared by button index so a key change mid-lesson is harmless. A wrong note replays the round; 3 misses or 20 s of silence end it kindly; walking off / the close button / "stop" end it. Tunes: the Sleepy Hen jig, the village air, the moon song (LES.TUNES); learned count in localStorage 'fr3d-tunes'; after all three, lessons are practice. Learning one: Fergus cheers, a sys line (n of 3), stateLine toast, the bar glows, he strikes up that tune (fidPlay tavern/day/moon). Playing a learned tune's 8 notes any time is recognised (JAM.h, lesHeard): near Fergus he says bravo, else a line. His first reaction to your whistle is now a lesson offer (JAM.offer); no small talk 90 s after a lesson. His bow follows the lesson phrase (fidBow uses LES.P during the demo). FIX: with Music off in Options (sounds on) the whistle was silent, because the music bus hushes when musSync0 returns early; jamBus() wakes it on every whistle note and during a lesson. Tests: t229; t226/t227 sandboxes got a LES stub, t228 accepts the offer line. Probe: the lesson runs start to finish in headless Chromium (desktop + phone).
- v2.28.10 (2026-09-29): A TIN WHISTLE (page only). Type /play (or /jam /whistle /flute /tune) to take out a tin whistle: keys 1-8 (Digit/Numpad, capture-phase listener that ignores inputs and modifiers) or the on-screen bar play 8 notes of the scale of whatever tune is playing (jamScale: MUS.p mood, else MUS.mood, else tavern; root folded into 60-71 then +12) on the 'rec' voice through a gain into MUS.bus. Walking, attacking, blocking, dodging, jumping, swimming, looting, dying, an emote or a new run put it away (jamTick). Pose: new emote branch 'jam' in rigPoseHum with IK-solved arm angles (jamfk.js, hands at .13/.28 of the 42 cm whistle; separate set for the wolfman snout), fingers lift on each note (JAM.k). Whistle = green mouthpiece + silver tube + 6 holes, child of the torso. Weapons stow while playing (bow on the back stays). Gold notes float up from the whistle (fidNotes with per-pool origin ox/oy/oz). Fergus reacts within 7 m on the 6th note (max every 90 s): with no tune and music on he strikes up the tavern jig under you, else one of 3 lines. The bar (.jam-bar) is appended to .stage (NOT body: the fullscreen .arena has z 2147483000), bottom right on desktop, a centered 4x2 grid above the joystick on phones. Only you hear/see it (not networked). Tests: t228.
- v2.28.9 (2026-09-29): THE GARDEN DANCES (page only). musPulse() gives the pulse of whatever tune plays (beats since its t0, a jig/shanty pulse = 3 eighths; null in silence, hushed or in a tune's 2.5 s tail). Your /dance keeps time with it (animRig emo.ph -> dp=ph*PI; the cubs' chase dance and silence keep eu*5.2). Stay folk other than Fergus nod on each pulse (folkBob -> st.bob added to the head pitch hx). Fergus sways on the pulse, and gold eighth-note sprites (canvas texture, pool of 6, raycast off, children of his folk group) float up beside his fiddle: one per new bow stroke at most every .45 s, rising .6/s for 1.7 s (fidNotes, spawn x .34-.44, y 1.6, z .4 so they clear his hat). Tests: t227 (pulse, nod, notes with fake sprites); t226's pose regex now allows the bob key.
- v2.28.8 (2026-09-29): FERGUS THE FIDDLER (page only). A new stay folk in the Sleepy Hen's garden, plan {id:'fergus',stay:'fiddle'} at tl(-1.9,.5) facing the village start, FOLK_NAME fergus:'Fergus' (name tag, target window via S.folk, hail), FOLK_LOOKS.fergus (Rooster's feathered hat in green/red/yellow, beard, burgundy tunic, cream sleeves, mustard scarf), FOLK_SAYS.fergus (3 lines as you pass). fidRig puts a fiddle (fidFiddle, x1.4) and a bow (fidBowPcs, x1.3) on the torso bone; basis in FID (vc, cp, d, n, b; bow lift .042 above the hand line). rigPoseHum st.fiddle: pR blends FIDA, six right-arm poses solved offline for the bow position s in [0,1] (bow contact .06-.56 from the frog; hand within 1.6 cm of the bow line), pL=FIDL on the neck, a small sway while playing. fidBow(f,t): on the tavern jig or a request (MUS.p.k 'tavern' or 'fg_*') each lead ('fid') note turns the bow (stroke length from the note length, eased), rests between tunes, saws a 1 s sine with the music off or out of earshot. Talk: fidNear (targeted within 4 m, or within 1.8 m and nearer than Chomp) sits after cubNear in the hail/talk/emote chains; folkNear skips him; npcOpen (double-click / U) plays his pick; E.npcReply wrapper -> fidReply: stop/quiet, mood keywords (FID_K: jig, shanty, lullaby, spooky, moon, village, battle, drone), encore/again, play/tune/anything (his pick, not the one playing), name, thanks, bye, hellos, else the menu; notes when Sounds or Music are off. fidPlay(k) makes MOODS['fg_'+k] = the place's mood with lead 'fid' and gap 3-8 s, sets MUS.req and switches at once; musSync0 swaps m 'tavern' for MUS.req (walking out of the garden or a boss clears it; it ends with its piece, then the jig). fidStop rests 30 s. t219's talk-chain regex accepts fidNear. Probe: talk 'play a shanty please' from his side -> fg_harbor with 46 fiddle notes, the bow tracking; target + double-click + hail work; fg4/fg5 (frog/tip), fg7 (medium shot). t226 (41 checks).
- v2.28.7 (2026-09-29): BATTLE DRUMS AND A BOSS THEME (page only). musFoes(S): any mob alive and aware within 12 m of a living hero is a fight; S.ml (the Moonless) or S.king (the Rat King) aware within 30 m is a boss. musDrums(now,on): while a foe was seen in the last 3 s (and not in the boss mood) a drum layer (own gain, fades in 1.2 s, out .5 s) plays MUS.DR patterns (taiko = 88->38 Hz sine + triangle + noise; tick) on the current piece's grid (half-beats; eighths in 6/8), or its own 120 bpm grid between pieces; never re-queues a slot (D.last). MOODS.boss: D phrygian dominant, 4/4 at 132, a galloping low lute in eighths, a pad, taiko + ticks, a fiddle tune, gap .5-1.5 s; it takes over after .4 s (the old piece fades .8 s, starts .9 s later). When the boss falls (MUS.boss hp<=0 while in the boss mood): the theme cuts (.5 s) and musSting plays a fanfare (lute D F# A D run, recorder D5 F#5 A5, harp chord, two taiko), then 8 s later the place's own tune. Leaving him alive fades his theme, no fanfare. musicSync is now a try/catch wrapper over musSync0 (a throw sets MUS.off: music goes quiet, the game never stalls). Levels: boss RMS .033 (day .024), fanfare peak .22. Probe: a wolf at the hero turns the drums on over the village tune (mfight.png). t225 (26 checks). t223's spot-radius check now allows tree spots (it was flaky, 11/30, a test bug).
- v2.28.6 (2026-09-29): MUSIC, the game's first soundtrack (page only, self-chosen). A procedural composer plus synths, all Web Audio, no files. musMood(S) picks one mood per place and hour: deep (E.sewerIn or the cave), moon (night, <14 m from run.W.moonc), tavern (<10 m from WX.tavern, the Sleepy Hen's beer-garden table, but not inside E.hallIn next door), dark (zoneOf dark), night (elevation < -.15), harbor (port), day. MOODS hold root/mode/meter/bpm, 8-bar chord loops (scale steps), an accompaniment pattern per bar (bass, fifth, arpeggio tones, strums, held pads, drums; optional chance), lead instrument, octave, range, lp (chance a 2-bar stretch has a tune), calm, gap. day D ionian 3/4 lute arpeggio + recorder; harbor A mixolydian 6/8 squeezebox (reed) + lute bass + whistle; tavern G 6/8 jig at 320 eighths, fiddle + lute oom-pah + tabor, gaps 3-8 s; night A dorian harp + pad + low recorder; moon E lydian harp + pad + bells; dark D aeolian pads + low harp, sparse recorder; deep D phrygian drone fifth + stray harp notes + rare bells. musPiece(k,seed) (mulberry32) builds 16 bars: phrase A (4 bars, half cadence), A' (A's first 2 bars refit to the new chords, then home), B (new, higher), A' again, plus the tonic rung out; strong beats snap to chord tones, the rest step. Instruments: Karplus-Strong plucks (lute, harp) cached per 4 semitones (22050 Hz below midi 64, 44100 above with a lighter averaging so high strings ring; f0 = sr/(N+S), playbackRate tunes; checked in tune to 1 cent by autocorrelation), PeriodicWave recorder (vibrato, breath chiff), fiddle, squeezebox (2 detuned), saw pads through lowpass, sine-partial bells, sfxTone/sfxBurst drums. Each instrument gets a lowpass + panner into the piece's fader; MUS.bus (vol .55) + a generated 2.2 s convolver reverb (.3). Offline renders: peaks .08-.18, RMS .01-.027, lead ~4 dB over the accompaniment. musicSync(S) runs after forestSync in sfxSync: mood must hold 1.5 s, then the old piece fades (2.4 s) and the new one starts 2.5 s later; the scheduler queues notes <= .3 s ahead, drops late ones; hush when the world stops drawing (setInterval, like FORA); a throwing audio context sets MUS.off. OPTS.music (default on, old saves keep it on) with a Music checkbox under Sounds; needs Sounds. Also the hide-and-seek count is bigger on phones (clamp 96px/24vw). t224 (30 checks, fake AudioContext). Probe: the village tune starts ~3 s in, the jig at the beer garden, the hall stays on the village tune (mtav2.png).
- v2.28.5 (2026-09-29): HIDE AND SEEK WITH WREN (page only; her own game, from her old line 'I don't run very fast. but I'm good at hiding'). Start: say hide/seek to her by day, or 'yes' within HIDE.ask after she asks (her play line now asks, c.askT), or pat her again after her pet hint (the v2.28.4 pat-again path now covers her, HIDE.hint). You count to ten: #hideEyes, a dark overlay with the number, appended to #view's parent (the full-page arena is fixed at z 2147483000, so a body-level overlay is hidden under it), pointer-events none, fades by itself 1.5 s after the last call. Meanwhile she runs (hideWalk, 6 m/s, round HIDE.O = chaseObs + the altar, tangent slide) to a spot from hideSpots(S): just past each of the six stones (page order a=pi+k*pi/3, kid names HIDE.moons), Greymane and every tree within tmax 7.4 (beyond the flat ground, y from run.W.height via hideY) on the far side from where you stood, >= 3.5 m from you, clear of everything else; or in the den (denL(0,-.3)); never the same key twice running (U.hideLast). Seek HIDE.dur 60 s: walk within 1.5 m, or pet/talk to her close up, and you found her (best time fr3d-hide, a ting, all three cheer); giggles every 8 s from 5 s (sfxPlay giggle, volume by distance, panned by the camera's right vector), a vague chat line at the 1st, ears pop up (she stands and hops 1.8 s) at the 3rd. Tuft and Bramble tell where she is if you ask them anything about playing (no moon-chase while she hides). While hidden she is not in S.folk or the click-pick list beyond HIDE.far 3.6 (no Tab cheat). Ends: time (she creeps out), quit (>14 m), night (Greymane calls). After: cheer, then walks home to the knee round the stones (c.back). t223 (27 checks). Probe shots hs1.png (the count), hs6.png (cheat cam: Wren behind the full moon stone, Greymane beside), hs7.png (found line).
- v2.28.4 (2026-09-29): poke-again: when Tuft or Bramble wriggles free after a pet and dares you ("poke me again and you're it!"), petting them again within 10 s starts moon-chase, no typing needed (on phones a double-tap on the cub pets it). A pet now also ends the cub's old pet pause at the start so it runs at once. t222 covers it.
- v2.28.3 (2026-09-29): BREATH MOONS for moon-chase (page only). A row of little moons floats over each runner (Tuft 4, Bramble 3), one per breath: each duck puts one out, all dark = tired and taggable. chasePip(c,n): one Sprite per runner in the cubs' group (not the cub, so psFit scale doesn't touch it), depthTest off, renderOrder 11, scale m*CHASE.pipS (.22) x .22, CHASE.pipY 1.12 over the cub; canvas textures (32 px per moon: a glowing pale disc with two grey craters, or a dark disc with a pale ring) cached per 'n/m' in CHASE.tex (9 in all). Hidden for a tagged cub and at chaseEnd; shown over a dancing cub too. Sounds: sfx 'giggle' (4 rising triangle chirps) on every duck, 'ting' on a tag, 'cache' on the win (chaseSfx guards typeof sfxPlay for the test sandboxes). The start line shows your best time. t221 (13 checks, a stand-in three.T/canvas). Probe shots mp1.png, mq3.png (Tuft out of breath, four dark moons).
- v2.28.2 (2026-09-29): MOON-CHASE, tag with the cubs (page only). Say play/tag/chase/catch/game/race/run/hide to Tuft or Bramble by day (alive, not night) and you are it: tag both within CHASE.dur 60 s. Runners (chaseStep, circle frame): the clearing between rin 1.35 (the altar) and rout 5.6, with solid rings for the fire .85, Greymane .65, Wren's knee .4, the den 1.25, the six stones (stone+sm .2) and the trees near the edge (TREE_R*s+.15), rebuilt at each start (CHASE.O = chaseObs(); chaseProj = 4 clamp/push passes + a final inner clamp). chaseDir scores 16 ways: .4 x distance from where you will be (hero velocity estimate G.hv, lead .35) + .6 x the best of 8 second steps vs you following at 6 m/s (no dead ends) + sep .5 x min(3, other runner) + continuity - .8 x wall push. Far away (>4.2 m) they dance at you. You are faster (6 vs 5.3), so BREATH: within tag+reach (1.25 m) a cub with breath dodges on its own held line (chosen against where you would be if you kept going, so it often cuts past you; 9 m/s for .45 s, cd .8) and loses a breath (Tuft 4, Bramble 3; stateLine 'ducks under your arm!', 'puffing' at 1); at 0 it is out of breath (3.4 m/s for 3 s, 'get him/her!') and only then can be tagged (.9 m); kept 3.2 m+ away it gets one back every 4 s, and a tired cub you miss gets 1 back. A tagged cub sits facing you; Wren cheers each tag. Ends: both tagged (time, best in localStorage fr3d-chase, rematch line), time up, night (Greymane calls them in), dead or 9 m from the middle (they give up). Running cubs only say busy lines and can't be petted until tagged; petting Tuft/Bramble by day gives a 'say tag' hint. Balance (t220 pure-pursuit chasers): 6 m/s ~8 s, 4.8 m/s ~10 s, 4 m/s never; real players steer worse and the stones block them, so expect 15-40 s. First tries (4.6 m/s runners on the ring inside the stones, then a stamina timer) were caught in 1.5-4.5 s: the hero cuts across the altar and the ring was too small. t220 (19 checks). Probe shots mc3-mc5.png.
- v2.28.1 (2026-09-29): THE CUBS ANSWER (page only). The cubs are friendly targets like Lizzie and Bobby: pickables() lists them and, after folkTick, their spots go into S.folk (so click, Tab and the target frame work, 20/20 hp, never a fight). Open one (double-click / E) = cubPet: within 2.6 m the cub stops for 2.4 s, hops, faces you and waves (rig.greet), with a line per cub; asleep, you stroke it (Wren stops shivering for 30 s, c.calm). E.npcReply is wrapped on the page: a cub name goes to cubReply (hi, a Wolfman hello, name, play, Greymane, the Moonless before/after S.mlDown, furs cold/warm, bye; asleep they only mumble), everyone else to the engine. cubNear(S,r) is checked first by talk, hail and emotes: a targeted cub within 4 m (6 for emotes), else a cub within 1.3 m that is nearer than Greymane. Wren sitting by Greymane now looks at you (and waves once) when you come within 4 m. Voices: Tuft (he) brags, Bramble (she) chatters, Wren (she) whispers. t219 runs the cub code in a stub sandbox.
- v2.28.0 (2026-09-29): THE CUBS' DEN (page only). Greymane's three cubs, Tuft (grey, moss tunic), Bramble (brown, rust) and Wren (pale, moon-blue, the smallest), are humanoid manokit rigs (keys npc-cub0..2, head bone x1.3, psFit DEN.size .56/.54/.47) in the world group. denModel: a hide lean-to in the circle's frame at DEN.at (2.15,-3.72), between stones k0 and k5, open side to the altar (forked poles, ridge pole, sloped hide roof with a sewn patch and moss, a fringed door flap, stick side walls, a straw bed, a stick sword and a rag ball), all one wBaker mesh; the three Shadow Pelts are a second mesh shown when q.moonRite==='done' and you are not a Wolfman. denTick (after Greymane in animCast): by day Tuft and Bramble chase round DEN.play (2.25,.5 r .85) with a hop and Wren sits at DEN.knee by Greymane looking at you (or the fire); once daylight<.3 all three walk to DEN.beds and lie on their sides (sit pose, roll 1.32), Wren shivering without pelts. One chat line per day and per night within 4.6 m (the night one waits until all three lie down). Hidden and skipped past fog.far+12. mcW/denL map circle/den local coords to the world. t218 checks the den/beds/play ring/knee stay clear of stones, altar, fire, Greymane and trees over 60 worlds (both circle facings); trees can stand just outside MOONC.cut 5.8, so the den was pulled in to radius 4.3. Desktop view at the circle 180 draw calls.
- v2.27.2 (2026-09-29): SMOOTHER (page only, for phones). FOG CULL: fogCullHide() before the one r.render and fogCullShow() after it (in a finally) skip every plain mesh (not instanced/skinned/frustumCulled=false, every material fog===true) whose bounding sphere lies wholly past fog.far+2 in VIEW DEPTH (three's linear fog uses view z, not distance); list rebuilt once a second (FOGC, __F3.FOGC; FOGC.on=false turns it off). MERGES: mergeMs(T,meshes,mat,vc) bakes pieces into one geometry (vc = vertex colours from each piece's material colour; r128 has no colour management so it looks identical); mergeKids(T,group,vc,keep) merges a group's unnamed childless opaque pieces (plain Lambert/Toon -> one vertex-coloured mesh per type with vc, others per shared material; skips mirrored pieces and keep). Used on the bat body (by material only: the fur still flashes when hit), the tavern cat (21 -> 6 meshes), Moon Light (18 -> 11; her legs and eyes are kept, moonPose moves them), each cache (7 -> 4) and the cave mushrooms + halos (41 -> 3). Measured at 1280x720: start area 320 -> ~205 draw calls, the cave 303 -> 209, the Dark Forest 229 -> 144. Next candidates: campfire flames (7 per camp, each flickers on its own: would need a shader), moonCircle (14), lodge/hall interiors. t217 (and t216's stamp check is now v2.27.1 or later).
- v2.27.1 (2026-09-29): the Moonless Mantle is visible: rigMlMantle() on the torso bone (two fur shoulder pads with tip spikes, an 11-cone ruff open at the front so the pendant shows, a flattened-sphere fur drape with a 5-cone fringe down the back), scaled to arm width, shown when equip.shoulder==='mlmantle'. Gear bit 1024 for net peers (mask 2047). Greymane notices it once per run (S.elderMl, after her rite/pelt errands, only when worn), and 'mantle' is a Moonless topic. t216. MOBS.moonless (lv 7, 150 hp, dmg 6-11, speed 5.6, leash 14, 900 xp, back 1e9 so the respawn timer never raises him), appended as the very LAST mob in makeWorld (after the Moon Circle, no dice; skipped with MOONC.skip), in the middle of the far shadow wolf pack (~151,-5, near Duskhollow and the gaunts), role 'haunt'. S.ml/S.king are found at makeRun (the sewer's K0 check now uses S.king, not 'last mob'). mlTick: he rises at dark on the new-moon night (ds.phase>.5, e<0, moonOf f===0), once per new moon (S.mlN), with a realm-wide line + a deep page howl (S.mlRise); melts away at dawn unless aware (hunting you). Kill: Moonless Mantle (shoulder, armor 4, unsellable) + 2 Shadow Pelts on his body, S.mlDown, dusk faction +40, coins 90-160. Model: wolfModel(1) at ML_S=1.9 with a silver ruff (two rings of swept-back cones on the body bone), a grey saddle, and additive star eyes on the head bone. Name is 'Moonless' (the game adds 'the'). Greymane talks about him (moonless/alpha/leader/big wolf/new moon). Tests t212/t213/t214 now compare only the older mobs. - v2.25.1 (2026-09-29): the moon-blessed shimmer: moonAuraTick(S) (called at the top of sewerTick, next to trophyTick) keeps one additive Points (MAURA, 18 motes, size .2, canvas radial sprite, fog off) spiralling up 1.9 m around three.hero while S.moonK>0; each mote fades by vertex colour (sin of its climb), the whole thing eases in and out (MAURA.a). Re-adds itself if the scene changes. 1 draw call. __F3.MAURA for probes.
- v2.25.0 (2026-09-29): MOON PHASES + the Wolfman's moon (fannar22's todo 'a real moon bonus at night?'). Engine: MOON {cyc 6, dmg .2, spd .08, dawn=asin(.15)/2pi}; moonOf(t) -> {n=floor(t/DAY_LEN), k=n mod 6, f=(1+cos(2pi k/6))/2 (1,.75,.25,0,.25,.75), wax k>3, name}. A run's first night is full, the 4th new. moonK() = f for a Wolfman at night (elevation<-.15), alive, not in the sewer (x>SEWER.edge); moonTick() after sewerTick: once a night the full moon (non-Wolfmen) / new moon line (S.moonN); off->on: first time a night S.howls++ and the big line (S.moonHowl), later 'The moonlight finds you again'; on->off: dawn 'The moon sets' / sewer 'cannot feel the moon'. S.moonK drives: melee() r.moon=round(capped dmg*.2*mk) added PAST the level cap; run speed x(1+.08*mk); fxList 'moon' (Moon-blessed, glyph moon, t = till dawn). No dice: every mob walks as before (t210, 21 checks; t206 now accepts the cat's moon lines). Page: sky shader uMF/uMW: a terminator across the disc (mp = tangent-plane coords / .0465, not .0436, or a dark sliver shows at full), the dark side col*.3 (hides stars), halo x(.12+.88f); the sky and the chip orb use moonOf(t-.3*DAY_LEN) so the phase turns over at mid-morning with the moon down. The sky chip says 'full moon' / 'new moon' on those nights, title 'tonight: <name>'; the orb's moon gets an inset shadow (--mdk). /howl emote (EMO_K index 6, older clients ignore it): pose muzzle up, sfx 'howl' (3 osc, 330->620->370 Hz with vibrato), a wolf answers from afar at night (farHowl), online heroes within 40 m hear your howl. hud() watches S.howls: the moon makes you howl (pose if standing still). Moon Light: 'bolt upright' at a full moon, 'personally offended' at a new moon (moonCatLine).
- v2.24.0 (2026-09-29): CHOMP'S CROWN, a side job tying fannar22's grumpy King to their Chomp. Engine: q.ratQuest locked/hunt/return/done (questState, questNorm). Stand by Chomp (npcFree, dist<=V.tradeR) with the tutorial done at level 4+ (CHOMP_Q.lv) -> 'hunt' (offer line; offerHave if the crown is already in your bag). Killing the king in 'hunt' -> 'return' ('Chomp will want to see that crown.'). Back at Chomp with a crown_r (invCount>0; invUse 1), or in 'return' with gear off: 'done', +200 coin, +600 xp (S.xpGain), port faction +FAC_T.quest, S.rewardFx. 'return' without a crown: 'no crown, no proof' once per visit (S.chompNo). questText tops the line with CHOMP_Q.hunt/ret while the job runs. NB E.invAdd on a stack item leaves cnt 0 (invCount 0): tests use E.invStack. No dice used: every mob walks as before (t209, 22 checks). Page: questWhere -> the Rat King (hunt) / Chomp (return); wpAim case 2 (you up top, waypoint down in the sewer) aims the chip ('manhole · N m'), the beam and wpQuest at the manhole; the realm map pins such a waypoint on the manhole and labels 'the manhole' (mapPlaces); the sewer inset rings it. rkCrownPcs() is now a named function (T=three.T inside) shared by the king and trophyTick: the crown (rigProp 'rkcrown', TROPHY k1.7 y+.06 tilt .14) on top of the Sleepy Hen's sign (WX.tavern.sign) for heroes whose ratQuest is done.
- v2.23.0 (2026-09-29): SEWER POLISH. Sounds: sfx 'squeak' (two triangle chirps, random pitch) when the count of aware sewer rats rises, 'squeal' (sawtooth+triangle falling, bandpass burst, twice) when the Rat King wakes or dies (SFX.kw/kd/ra, only while underground; SFX.kd seeded silently so a loaded kingDown never squeals), plus faint far squeaks every 4-13 s in forestSync ug (FORA.rq). Waypoint: wpAim(h) returns the ladder [SEWER.x+lad.x, SEWER.z+lad.z,1] while you are underground and the waypoint is up top; wpHud/wpSync aim the arrow, chip ('ladder · N m' / 'the ladder') and the gold beam (rises out of the shaft, looks great) there and never arm/clear the waypoint underground; wpQuest measures from the manhole and says 'Climb the ladder first.' Map: mapSewer(g,cw,ch,S) draws an inset top-left (over the sea) while underground: dark box, brick runs, green channels (SEWP.chan), heap, ladder glyph, aware rats as red dots, the king's crown once S.kingSeen (an x once he is dead), your arrow. Size min(ch*.5,230) tall, capped at cw*.38 wide so the manhole marker at the map centre stays visible on phones. __F3.mapSewer/wpAim.

- v2.21.0 (2026-09-29): THE DOMINOS SEWERS, step 1 (fannar22 09-28 'I think a sewer will be the neglected part within Dominos Port'). Engine: SEWER {x:300,z:0,y:0,edge:K.XE+60,hole,drop,lad,out,R rects,rats}; sewerIn/sewerOk/sewerPush; height() flat past edge; zoneOf 'sewer'; collide/mobSlide/spotOk use sewerPush/sewerOk past edge; sewerTick (hint once per run within 3.2 m, arm 1.25 so no bounce, S.sewEv/sewTo/sewDown). 15 'rat' mobs (sewer rat lv2 hp9, role scurry) APPENDED LAST on their own dice (srd seed^0x5e3e; calm() swaps wr->rw for m.sew) so older mob ids and walks are byte-identical. The manhole (SEWX.hole) sits in the port grass at SEWER.hole, y from the run's height. - v2.20.0 (2026-09-29): MOON LIGHT, the second cat (fannar22 09-26 'the cat on the Window ceil, let name it Moon Light', 09-28 'can we call the cat Moon Light?' + 'keep tavern cat sloppy'; the voice promised her). A silver-grey cat with a pale crescent on her brow in the Sleepy Hen's front window box (WX.tavern.sill {x,y,z,a}: the window at .62,1.12,1.11 of the HUT_Y-scaled hut, box top .855*HUT_Y, 1.18 out; a = the way out of the window). By day a breathing loaf, eyes shut; walk within 3.4 m and she half wakes, ONE eye opens, head turns to you. From daylight < .3 she sits up and watches the moon (sky uMoon direction, head yaw clamped 1.1, pitch up to .62) and turns to you when near. One chat line per day-half within 2.8 m (MOON_SAYS). Name tag 'Moon Light' #d4e4ff. TAVERN_SAYS gains a line (Chomp: she only wakes once the lanterns are lit). __F3.moonCat. t206.js (24 checks, engine byte-identical to pre41).
- v2.19.0 (2026-09-29): THE HUNT (wolves chase the deer back). ENGINE: HUNT={see:16,dur:7,sprint:1.5,cd:45,far:24} next to ROLES; huntStep(m,T,dt,home) runs first in calm() for kind 'wolf' when S.prey exists: a calm non-pack wolf with a deer within 16 m sets m.hunt {id,t} (S.hunts++), walkTo()s the deer at speed*1.5 (7.5 m/s; deer bolt at 9, so they get away) until 7 s pass, the deer is gone from S.prey, or it is rad+24 from home; then m.huntCd=45 and the old home branch trots it back. Pack members copy their calm lead's hunt. NOT m.chase (that's the existing no-leash hero chase). Nothing changes without S.prey (t205 compares every mob against pre40 over 60 s). PAGE: wildSpawn gives each animal an id (WILD.n), wildTick hands the deer over as S.prey=[{id,x,z}] each frame; the deer already run from wolves (wildThreat). Emergent: a hunting wolf can run within 6 m of you and turn on you. Probe hu3-6.js (sim is ~.07x speed in headless; set document.title='SW' from cdpeval to take the shot when you want it).
- v2.18.0 (2026-09-29): JUMPING FISH at the harbor (my todo; Tatum's show-don't-tell thread). leapTick(S,ds) after starTick. Within 45 m of the shore (not indoors), every 6-14 s (x.55 at dawn/dusk daylight .12-.6, x.6 in a light rain .1-.6, x1.4 in a downpour, x1.6 at night) leapSpot() picks water 7-30 m out, mostly inside the view (W.height<sea-.5, x<shore-3, clear of the pier (E.onDeck +-1.2), the boat hull and the port walls |z|>37 east of x -68). 40 % are only rises (rings + a quiet plip), the rest a fish (lathe body with vertex colours dark back/silver/white belly, Phong for a glint, forked tail on a wagging pivot, dorsal fin, eyes; pool of 3) on a ballistic arc (dur=2*sqrt(2(h+.3)/9.8)), pitch from the arc slope, a roll that flops. Splash on the way out (small) and in: 2 expanding RingGeometry ripples (pool 12), a Points spray (90 slots, gravity), sfxPlay('plip'|'plunge') panned and faded by distance. Once a game day, 1 in 10 past the pier end is a BIG one (scale ~2, 1-1.3 m high, plunge sound). 30 % (70 % big) send the nearest visible gull (birdsTick stores b.px/py/pz and Bd.vG) into a dive: b.dv {x,z,t0,dur 1.9+d/14}, wings held up in a V down to the water, splash at q .42, flapping back to its circle. ENGINE: S.rises gets [x,z,S.t] per jump/rise (last 8); riseNear(S,x,z) (exported) = a rise within 6 m in the last 30 s; fishChance(p,rain,rise) doubles again (stacks with rain); the catch line says 'doubled where the fish were jumping'. t204.js tests it (600 sim casts: .31 near vs .17 far at 45 Fishing). Probes: __F3.leap({x,z,yaw,big,rise,gull,pin}), __F3.splash(x,z,sz,vol), a gull's b.dv.pin; lf1-5.js. GOTCHA: page functions aren't window globals, add a __F3 hook instead of calling them from a probe.
- v2.17.0 (2026-09-29): FALLING STARS (Tatum: shooting stars as a rare night event). starTick(S,ds) after owlTick. While sky uStar>.5, rain<.08 and not indoors, a meteor every 35-80 s (first 12-37 s in): a camera-facing quad (starMesh, additive, depthTest on so trees hide it, fog off, renderOrder -8) moved along az/el at 85 m around the camera, placed in the upper part of the current view (el from cam pitch + vfov*.6-.9, az within the horizontal fov, so phones see them too), .6-.95 s. One in four, max one per game day and none while a fallen one is out, falls: 1.9 s down to el -.02 behind the trees, then starLand() picks a spot 35-60 m out in that direction (land, x in shore+12..118, |z|<ZW-4, no trees, not in E.lodgeIn/E.hallIn), 1.2 s later sfxPlay('starfall') (a far thump + shimmer, panned) and a chat line. fallenMesh: glowing icosahedron core, additive sprite halo, a 26 m additive open cylinder beam (fresnel-ish soft edges, fog off so it's a beacon), a soft scorch decal. Walk within 1.8 m: +60-119c to S.wallet (and S.loot), +20 xp via S.xpGain, cache sound + coinClink. At daylight>.35 an unclaimed one fades over 6 s with a line if you're within 70 m. Probe: __F3.star({fall,pin,land:[x,z]}), st1-6.js. GOTCHA: lodgeIn/hallIn aren't client globals, use E.lodgeIn/E.hallIn (a probe caught the ReferenceError).
- v2.16.0 (2026-09-29): THE OWL. owlTick(S,ds) after flyTick. At night (daylight<.15, rain<.3, not indoors/swimming/at sea) an owl calls every 40-90 s (first 20-50 s in): sfxPlay('owl') is a soft hoo ... hoo-hoo-hooo from sine/triangle glides ~400->340 Hz with a breathy bandpass, panned randomly. Sitting rabbits within 30 m go to 'freeze' 2-3.5 s. One chat line per game day ('An owl calls, somewhere off in the dark.'). 60% of calls (always with __F3.owl()) a pale barn owl (owlMesh: pale body/head/face disc, buff back, cream ShapeGeometry wings, ~2.3 m span at scale 1.2, not raycastable) glides across 1.2-2.2 s later: from the edge of the view to the other edge at 12-16 m / 9-12 m out (span from cam fov*aspect + 5 m, so phones get a short crossing too), 5-6 m/s, at the camera height + tan(pitch+.14..22)*dist (a little above the middle of the view), never under ground+2.2; flaps in the first third and last fifth, glides with a slight dihedral between. Probe: __F3.owl({k,a,b,y}) pins a pass; owl1-4.js. GOTCHA: setInterval probes get starved at night in headless (fireflies+wolves), pin state through a hook instead.
- v2.15.0 (2026-09-29): TRACKER'S EYE (Tatum's idea), a settings switch (OPTS.track, off by default; __F3.track(v) for probes). trackTick() after wildTick: a sprite sign over each animal (… at ease, ? wary, ! running, * the fox hunting; canvas textures cached per glyph, scaled up with camera distance) and one LineSegments (depthTest off, 240 verts): amber/red line to what it's wary of/running from (wildTick now stores a.th), green to the fox's pounce spot, a white arrow on the ground for its heading, a red dash for a fleeing mouse. Lines are drawn twice 5 cm apart so they read thicker (WebGL lines are 1 px). GOTCHA: trackTick runs before OPTS exists on the first frames, guard typeof OPTS. Probe tk1.js.
- v2.14.0 (2026-09-29): FIREFLIES (Tatum's idea, to balance the misty mornings). flyTick(S,ds) after wildTick: 90 additive points in one draw call (FLIES.m), anchors 2.5-19.5 m round you (biased near), respawned beyond 21 m, never over the sea; each drifts on its own sine loop and flashes on its own 2.6-5 s period (flash done in the shader from uT and aSeed). Amount = night (daylight .5 to .22) x no rain x (1 - .55 mist), eased; hidden indoors/swimming. In the Dark Forest they turn pale blue and a little bigger (uSz .34 to .5). One game-chat line per game day. Tatum found the first size too big (fairy-like), so they're .34 m now. Probes: ff1.js (dusk at 40,0; FLIES.force=1), ff3.js (counts in view), ff5.js (Dark Forest, phone).
- v2.13.1 (2026-09-29): what the fox dives onto (Tatum's question): a field mouse. foxLand() on each landing: ~30% catch (the mouse rig is parented to the fox's skull bone, the fox 'leave's with head up; wildGone unparents it so a pooled fox never respawns carrying one), ~30% miss (the mouse zigzags off for 1.4 s at 2.8 m/s, shrinking into the grass, the fox turns after it), else dig. One game-chat line within 20 m, at most every 45 s. WILD.pf forces 'catch'/'miss'/'dig' for probes (ms1.js, ms3.js). The fox also sits lower now (body .37, shorter legs).
- v2.13.0 (2026-09-29): THE FOX. foxModel() (wolf rig kind, bones like the deer, cache key 'fox', ~.75 m to the ear tips) and foxStep(): trot / sniff / jump (the mousing pounce: crouch, arc .62 m high over .8 s with body pitch -.75 to +1.2, legs tucked, nose-first landing) / dig / watch (at 14 m, faces you 1.2-2.8 s, then 'leave') / leave (trots off at 3.1) / flee (<7.5 m, or 11 if you run; 7.5 m/s). It leaves by itself after 70 s. At most one fox, spawn chance per 1.2 s tick .035 by day and .12 at dusk/night, none in heavy rain. Rabbits treat a fox as a threat (0.8x its distance). Probes: fx1.js (zoomed, fov 24, profile pounce; keep setHero retrying until the hero is really at 40,0), fx7.js (states).
- v2.12.1 (2026-09-29): the wild keeps an eye on the wolves. wildThreat() picks the nearest threat per animal: the hero, or any living mob in S.mobs (wolves and shadow wolves count at 0.7x their distance, other monsters at 1.35x), and deerStep/rabbitStep alert and flee from it. A fleeing deer now wheels round before it bounds off (speed x max(.12, cos of the turn left)), so it no longer charges at the threat for a second. Probe: wd7.js (setHero(40,0), pin the nearest wolf 14 m ahead, spawn deer and a rabbit between).
- v2.12.0 (2026-09-29): MISTY MORNINGS. mistAt(t): day d=floor(t/DAY_LEN+.5), 42% of dawns (wHash(d*1.37,4.2)), peak .7-1, from phase -.1 to .15 round sunrise (phase 0). mistTick(S,ds) before worldDay eases MIST.amt (.35/s); k() mixes fog/hor/mid toward a pale warm grey, fog near x(1-.7m), far x(1-.5m); 28 billboard mist banks (one draw call, own fade 1.5-6 m and 28-40 m, drift +x) round the hero, hidden indoors; chat 'Mist lies low over the forest this morning.' once a dawn. Off with the weather option. __F3.mist(v) forces it. t203.js checks the share (~.45) and no mist at noon/night.
- v2.11.0 (2026-09-29): WILDLIFE. wildTick(S,ds) after birdsTick. Up to 2 deer (35% stags with antlers) and 3 rabbits (1 at night; rain >.4: 1 deer, no rabbits) spawn every 1.2 s check at 22-37 m, never in the camera's forward cone (dot>.3), via wildOk (x from shore+9 to DARK_X-7, |z|<ZW-3, deer x>3 and 20 m off START, rabbits 9 m, not indoors, no tree within 1.1). Gone past 48 m (or 18 m when you go indoors/swim); models pooled per key (dd/ds/r). Deer = rigAssemble('wolf', ...) with an extra skull bone so rigPoseWolf walks them; states graze/look/walk/alert/flee; head drop = head.x += g*1.75, skull.x = -g*.9, body.x += g*.16. Alert under 10 m (15 if you run), then bolt at 9 m/s with bounding, wildAvoid steers round trees and the map edge. Rabbits = own 5-bone rig, sit/freeze(<11 m)/flee(<6.5 m) with zigzag hops. Not targetable (body.raycast off). WILD.calm = probe flag that stops the scaring; __F3.WILD, __F3.wildSpawn.
- v2.10.0 (2026-09-29): BIRDS. birdsTick(S,ds) after worldDay in drawFrame. 32 birds in two InstancedMeshes (bodies: 4-sided cone; wings: 2 per bird, hinge on z, left one mirrored with scale(-1,1,1), DoubleSide Lambert, instanceColor). 3 forest flocks of 7 in a loose V circle (r 9/12/14) round anchors 16/24/30 m ahead of the camera, lagging (k .2/s), jump when >60 m off; y gy+6..9.6 so they sit in the sky band (the camera pitches ~13 deg down, 55 fov: anything above ~14 deg elevation is off screen). Flap/glide cycles. 5 gulls (white x1.5 to read against the sky) over the sea when hero.x < shore+70; 6 bats round the hero at night at head height. Rain sends them off (flocks by .12-.4, gulls .5-.9), none indoors (rainIn). Reduced motion: smaller flaps. __F3.BIRDS for probes.

A thud on landing (by S.landV) and a splash when S.splash rises. Probe (per-frame setHero walk, sfxPlay logged): grass sg, pier sw, sand ss, Dark Forest sl, errs []. The chat line says which way to look (Dark Forest or sea). renderOrder -5, fog off, trees in front hide it.

Draw calls 250 -> 252 in rain.

RAIN (declared with WU so worldDay can read it) eases toward it; OPTS.weather (#optRain) off = clear. rainMesh: 2000 camera-wrapped streak quads, fall + wrap in the vertex shader off WU.uTime/uCam, one draw call, hidden inside lodge/hall/shack/cave (rainIn). worldDay: RR greys sky/fog/clouds (k()), fog near*(1-.4RR) far*(1-.3RR), sun*(1-.62RR), hemi*(1-.14RR). Over .62: lightning every 16-46 s (double flash via RAIN.flash on hemi + sky, skipped with reduced motion) and thunder() .5-3 s later; chat lines on start / first thunder / stop. Rain hiss loop (muffled lowpass 1300 under a roof), birds hush over .25, crickets over .4, wind x(1+.9RR). sfxBurst noise now loops (long bursts). Test hook __F3.rain(v|null), __F3.RAIN. Probe: amt 1 -> fog far 34, streaks visible desktop + phone night, flash lifts hemi .53->.62, hidden in the lodge, errs [].
- v2.6.0 (2026-09-29): the forest and the fights make sound, all synthesized in the page (no engine change). sfxSync(S) from drawFrame watches engine timers for rises: h.swing (whoosh), h.shot (twang), m.hit within 16 m / knight.hit / dummyGlow (thwack, louder when closer), hp drop (grunt, or the fall sound at 0), S.kills (thump), S.misses (ting), cacheFound() count (sparkle arpeggio + coinClink). forestSync: looped filtered-noise wind (gusts, fades in from the shore, x>PORT.shore+20), Dark Forest drone 55/55.6/82.4 Hz (x>DARK_X-6), birds by day (daylight>.45), crickets + owl at night (elevation<-.15), far howls in the Dark Forest; a 500 ms interval hushes the loops when the world stops drawing. OPTS.sound turns it all off; label now 'Sounds: the forest, your fights, the harbor bell'. Probe: AC running, wind .019 at x=40, drone .027 at x=112, no errors.
- v2.5.0 (2026-09-29): hidden caches. Engine: CACHE (8 x-bands, reach 1.4, glint 16, all-found bonus 100c, flavour lines), makeWorld places one per band last with its own dice (seed^0xcac4e) so nothing older moves: off the path (>=4), no tree within 2 m (pine skirts hid chests at 1.25 m), a tree within 3.4, clear of camps/cave/lamp/Knight clearing/town/hall/mobs. cacheTick (after markTick) opens one on walk-up: coins + xp + a line, 'caches found here N of M', last one pays CACHE.all. Found list per hero per forest in S.caches {seed:[band ids]} (cachesNorm, heroNorm, saveP keeps realm + current seed, makeRun opts.caches). Page: cacheSync builds toon chests (lid group opens to -1.9, coins show inside) with an additive glint sprite while shut and within 16 m; cacheHud says 'Something glints between the trees.' once per cache per session (not within reach, so it never overwrites the found line); map draws opened chests and the header adds 'caches N/8'. Test t201.js.
- v2.4.0 (2026-09-29): 'show the way' chip (#qWay, dashed gold) under the quest line whenever questWhere() leads somewhere and no waypoint is set; one tap pins the waypoint there (wpQuest) and the waypoint chip takes its place. qwayTick runs every 500 ms from hud. New players get a way to their first quest without opening the map.
- v2.3.0 (2026-09-29): fog on the map. 4 m cells over MAPB (60 x 21), a cell is seen once you pass within 24 m of its centre (fogTick, every 400 ms from hud). Saved in localStorage 'fr3d-seen' {seed: 6-cells-per-char string}, keeping the realm and your latest wild forest; saves at most every 4 s, and on pagehide / hidden tab. Drawn as one 60 x 21 parchment-haze image, smoothed, over land, trees, buildings, camps and the cave; place and folk names in unseen cells are skipped, region names stay. The quest, pins, bodies, heroes online and you are drawn on top. The header shows 'N% explored' (land cells only: x > PORT.shore and |z| < 40, so the sea does not count). Option 'Fog on the map' (OPTS.mapFog, default on). Old players start fogged: there was no record of where they had been.
- v2.2.0 (2026-09-29): the quest on the map. questWhere(S) mirrors questText's branches (dark elf orcs/Grudge, ranger target/Bob/wolves, Enzo, the dummy, goblins, the Cursed Knight's clearing) and returns {x,z,n,k}; for hunts it is the nearest living mob of kind k (only kind 'goblin' counts for Enzo's quest). The map draws every living foe of kind k as a red cross and the place as a red wax seal with '!' and its name. 'pin quest' (#mapQuest, hidden when the quest leads nowhere) and a tap on the seal pin the waypoint there. Phone: the map buttons stay on one row (max-width 30rem rule). KEEP questWhere in step with questText when quests change.
- v2.1.0 (2026-09-29): waypoints. A tap on the map (under 6 px of pointer travel; a drag still pans) pins a spot, a tap within ~17 px of the pin clears it. The pin: a gold teardrop with a dotted line from you and the distance. In the world a gold additive shaft (outer r .95 + core r .17, 34 m, fades upward, fog:false) with a ground ring, built lazily in three.sc by wpSync. A chip `#wpHint` beside the corpse chip points the way (same camYaw-bearing arrow as corpseHud) and counts metres; click it to clear. Reaching it (<3.5 m, once you were >6 m away) says 'You reach your waypoint.' and clears it. Saved in localStorage 'fr3d-wp' {x,z,seed}; only shown in the forest with that seed, so a wild forest hides it and the realm brings it back. Double-click on the map no longer follows you (it fought with tap-to-pin); the follow-me button does. Probe: mkoven who=wp (&shut=1 closes the map and faces the beam, &clr=1, &arr=1; nested setTimeouts in probes often don't report before done(), read them live with scratchpad cdpeval.py instead).
- v2.0.1 (2026-09-29): the map was mirrored. Three.js is right-handed: facing +z, +x is on your LEFT (probe who=mir: camera right = (-1,0,0) at yaw 0). The map now draws +x right and +z DOWN (Y(z)=ch/2+(z-cz)*ppm, base rows from z0, pan and zoom signs flipped, hero arrow rotate(PI-yaw)), so turning left in the forest turns left on the map. No north mark: the realm's text says +z is north (Mira's hall 'north of the huts', the lodge 'south side'), but the zone names put West Dominos Port at -x; an unmirrored map can't honour both, it keeps west on the left.
- v2.0.0 (2026-09-29): THE MAP (M, toolbar map button, remappable as ACTS 'map'). Now that the realm is one forest it can be drawn: a parchment canvas in #mapWin (draggable like the other windows). mapBase paints the ground once per seed at 1 px a metre (W.height hillshade, sea by depth under PORT.sea, ink shoreline, W.pathDist roads, zone tints: port sand, forest green-beige, Dark Forest grey-blue, Duskhollow lantern glow) and scales it soft; mapDraw adds crisp vector layers at ~7 fps while open: port walls + towers, every tree, hall/shack/lodge floors and wall rects, the pier and the Enterprise, the huts, well/forge/bank/Duskhollow stalls, the lantern, Snagtooth's cave ring, goblin camps (tent + fire), the knight's clearing when the knight is on, names (regions always, places from zoom ~2, folk from ~5.5), your bodies ('your body'), heroes online (blue + name), your target ring, you (red arrow by yaw, pulsing). Drag pans, wheel/pinch/−/+ zoom 1–10 (fr3d-mapz), 'follow me' or double-click re-centres. Header shows zone and x, z; the canvas aria-label says where you are and which way you face. First open ~60–110 ms headless. Probe: mkoven.py who=map (&z=-1 zoom out).
- v1.99.0 (2026-09-29): ONE REALM. Every hero walks the same forest (REALM_SEED 20260929: hills, 1082 trees, the cave mouth at 62,-31, the mob camps) on every visit and for everyone online, so heroes online stand on the same ground and Snagtooth's cave stays put (it moved with every random seed before). Options → 'A new forest each visit' (OPTS.wild, off by default) brings back a random seed from the next visit; the 'new forest' button and hero switches use newSeed(), which follows the option. Footer says 'the realm forest · seed …' or 'a wild forest · seed …'. From fannar22's online list (shared seed was first after emotes; factions shipped). Probe: mkoven.py who=realm.
- v1.98.0 (2026-09-29): each row of the Factions window shows the last deed that moved it, with a green ▲ or red ▼ and a count when it repeats ("▲ killed a goblin ×4", "▼ struck down Garrick"). facAdd(k,n,why) → facWhy keeps S.fac.why[k]={t,n,up}, saved inside H.fac and trimmed by facNorm (48 chars, n ≤ 999, known factions only); kills, NPC/guard deaths and the three quest rewards pass their deed, a capped standing keeps its last real deed. My own polish after fannar's list. Tests: SP/fr195/t200.js.
- v1.97.1 (2026-09-29): the oven remembers. S.oven is saved with the hero (saveP H.oven, heroNorm and makeRun opts.oven, ovenNorm: four shelves, null or {t seconds left}, done fish stay done, clamped to OVEN.bake); ovenTick picks a saved oven up even before the window is opened. Tests: SP/fr195/t199.js. (Was a todo from v1.95.0.)
- v1.97.0 (2026-09-29): CHOMP'S COUNTER (fannar22 07:24 'a merchant window for Chomp showing he sells 1 ale, ale for 20 within a stack, no getting drunk when clicking Chomp'). ITEMS.ale 'Bottle of Ale' (drink:1, 10c, stack 20); CHOMP_SHOP=['ale']; shopKeep adds Chomp (VILLAGE.chomp within tradeR); Chomp never buys (keeperRefuses, 'I pour, friend'). Double-click / U on Chomp opens the shop window titled 'The Sleepy Hen' (shopTk, npcOpen). drink(i) in the engine: the old counter effect (ALE heal 10, a minute of courage, 4 max, 3 s between) from a bag bottle; the bag click drinks. buyAle()/aleBuy() stay but nothing calls them. Price read as 10c each ('20' = the stack). Chomp is 'they' in the game's lines (Old Pell). Tests: SP/fr195/t198.js; probe mkoven.py who=Chomp&shop=1&d=2.
- v1.96.0 (2026-09-29): FACTION WINDOW + MOVABLE WINDOWS (fannar22 07:17 'I want Faction to have its own window', 07:26 'all windows you can open be moveable, like banking window, loot window, faction window'). #facWin (class inv facwin) holds #facList (moved out of the character sheet, facUI unchanged); toolbar flag button #facBtn (under the character button) and key P (ACTS 'fac', appended so ACTS[12] stays 'char'; keyMapBuild relabels the button); Escape closes; a run ending closes it. Beside the character window when both are open (besideBook also toggles facWin.beside). winDrag now also on bankWin, lootWin, ovenWin, facWin (title bar, positions in fr3d-wins, double-click resets). Phone: the usual bottom sheet. Probe: mkoven.py who=facw (&inv=1).
- v1.95.3 (2026-09-29): fannar22 07:39 'when you kill a monster the clock starts ticking for its respawn time': the clock is up at 90/120 s whatever the body holds; the monster stands and anything left on its body goes with it (bodyGone, silent). Only a body you are kneeling over (S.looting===mi) holds it down; RESPAWN_CLEAR near its home still delays it as before. So bodies now last min(LOOT.decay, respawn time). t197.js covers it.
- v1.95.2 (2026-09-29): RESPAWN TIMES (fannar22 07:28 'goblins every 1 minute 30 seconds, wolf every 2 minutes, skeleton every 2 minutes'). MOBS back: goblin, goblin guard, goblin scout 90; wolf, shadow wolf, skeleton (dark ones too, same kind) 120; everything else keeps RESPAWN 30, Snagtooth 180. guardKill now uses the kind's back too. The clock now runs from the kill, also while the body lies there (it used to start only once the body was gone); a body with loot left still keeps its monster down until it is emptied or crumbles (LOOT.decay 300), so a looted goblin is back 90 s after it fell. Tests: SP/fr195/t197.js (engine steps cap dt at 0.1, measure with 0.1 steps). fannar said no early-warning tell before a respawn (07:38).
- v1.95.1 (2026-09-29): ONE-CLICK LOOT (fannar22 07:19 'when you left click on your corpse and monster corpse it open its loot window'). clickPick first asks corpseAt(x,y) (raycast into three.corpseG, returns the S.corpses index) and loots it with lootGo('c'+i); a click that picks a dead mob with a body sets the target and calls lootGo(i). Out of reach still says 'walk up to the body first'. Headless probe: corpse click -> looting c0, dead bat click -> looting its index, loot window open. A body with only coins still empties and closes, as before.
- v1.95.0 (2026-09-29): SMITH'S OVEN (fannar22 07:11 'an oven in Smith's shop with 4 baking slots, cook a raw fish into a cooked fish, a cooking skill that starts at 0 and goes up every so often'). A brick oven (plinth, body, dome, mortar lines, four mouths, a flue through the roof) against the back wall of the shack on the customer side, by the fish barrel (OVEN shack-local x .95 z -1.82; a SHACK_RECTS rect blocks it). Click it (ovenAt: a ray against an invisible pick box, only within 7 m, and not when a character is in front) to open #ovenWin: four shelves, put a raw fish in / baking N s / take it out. OVEN.bake 20 s; cookBurn = 35 % at 0 Cooking falling to 0 at 50 (snapRoll(seed+11, cooks)); a burnt fish is just gone. Cooking (SKILLS.cook, rise, SKILLUP_FREE) rolls a skill-up on every fish taken out. Cooked Fish: +1 strength for 3 minutes (raw 1 min, fried 2), sells for 6c. The mouths glow orange while baking and gold when done; a thin smoke thread rises from the flue while anything bakes. Smith hints once when you walk in with raw fish; ask him about the oven. S.oven is saved since v1.97.1: fish left in the oven are lost on reload (todo). t196.js. My own additions: burning, the glow, the smoke.
- v1.94.2 (2026-09-29): SUSPICION TAX. fannar 07:08: apprehensive sellers charge 10% more, dubious ones 20% (FAC_T.mark by tier; priceAt(seller,price) rounds up; buy() and buyScroll() charge it, the receipt says '+N% suspicion tax', the shop windows show the marked price with (+N%)). So dubious no longer shuts shops: FAC_T.shun 5 -> 6, traders, trainers and bankers turn you away from threatening on. Training costs are untouched (fannar said items and equipment). Selling back is unchanged. fannar 07:07: every goblin kind (goblin, guard, scout) now gives guard +1 and merchant +1 (was 6/2, 10/4, 8/3); warrior and lodge keep 2/4/3 and 2/4/2. t195.js checks Doug's price at four standings.
- v1.94.1 (2026-09-29): fannar 07:06 'if you kill a merchant or guard you lose -100 faction with them': FAC_T.npc and .guard -100, kin (the other city people) -50, rival +40 unchanged.
- v1.94.0 (2026-09-29) FACTION LADDER (fannar22 06:50-07:05): fannar's 8 tiers replace EverQuest's 9: ally 2001..2500, friendly 1501..2000, warmly 1001..1500, indifferent 501..1000, apprehensive 1..500, dubious -999..0, threatening -1999..-1000, scowl -3000..-2000 (FAC_T.max 2500 / min -3000). Peoples (FAC_KEYS): guard (the Dominos City Guard: Garrick, Wynn), merchant (the Dominos City Merchants: Mira, Doug, Smith, A Shaman Trainer, Rooster), warrior (Enzo), ranger (Bob), dusk (the Duskhollow Merchants: Vesh, A Necromancer Spells), dwar (the Duskhollow Warrior Guild: Grudge; dark elf monsters belong here, FAC_MOB), dnec (the Duskhollow Necromancer Guild: A Necromancer Trainer), lodge (A Shaman Spells), port (A Banker). Starts: human/wolfman indifferent (750) to all four city peoples, dark elf scowl (-2500) there and indifferent (750) to both Duskhollow guilds; human scowl with Duskhollow, wolfman threatening (-1400). Rules (fannar 06:55 "at Dubious guards or merchants stop attacking but watch you closely"): threatening or worse (FAC_T.kos 6) = guards hunt, folk attack, dark elf mobs aggro; dubious (FAC_T.shun/watch 5) = traders refuse, the nearer free guard walks over and keeps FAC_T.gap 4 m off you in town, a FAC_T.eye line every 25 s. Kill/quest numbers doubled for the wider scale (npc -300, guard -200, kin -100 to FAC[k].kin, rival +40, quest +100; Enzo's quest → warrior + guard/2, Bob's → ranger/2, Grudge's → dwar + dusk/2). Plural names (pl:1) use facLine ('the Merchants glower'). Consider line names the people: 'Garrick glowers at you dubiously (level 20 · the Dominos City Guard)'. OLD SAVES (no fac.v): facNorm maps each old value through FAC_V1.map (old tier edges → new edges, straight between) and keeps only what was earned on top of the old race start (FAC[k].v1 names the old people: village for the city four, dusk for the Duskhollow guilds). Tests SP/fr195/t195.js (engine, all pass), sheet shots SP/fr195/mkfac.py (?dub=1 sets guard -500 merchant -400).
