- 2026-09-28 22:1x CHECK, no new version: app_stats still lists "Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'matrixWorld') @three.min.js:6" (5x, 2 visitors) + "Script error. @three.min.js:0" (3x). That file was written 21:02 over a 7-day window, one minute after v1.89.2 went live, so these are the pre-fix skeleton-jaw errors; expect the line to linger until about 2026-10-05. Re-verified in headless (SP/fr192/mkpick.py): on v1.89.1 a real mouse hover and a right-click on a skeleton mob each throw exactly that message @three.min.js:6 (r128 SkinnedMesh.boneTransform, bones[15] undefined); on v1.91.0, 74 model builders / 144 SkinnedMeshes have no skinIndex >= bone count, no rig clamp warnings, 1776 unguarded rays 0 throws, and hover/right-click target the skeleton with 0 errors. The only raycast in the app is pickAt (hover, click, long-press); nothing else hands three an undefined bone/camera (every .project uses three.cam, lookAt gets numbers, getWorldPosition is guarded by o.parent). If the error shows up AGAIN after 2026-10-05, it is a new cause.
- v1.91.0 LAZY CAMERA (coldpresss 15:50 "turn the camera as the player turns around"; they said at 16:06 "camera is already done and good", so this refines the old follow): the old per-frame ease in drawFrame (.06/.18 a frame toward h.yaw, also while standing) is gone. camFollow(dt) runs in tick after hud(): only while h.moving and OPTS.camFollow, k=CAMF.k 2.4/s times max(0,cos(angle to the hero's heading)), step d*(1-exp(-k*dt)) the short way round (atan2). So running away from the camera swings it round in about a second; strafing or running at the camera leaves it alone (with the camera-relative stick a held diagonal makes a steady gentle circle, ~1 rad/s at 30 deg). Standing still never moves it. Keyboard tank turning (camTight) keeps a tight k 12 (≈ the old .18 at 60 fps) even with the switch off, since that scheme steers by the camera. A drag sets d.moved and keeps cam.hold at CAMF.hold (2 s) while held, then counts down after release. Respawn/teleport (hero jumps >6 m in a frame) or start() snaps the view behind the hero (CAMF.lx/lz). Options checkbox #optCam "Camera follows when running" (OPTS.camFollow default true, saved in fr3d-opts). __F3 gains stick and CAMF for probes. Probe SP/fr191/mkcam.py (?m=chk): still 1.2 kept, 30 deg stick 1 s = 1.06 rad, strafe 0, backwards 0, 30 fps vs 144 fps 1.05 vs 1.08, drag 0, 1 s after release 0, 2.1 s after 0.11, switch off 0, tank turn follows (lag .18), 20 m jump snaps. The probe stubs the renderer while stepping (hundreds of SwiftShader renders hung the capture).
- v1.90.0 EMOTES: /wave /cheer /bow /dance /sit typed in chat (/emotes lists them; help card tip mentions them). EMO={k,u} on the hero, EMO_T durations (sit lasts until you move); emoTick ends it on moving, swing, shot, block, dodge, death, air, looting or swimming; refused while moving/air ('stand still for that') or dead/looting. Chat line 'You wave at Bobby.' ('net' class, italic blue, talk filter); target = folkNear(h,6)||npcNear; wave/bow/cheer make that villager wave back (rig.greet=1.8) unless mad, and a wave gets E.npcReply(who,'hello') after 600 ms. Poses in rigPoseHum (st.emo {k,u}) before the camp-life block; ground sit = R.sitGK eases hips to R.hy*.22, thighs -2, knees 1.5 (knees up, feet flat; checked side-on for human and wolfman). Online: postcard e (EMO_K index) + en (counter); both urgent; receiver resets p.emoT on a new en and adds 'Fannar waves.' within 40 m, max one line per peer per 1.5 s; bad e -> 0. Probe SP/fr190/mkemo.py (m=chk functional, m=shot&k=..&u=.. front view, m=peer remote dancer). Probe gotcha: netPost throttles 90 ms, and the frozen hero has dt=0 (EMO.u and sitGK must be set by hand in probes).
- v1.89.2 FIX (app_stats: 2 visitors, 5x "Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'matrixWorld') @three.min.js"): the skeleton's jaw piece sat on bone 15 but the skeleton has 15 bones (jaw is 14), so the jaw rendered collapsed (invisible) and clicking any skeleton (mob or necro pet) threw inside r128 Mesh.raycast -> SkinnedMesh.boneTransform. Fixed the index; rigAssemble now checks each cached rig once and clamps any skinIndex >= bone count to 0 with a console.warn; pickAt wraps the raycast in try/catch (falls back to the nearest-on-screen pick). Probe SP/fr190/mkskin.py lists every SkinnedMesh with max skinIndex >= bones and ray-tests every folk/guard/mob/knight. Lesson: r128 raycasts skinned meshes through boneTransform, so a bad bone index only crashes on click.
- v1.89.0/1 ONLINE (fannar22): one Supabase realtime room 'fantasy-realm-3d:world' (dynamic import of /supabase-config-fixed.js from the classic script, anon sign-in first like card-lounge, fails quietly to offline). Presence tracks {id,n,r,c,f,lv}; broadcast 'pos' postcards (x,z,yaw, gear bits g, action bits a, swing/shot counters) at most 5/s while anything changes, every 2.5 s idle; broadcast 'say' from chatSay (non-command text, 1 line/1.1 s) reaches everyone online. Remote heroes: rigHero of their race/class, slide to the last postcard (snap >8 m), stand on YOUR terrain (forests are per-seed, town is shared), blue NAMES tag (e.pl, follows OPTS.online), a speech bubble sprite over the tag. Hidden after 12 s without a postcard (background tab), dropped 2.5 s after leaving presence or after 120 s; max 8 drawn, within 90 m. Everything from the room is cleaned (netTxt strips control/bidi chars, whitelisted race/class, Object.create(null) maps, textContent only). Options: 'Other heroes online' (off = untrack + removeChannel). Probe: SP/fr189/mkui189.py + fake module gaunt/supabase-config-fixed.js (?fakenet). Gotcha: SwiftShader probes are slow (26 s for ~15 draws), so re-send postcards right before the final frame or the 12 s rule hides them. NOT yet seen against the real realtime server (sandbox can't reach it).
- v1.88.0 YOUR OWN BONES (fannar22 19:1x: corpse keeps coins, loot window for it, 3 real days for levels 1-10; 11+ not set yet, same 3 days): CORPSE={r:1.8,max:3,keep:3*86400}. corpseDrop moves S.coins onto the corpse {x,z,items,coins,born} and zeroes the purse; at max 3 the oldest folds items+coins into the new one. No walk-over pickup any more (corpseLoot still exported, unused). Real clock: makeRun opts.now (page passes Date.now()), S.clock0/clockT, nowMs(); corpsesNorm(a,now) drops corpses older than keep, gives old saves born=now, keeps coin-only corpses. corpseTick crumbles one while you play ('crumbled to dust'). S.looting is a mob index OR the string 'c<n>' (lootSrc()); corpseGone(i) fixes the index. lootOpen() prefers corpseNear() over bodyNear(); coins go straight back to the purse; lootTake re-wears an item marked sl when that slot is empty. Page: lootView.title 'Your corpse' + 'crumbles in 2 d 23 h', pill 'loot your corpse', death overlay + corpseHud say kneel (L). Tests fr188/t188.js 0 FAIL + t187 0 FAIL (t186 is slow, >4 min); browser probe fr188/mkui188.py (st=o death screen, b window after 'new forest', c re-wear, e loot all) all good.
- v1.87.0 THE LOOT WINDOW (fannar22 03:25-03:32, 19:09 "open right beside your bag"): killMob no longer auto-drops coins; bodyMake(m) puts DROPS coins (+ bone / pelt / Snagtooth's crown / the chain) on m.body {coins,items,t,x,z,yaw}. LOOT={r:2.4,decay:300,move:.9,slots:8}. run.lootOpen(i?) (nearest body if no i) kneels: coins straight to the purse, an empty body sinks at once; lootTake(k) → bag (stack or first free slot, returns at), lootAll, lootClose, lootView, bodyNear. The respawn waits until the body is gone; walking off (> .9 from lootAt), dying or the body fading (5 min) shuts it. Page: #lootWin (dirt-brown .slots.loot) shares the vault's spot beside the bag (besideBank handles both); open by double-click on the body (npcOpen ref.t 'mob'), L (ACTS 'loot', rebindable) or the gold pill pinned over the body (projected with three.cam). Items: click, drag onto #slots, or drag onto an .equip .drop (take then wear). Emptied by hand: the window shuts and the bag stays open. Bodies lie on their side (rotation z 1.42 eased in, rigPoseWolf calm=0 when down), gold glint above, sink in the last 10 s; hero kneels (st.kneel, R.kneelK, reach pose). Also: actKey(id) — the auto-attack/provoke hints read ACTS[7] (jump) since v1.36-ish; bagBtn read ACTS[11]; a strike on a fight villager now asks first (provokeAsk(S,'strike')). Tests fr187/t187.js 0 FAIL + t186/t185 + browser probe fr187/mkui187.py (st=a pill, b window, d double-click, c take, e loot all; hold=99 keeps the camera).
- v1.86.0 SNAP (fannar22 03:05: "the fishing pole can break, then you buy a new one"; chance was never set — my pick, fits "punish you along the way"): FISH.snap .04 / snapMin .015, poleSnap(p) falls with the Fishing skill to 1.5 % at 50; snapRoll(seed, S.casts) is a separate hash so fishr's sequence (and old fishing tests) is untouched. On a snap the pole leaves the hand it was in (weapon first), S.snaps++, fishEv k:'snap' (pale ripple); a caught fish still comes in. Smith: 'broke/snap/crack' → "they all snap. that's why I sell them." (before his pole line); the item card says it can snap. Tests fr186/t186.js 11 PASS (3.3 % new / 1.4 % master over 3600 casts) + browser probe.
- v1.85.0 THE PRICE (fannar22: "attack guards and class trainer and merchant", "merchants can always respawn", Doug L20 25 dmg 300 hp, Mira L20 270 hp 22 dmg): engine NPCF (per-NPC lv/hp/atk, roam for the ones in the open, bow for Bob) + NPCF_T (period, reach, back=180 s down, calm=20 s, want=90 s wanted, see=28 from a guard post, hunt=34, xp 150*lv, cry/mad/shut lines). S.npcf created lazily by npcF(); S.wanted. provoke(n) makes an NPC mad (or a guard: crime at its post) and crime() sets wanted only when crimeSeen (zone 'good', within see of a post) — the port (Smith) is lawless. npcTick: roamers step up to `roam` from home via mobSlide, calm down 20 s after you leave their reach, walk home and regen; a down NPC is back after 3 min ('<n> is back.'). guardTick: while wanted the guards hunt you in town within 34 of their posts, routed through the hall door (hallRoute), 1.15x speed; they give up in the port. The 12 near*() + shopKeep + npcNear are gated on npcFree (alive and not mad); npcShut() says whose shop is shut. Naming quirk: 'Enzo' is rig three.trainer, 'Bob' is rig three.enzo. Page: first press of attack on a villager/guard warns (orange line, says if the guards will see), a second within 4 s starts it (provokeAsk in autoToggle); Wanted (eye glyph) in the Effects window; npcPose in animRig (face you, swing, step out, down pose 6 s then hidden until back); pickables only visible rigs; the fallen don't trade or answer hails. Tests: fr185/t185.js 41 PASS (engine) + a browser probe (Mira fight, guards through the door, Mira down + hall shut). Not yet: spells and pets don't hit villagers, no guards in the port or Duskhollow, Chomp/folk not attackable, villager state resets when you die (new run).
- v1.84.0 MIRA'S HALL (fannar22: "enterable merchant hall for Mira with room for a second merchant later", "armor hanging on the wall, same with weapon and shield", north side): engine HALL at (-18.6,10.4) 9.2x6, floor y .3, laid out in a local frame with the door on local +z and turned π (world = H.x-lx, H.z-lz); HALL_RECTS (walls, both counters with returns so nobody gets behind them, stand, the arrow barrel outside) via hallPush in collide() and mobSlide; onDeck floor; height() flattens within 1.5 of it (hallDist-4.5 term); trees within ~2.5 are cut AFTER the tree loop (keeps every seed's rnd sequence); spotOk refuses respawns within 2.5. VILLAGE.merchant = hallW(HALL.mira) (-16.15,12.5); nearMira/shopKeep/npcNear only inside (inside the hall you hear only Mira). Page: hallModel/hallSync (lodge pattern, fade logic in the turned frame), her old stall canopy is the window awnings, the MIRA'S sign, an empty sign bracket on the west half, heirloom blue/gold shield behind her (not for sale — her lines), armor wall (breastplate, leggings, ear muffs, belt/bracers, boots), dressed stand, steel wall (crossed swords, round + kite shield, axe, bow), bare second counter with a dust cloth. rigMira(stall) no longer adds the stall. Folk Hilde's errand is the hall door. Lore (fannar22): the townsfolk all remember the hall "always being there".
- v1.83.0 NO STRAW (fannar22 17:39 "I want to remove striking dummy from warrior quest", 17:40 Grudge's warriors neither): the tutorial is talk → done in one step, and Enzo's welcome line starts the ten-goblin hunt (goblinQuest 'hunt'). questNorm maps old 'dummy'/'strike' saves to 'done'. Grudge already skipped it (the orc pawns set tutorial done). The dummy stays as scenery; its strike code is unreachable now (tutorial never reaches 'strike').

- v1.82.0 THE SHAMAN LODGE (fannar22 17:05-17:15: "shaman trainer who is wolfman male name A Shaman Trainer in a building big enough for player to enter", "South side of West Dominos Port", "A Shaman Spells ... sell Minor Shielding and Minor Healing"): PORT.lodge at (-37,-10), 7.2 x 6, door gap on the road (north) side. Engine: lodgeIn / LODGE_RECTS (walls, counter, fire pit, totem, drum) / lodgePush in collide(); the lodge NPCs are heard only from inside and the port NPCs only from outside (npcNear); SHAMAN_SHOP = scroll_heal1 + scroll_shield1, shamans only; TRAINER_OF.shaman = SHAMT (Enzo and Bob redirect shamans). Page: lodgeModel (timber-and-plaster walls with glowing windows, teal shingle roof, SHAMANS paw sign, totem pole, fire pit with flicker, drum, counter, glowing green jar, smoke) + lodgeSync fades the walls like the shack. Rigs rigShamT (grey wolfman with a totem staff) and rigShamS (white-grey, glasses). The roost shop window is shared with the necromancer seller (sh flag). v1.82.1 put the tavern cat back to Sloppy (fannar22 17:29 "I want to keep tavern cat sloppy"; the 17:27 "Moon Light" was not for her). Gotcha: a glow pane behind a timber disc disappears; the cyl is radius 1 so .33 scale = .33 radius, the pane has to sit past t/2+.05. Headless renders need --disable-dev-shm-usage (/dev/shm is 64M; without it: black frames, shader errors).
- v1.81.0 DODGE BY ITSELF (fannar22 16:06-16:08: "Dodge skill should automatically happen instead of pressing a button", "I grew up with dodge just automatically happen"): the passive slip already existed (slip(): .5 % a Dodge point + AGI_SLIP past 50, cap 25 %), but Dodge was not in SKILLUP_FREE, so an untrained hero stayed at 0. Now dodge is free to rise from 0 and hurt() rolls skillUp('dodge') on every blow that lands (next to Defense). EQ-style line: "the wolf tries to hit you, but you dodge!". The Shift/DODGE roll stays as an extra (i-frames). t181: a new warrior reaches ~6 % at level 1 (cap 10 + human agility).
- v1.80.0 CLICK-TO-CON + DUSKHOLLOW + AUTO ON STRIKE: (fannar22 15:26 "when you click on npc or another player I want it to show it consider ring without using consider command. when you use consider command it just bring the message") setTarget marks conSeen for anything with a level (or hostile), so the target ring, the target frame name and the hover tag take the consider colour at once; C / consider only prints the line. (fannar22 16:05 "Duskhallow is also a newbie zone") zoneAt(h,cur) replaces the inline zone picks: Duskhollow is its own zone x 112..150 inside the Dark Forest (DUSKB), with its own banner line. (coldpresss 16:02 "auto attack should be on by default") a strike on a living foe turns auto-attack on (OPTS.autoStrike, default on, options checkbox #optAuto); 1 still turns it off.
- v1.79.0 EAST DOMINOS PORT (fannar22 15:07-15:09: "rename Good Forest be called East Dominos Port", "East Dominos port is a newbie zone"; 16:00: "coins should make noise when dragging from inventory to the bank"): every player-facing 'Good Forest' (zone banner, ZONE_NAMES.good, zoneTick, resume/boot banners) now reads East Dominos Port; the engine zone key stays 'good'. The zone banner gets a line under the name (ZONE_SUB: East Dominos Port = the newbie line, Dark Forest = 'the lanterns stop here'). A fingerpost at PORT.x0+.2, corridorZ+2.7 (north of the road): post + two canvas arrow boards (front faces -z; from the road +x/east is on your LEFT, so the front East board points texture-left; the backs are drawn the other way round). coinClink(n): short sine partials (1250-1670 Hz x1/2.76/5.4), 1/2/4 clinks for 1/10/all, played by bankDo for coin keys (cs*/ct*/cx*) — respects OPTS.sound.
- v1.78.0 THE BANKER'S COUNTER + WALK-AWAY WINDOWS (fannar22 15:17-15:22: "the section of Your bag gone and have 4 coins slot Platinum, Gold, Silver, and Copper with the lay out just like ... the inventory window", "open inventory window and drag what coins or item you want to drag into the vault", "exchange your lower value coins to high value coins", "when you walk away to far from a merchant or class trainer their window automatically close"): the bank window lost its own bag list; opening the vault opens the character/bag window with it (and closes it again on close if it opened it). Wide screens: vault beside the bag (.bankwin.beside, needs inv width + bank width + 100 px; right offset uses the 38rem inv width — besideBook had the same stale 23rem, fixed too); otherwise stacked (.pair: vault top half, bag bottom half; phones: sheets 44vh/50vh). bagInView() scrolls the bag into view. Vault coins = 4 tiles like the purse row (#bankCoins, data-k ct*); purse tiles (#coins, data-k cs*) click-store while banking; both drag (coinHooks, pointer ghost, drop on the other row). Bag items click-store while banking (after tapFirst), drag onto #bankVault (dragEnd). Change row #bankX: copper→silver, silver→gold, gold→platinum from the purse (run.bankChange, 1/10/all). winsAway() in the frame loop closes shop, trainer, roost and bank windows 1 s after you step out of reach (only once you had been near); focus goes back to the canvas.
- v1.77.0 THE PORT WALLS (fannar22 14:59-15:08: "castle wall along North side edge of West Dominos Port, same with the South edge", "until it get to good forest", "wade few paces out into the water", "I don't want moss", "look like town folk do their best to keep it looking clean", no east wall because the forest is to become part of the town): PORT.wall {x1 -63.5 (in the surf), x2 -26 (the forest line), z +-40.7 (centre; inner face 39.95, the hero clamp stays 39.5), t 1.5, h 3.4, towers [x, r, height over the wall]} + solid circles for the three round towers per side (collide). Page: pale limestone courses on both faces over a darker core (the joints), a plinth (wet-grey in the sea), coping lip, outer crenellated parapet, 2.5-pace sections that step up with the ground; towers get a footing, courses, corbel + parapet ring, merlons, arrow slits, a lantern (spark) on the port side and a sea-blue pennant. The port border wood (pbd) is pushed out to |z|>=44.8 along the walls and the Good Forest border trees (bd) skip that strip, so no canopy pokes through the stone.
- v1.76.0 MORE SEA (fannar22 01:28 "expand West Dominos Port ... add more ocean, maybe by 20 paces"; 14:55 "nothing but ocean", so no lighthouse/islet/buoys): PORT.west -72 -> -92; groundY past the pier end drops the seabed another 3.4 (smooth over 16 paces); the seabed painter and the sea sheet shader (uDeep = pier.x2-2, cache key sea2) go deep blue and less see-through past the pier; a swell line when you swim into the west wall.
- v1.75.0 (2026-09-28, fannar22 01:38-01:41): THE EFFECTS WINDOW. #fxWin (a .frame under the hero frame, every class, hidden when nothing is on you) lists run.fxList(): Minor Shielding (S.buffs.shield) and food (S.fed, S.fedId → Fried Fish / Raw Fish, +1 strength) in gold, harmful effects in red, each with an icon (GLYPH fish/poison/curse), what it does, m:ss left and a draining line; keyed DOM update in fxHud() (frame loop, after spellHud). The ale never shows (fannar: you know when you're drunk). The old caster-only #buffs chip is gone. Harmful effects, the first two (my pick, so the red side has something in it): AFFLICT.poison — a goblin scout's landed blow poisons 25 % of the time (MOBS.goblin_s.fx), 1 damage every 2 s for 12 s, it can kill you ('the poison finishes you'); AFFLICT.curse — the Gaunt's landed blow curses 35 % of the time, -8 defence for 30 s (buffDef adds it; can go below 0). S.fx holds them (run only, cleared when you fall); a fresh hit restarts the timer. hurt(dmg,who,T) gets the monster type. NOTE: makeRun's engine already has a global effects(p,cls) (stat effects) — don't name anything else `effects`. Node tests 16/16 + the bank suite unchanged; probe clean at 800x500 and 390x844.
- v1.74.0 (2026-09-28, fannar22 03:48): A BANKER. A booth on the north side of the West Dominos Port road (PORT.bank at x -39, z 6.2; solid desk/strongbox circles): green-and-cream striped awning with a scalloped valance, oak desk with green felt, coin stacks, ledger + inkwell + quill, brass scale, iron strongbox, coin sacks, a gold coin sign and a lantern. A Banker (rigBanker: bowler hat, brass spectacles, bottle-green waistcoat, cream shirt, red necktie, watch chain; animRig st.count slides coins when you're close) stands behind it, VILLAGE.banker, NPC_HP 22, targetable, talk topics fee/change/vault/rob/scale/hat. Engine: bankState() {slots[8],cnt[8],coins} lives on the hero (h.bank, heroNorm → bankNorm drops unknown/class items and empty stacks), S.bank in the run; run.bankStore(i) / bankTake(j) (near the desk, class kit refused, stacks merge, 'full'/'bag'), bankCoins(k,'store'|'take',n|'all') moves one coin kind between purse and vault, bankChange(k,times|'all') trades 10 of a coin for 1 of the next tier up (coinUp; nothing above platinum). The vault stays when you fall (corpseDrop only touches S.inv). Page: #bankWin (click the Banker; opens from anywhere, but only works at the desk; the note tells you where he is), vault tiles + bag tiles (click or drag between them), a Coins section with 1/10/all chips and per-coin store/take/→ next-tier rows; Esc and every other window close it; refreshes on bankSig. Node tests 33/34 (the death-sim test can't force a fall in node; checked by code), browser probe clean at 800x560 and 390x844.
- v1.73.0 (2026-09-28, fannar22 02:28-02:31): FOUR STACKS. The purse is four piles S.coins {p,g,s,c} (hero.coins; hero.wallet kept as their worth). Coins never merge on their own (a Banker will change them later). Gains come in the fewest coins (coinsAdd = greedy split). Paying: coinsPay picks the coins that cover the price with the least overpay (on a tie the most coins, so small change goes first) and adds the change greedily; S.wallet is a getter/setter over S.coins so every `S.wallet<price` / `S.wallet-=price` / `+=` keeps working; the setter leaves S.change and payNote() adds ' · paid 1g, 7s change' to the purchase line (Chomp's ale: in brackets). Purse text everywhere is pileText(S.coins) ('12s 57c'). Character window: a coin row (#coins, coinUI) under the equipment grid, labelled like the slots, with CSS coin discs. heroNorm migrates old wallets (greedy split) and pays the v1.72 refund as coins. Also fixed: v4 roster conversion compares skillNorm(old.skills) with the slimmer skillState.
- v1.72.0 (2026-09-28, fannar22 02:53-02:54 "I want all of them gone"): NO TRICKS. The Stun Punch (Enzo/Grudge/the Necromancer Trainer) and the Pinning Arrow (Bob) are gone: no Ability list in the trainer window, no offers after the goblin/orc/archery quests, no every-third-hit stagger/pin, no hidden village learn buttons, run.train/trainPin removed. skillState is {dual} only (Dual Wield is a skill and stays). heroNorm refunds TRAIN.price/PIN.price (30c each) for an old save that had them and sets h.refund; start() says 'the trainers count N back into your hand' once. The trainers' talk lines now say 'no tricks'.
- v1.71.0 (2026-09-28, fannar22): THE HUNT. Zones by x: zoneOf(x) = 'port' west of PORT.x0 (-26), 'dark' east of K.DARK_X (100), 'good' between (ZONE_NAMES). A monster that has fought you (you/your pet hit it, or it got within striking reach while aware) sets m.chase: no leash any more (movement ignores home<T.leash), it stays aware until you stand in a different zone from its home (then "the goblin gives up the chase at the edge of the Good Forest", once per 6 s, and it walks home via calm()). m.chase clears whenever it isn't aware. Gated by huntOn = opts.hunt||opts.guards (the page passes guards:true; old suites unchanged). TRAINERS DEFEND (fannar22 02:38): trainerTick (with guardsOn) gives Enzo, Bob, Grudge and A Necromancer Trainer a TDEF strike: the nearest AWARE monster within 4.2 of them whose home is 7.5+ away (so Duskhollow's own orcs/skeletons, homes 3.2-7 from its trainers, are neighbours, never struck) takes 30 every 1.3 s; kills give you no xp ("Enzo cuts down the goblin", shouts like Enzo: "not in my village!" once per 20 s). Node tests 11/11 (chase 24 paces from home, gives up in the port zone, walks home, Enzo kills an intruder wolf, calm wolf left alone, resident skeleton near the Necromancer Trainer untouched, no chase without opts); browser probe clean.
- v1.70.0 (2026-09-28, fannar22): HEAVY IRON. The HEAVY SWORD (sword_h: 12 damage, delay 38, 1 platinum at Doug, Primary only, two:1): it takes both hands, so wearing it sends the shield/second blade to the bag (invEquip refuses if the bag is full; twoFix() also runs in invNorm and corpseLoot), a shield can't go in Secondary beside it (wieldOk(id,sl,sk,inv), page twoMsg()), shieldOn() is false with it, buying one with a shield on puts it in the bag. Every landed Heavy Sword blow bumps S.thud and the page plays a low THUD (thudSync next to bellSync; sine 130→40 Hz + muffled noise). 3D: P.hsword (rigSword 'hsword' len 1.12, scaled 1.35 wide) in the right hand. WEAPON SKILLS: h1 '1H Slashing' (Warriors + Rangers, Enzo/Bob teach), h2 '2H Slashing' (Warriors, Enzo), pierce '1H Piercing' (Necromancers, the Necromancer Trainer); SKILLS[k].cls may be a list now: skOk(k,cls) gates trainSkill, the trainer list (sk.only = lock text) and the skills summary. Items carry wk (Training/Short/Iron/Ranger's swords h1, Training Dagger pierce, Heavy Sword h2; the Training Club none). Each skill: +1 damage every 5 points with that weapon kind (strikeOf wsk), rises from 0 on landed blows (SKILLUP_FREE). HIT CAP: a hero's blow/arrow lands for at most hitCap(level) = 14 through level 15, +3 a level after (level 20: 29); melee() wraps melee0() (r.raw/r.cap/r.wk/r.heavy), the second-blade join is capped too, bowOf caps arrows; pets and spells are uncapped. Texts show the capped strike (effectText/skillText/itemLines + 'this blade would hit N'). Doug talks up the Heavy Sword. SKILL_KEYS order: offense, h1, h2, pierce, defense, ... (old saves get 0s). Node tests 22/22 (equip rules, full bag, invNorm fix, cap 14/29, thud per hit, 2HS rises, class gating); browser probe: wear swap message, shield refused, trainer list shows 1H/2H Slashing, 3 heavy hits = 42 damage, no errors.
- v1.69.0 (2026-09-28, fannar22): WILD THINGS. Four GOBLIN SCOUTS (goblin_s: level 4, 35 hp, 10 attack, aware 6.5, role 'scout' ranging rad 7) roam the Good Forest off the road at SCOUT_XS, with a moss-green hood + crow feather (goblinScoutModel = goblinModel x1.08 + rigProp 'scouthood'); spotting you prints "a goblin scout spots you, whistles, and draws a jagged blade!" (once per 15 s, S.scoutT). Six FOREST BATS (fbat: level 1, 10 hp, 5 attack, PASSIVE: MOBS passive:1 skips the awareness check, so they only fight once hit; leash aware*4) flit under the trees at BAT_XS (role 'flit'), brown fur + amber eyes (batModel(1), body scale .85; batPose now runs for any o.userData.bat, roosting only for cave 'bat'); first hit prints "the bat shrieks and dives at you!". Both spawn LAST with their own dice mulberry32(seed^0xba75) via wild()/wildAt (clear of camps >5 and other mobs), so every older mob keeps its id. DARK ELF mobs removed from the Dark Forest (the DARK_ELVES spawn line is gone; kind stays in MOBS/DROPS unused). WOLVES spread out: pack mates now stand WOLF_GAP 3.4+ apart (Good Forest packs + shadowwolf groups via group(...,gap)), so you can pull one at a time. Drops goblin_s 4-10, fbat 1-3; both count as guard foes. Node world test 40 seeds: 4 scouts + 6 bats every world, 0 dark elves, min wolf gap 3.40.
- v1.68.0 (2026-09-28, fannar22): TOWN LIFE. NAMES: the woman at the well is Lizzie and the child is Bobby (the same two folk, renamed; internal ids stay hilde/pip, FOLK_NAME maps them, FOLK_SAYS lines renamed). Both are target NPCs now: engine NPC_HP Lizzie 24 / Bobby 12, targetInfo/targetList read S.folk (folkTick publishes the visible, not-inside folk every frame; null when far), gold name tags (#ffd98a) over their heads, pickables add them, Tab cycles to them, bold chat speaker. Hail (H, typed "hail", or a double-click) answers in their voice; Bobby: "I want to be an adventurer just like you one day!"; npcReply has a few topics for each. /loc (or /location) in the say box prints "Your location is x -17.2, y 0.0, z 0.6" (1 decimal, y = feet height, sys channel, no -0). TARGET WINDOW: the train/shop/ale button (#tgtAct) is gone, and with it the Options "Mouse" switches (dblShop/dblTrain, dblOk): a double-click / double-tap on an NPC always opens their window (trainers: skills; Mira, Doug, Vesh, Smith: shop; Rooster, NECROS: roost; the class refusals are unchanged). MERCHANT GREETING: opening a keeper's window posts "Welcome to my shop..." in their own voice (SHOP_HI: Mira polite x3, Doug pushy x4, Vesh/Smith/Rooster/NECROS x3 each), naming a random item from that keeper's real stock (shopStock); once per opening, 20 s cooldown per keeper (shopHiAt). Probes on 4 heroes (human warrior + ranger, dark-elf warrior + necromancer, 800x500): every trainer and merchant opens by mouse double-click AND touch double-tap, every greeting names an in-stock item, names/tags/Tab/hail//loc OK, a fight step, fishing still casts, no errors. The 1280x720 + 390x844 shots show the HUD right (Bobby/Lizzie target frame without the button, chat lines) but a black scene: this sandbox's swiftshader loses the WebGL context at those sizes (the live v1.67 build too); the v1.67 fishing shot probe on the new build rendered fine at 900x560.
- 2026-09-27: three.min.js now loads with crossorigin="anonymous" (cdnjs answers ACAO *), so errors thrown inside three.js reach the visitor stats with their real message instead of "Script error. @three.min.js:0" (one visitor hit that 3 times this week while playing 15 min; cause unknown, maybe a lost WebGL context).
- v1.67.0 (2026-09-28, fannar22; published by Fela in def666487): FISHING. Pole in either hand + a Tin of Worms in the bag, out of the water, facing the sea: Z (or click the worms) casts; the hook lands 3.5 paces ahead (fishSpot: must be sea, not a deck). Each cast eats one tin; the float bobs 3-6 s (nibble in the last second), then one roll: 1 % + 1 % per 3 Fishing points (fishChance). New skill 'fish' (rises on its own, SKILLUP_FREE, no trainer); new item Raw Fish (food str+1 60 s, price 4). Moving >0.6, swimming, dying or unequipping the pole reels in (worm lost). 3D: red/white float, line from the pole tip (rod tilts up), ripple ring on miss/catch (gold on a catch).
