- v1.65.0 (2026-09-27, fannar22 15:35–16:13): RISING BONE + A NECROMANCER SPELLS + DARK FOREST SKELETONS. SPELLS.bone1 (10 mana, 3 s, pet:1, reagent 'bone', cls ['necromancer'], glyph 'bone'); cast() refuses non-Necromancers ('necro'), a second pet ('pet' — one at a time), no Bone Chips ('reagent'); spellTick re-checks, then invUse(1) + petRaise(). S.pet (PET consts: lv 1, 20 hp, 2–3 dmg every 1.6 s, reach 1.45, speed 6.4, follows at 1.8, leash 22, warps back past 30, rises 1.2 s, regen 1 hp / 3 s when not fighting — no levelling, fannar22: stronger pets come from spell upgrades later). petTick moves like a guard; mobs fighting it call petHurt; a mob that swings at the hero calls petDefend (skipped while the pet is busy or for 3 s after stop). Orders: pet frame buttons, G / X (ACTS petgo/petstop), '/pet attack|stop' in chat. It crumbles when you fall (hurt()) and is not saved. killMob credits 'X cuts down the …'. A Necromancer Spells (NECROS) at VILLAGE.necros {134.6,8.1} beside Vesh: RIG_LOOKS.necros + rigNecros() table; the scroll window (roostWin) now serves roostWho = Rooster | NECROS; buyScroll(id,who) sells NECRO_SHOP (Scroll of Rising Bone, 2 silver) to Necromancers only. Hail/chat lines, target, name tags, pickables, dblOk. 6 DARK_SKELETONS appended after the Lost Lamp with their own RNG (seed^0x5ce1), role 'haunt' — older mob ids unchanged. Probe: raise, kill a wild skeleton in 6–8 s, defend, /pet stop, crumble on death all pass; 3D loads clean (headless can't render the scene).
- v1.64.0 (2026-09-27, fannar22 15:41–15:43): HAIL. H (rebindable, ACTS 'hail', listed on the controls card) hails your targeted NPC if within HAIL_R 8, else npcNear (3.2); guards answer 'move along, citizen'; nobody near → a hint. npcReply catches / hail / first: trainers ask 'ah, here for <Class> training I presume?' when they train you (Enzo: every class but Rangers; Grudge: Dark Elves; Bob: Rangers), others redirect; Mira, Vesh, Doug and Smith have their own hail lines; anyone else treats hail as hello. Typing 'hail' in chat works too. The Necromancer Trainer (next) gets the same line.
- v1.63.0 (2026-09-27, fannar22 15:15–15:32): THE NECROMANCER. CLASSES.necromancer: Dark Elves only (race:['darkelf']; RACES.darkelf.cls now ['warrior','necromancer'], plural 'Dark Elves'), mana:true, hp 20 at level 1 ('20 starting Health') then the Ranger's table (hpLv [20,35,45,48,60], +10), atk 6, skill 'dodge' (Shift always rolls; a shield is AC only). Starting kit: only the Training Dagger (dagger_n, 3 dmg, delay 24, cls necromancer, dagger:1, weapon slot only). classWeapon(cls) replaces the club/sword ternaries in invState/invNorm/corpseKeep. No spells written in (classSpells gives nothing) — they come from 'A Necromancer Spells' (next step, with 'A Necromancer Trainer'); until then Rooster sells them the common scrolls. TRAINER_OF.necromancer='Enzo', so Grudge/Enzo teach their skills for now; no Dual Wield (itemLines says '<Class>s never dual wield' for any class without DUAL.lv). Grudge's orc quest is their first quest (the darkelf non-ranger path). 'no shield: Shift rolls' notes now key on skill==='block' instead of cls!=='ranger'. 3D: rigTrainDagger (bone grip, violet pommel bead) as P.dagger in the right hand. Next: the trainer + spell merchant in Duskhollow, Rising Bone (fannar22 15:35: 1 Bone Chips summons a level-1 skeleton pet, attack/stop commands, defends you; sold for 2s by A Necromancer Spells), skeletons in the Dark Forest (15:36).
- v1.62.0 (2026-09-27, fannar22 15:19–15:27): ARMOR CLASS + MIRA'S LEATHER SET. AC (fannar22: 'make Defence into A.C', 'if Armor is 6 then it adds 6 to A.C'): hurt() uses dv = S.def + defOf (shields) + armorOf (all worn armor) + buffDef, and armor NO LONGER shaves a flat amount off each blow (with 16 slots a flat cut would make you immune: 18+ armor vs goblin bites of 2–4). Balance shift for old heroes: armor used to be strong per point, now each point is DEF_MISS .25 % miss + a DEF_K share. acUI() (split out of sheet()) is also called from invUI, so the stats line 'AC 61 (40 Human + 21 armor) · foes miss you …' follows your gear live (sheet() never ran on gear changes before, so the shield's defence line was stale too). Items (all Mira, fannar22's numbers): Leather Belt waist 3 · 30c, Leather Bracer wrist 2 · 30c (fits wrist1/wrist2, pair:1: buy() and roomFor() put a second one on the free wrist), Leather Boots 3 · 28c, Leather Shoulders 2 · 27c, Enchanted Ear Muffs of Warmth head 1 · 77c (pushedbutton's joke, fannar22 put it on the head). Leather Breastplate 2→5 (fannar22), so I raised Studded 3→7 and Chain Shirt 5→10 to keep the pricier chests better (my call — ask fannar22 if he wants other numbers). Enzo's/Grudge's reward lines say armor 5. Mira answers about muffs and the leather pieces. Item info: 'armor N — adds N to your AC', shields 'AC +N'.
- v1.61.0 (2026-09-27, fannar22 15:05–15:17): THE PAPER DOLL. Sixteen gear slots: EQUIP_SLOTS gains ear1 · head · neck · ear2 · wrist1 · gloves · shoulder · wrist2 · boots · waist (one gloves slot: fannar22 listed gloves twice in his rows, the second became waist, he confirmed 'only 1 gloves slot'). EQUIP_GRID is the display order (ears/head/neck, wrists/gloves/shoulders, boots/waist/chest/legs, primary/secondary/range/ammo). invState builds equip from EQUIP_SLOTS; saves stay inv v:3 (invNorm walks EQUIP_SLOTS, so an old save's new slots come back null — probe: old equip normalises to 16 keys, chest/legs kept). Character window: .cwrap two columns when #inv is ≥30rem wide (container query; #inv is 38rem on wide screens) — equipment grid + the one-line #eqNote (unarmed / Shift rolls / bow stats / no arrows, what the old wide slots said in small print) + #itemInfo on the left, Stats heading + sheet + Bag on the right; phones stack them. Each cell: tiny label above, the item name inside, an empty cell shows a faint outline of what goes there (CSS mask from an inline SVG per slot, --ic on [data-slot]). Armor total moved to the Equipment heading (#eqArmor). No items for the new slots yet (next).
- v1.60.0 (2026-09-26): WET. dripTick (from splashTick): leaving the sea (S.swim true→false on the ground) sets wetT=3.2 s; an 18-drop Points pool drips from 0.45–1.35 up around you, faster when freshly wet, each drop falls with g=9 until it reaches standY. Wolfmen also get shkT: .55 s after climbing out (so the lean is upright) a .95 s dog-shake: H.rotation.y += sin(40t)·.45·env plus a small roll on rotation.z (reset to 0 otherwise), and splashFx(true) throws a sideways spray at chest height. __F3.drip = {p, wet, shk}; dripprobe.js (#x|race|mid for a mid-shake shot).
- v1.59.0 (2026-09-26): SWIM WAKE. wakeTick(dt) (called from splashTick): a pool of 12 flat rings (RingGeometry .87–1, basic material, depthWrite off) at PORT.sea+.02. While swimming (S.swim && !air) a ring every .26 s when moving (grows .35→1.9 over 1.5 s, opacity .46), one every 1.1 s while treading water (.3→1.25 over 1.9 s, .32). Rings stay where they were dropped, so moving leaves a trail. The pool rebuilds if three.world changes. __F3.wake exposes the pool for probes (wakeprobe.js).
- v1.58.1 (2026-09-26): the controls card on phones now lists the jump and auto buttons too (the touch list had missed both), and the first card gets out of the way once you start walking (after it has been up 5 s) instead of sitting for the full 12 s. hintsAt marks an auto-shown card; opened by hand with ? it stays until closed. Phone-width check at 400×800: strike/dodge/auto/jump don't overlap, jump is 46 px.
- v1.58.0 (2026-09-26): THE PORT'S SOUNDSCAPE. ambSync(S) (called from bellSync each frame): looped 3 s brown-ish noise → low-pass (420–1120 Hz swelling) → gain .05·near² (near = 1.1 − (x − shore)/40), wave swell from two slow sines; a gull (gullCry: 1–3 falling triangle chirps) every 18–48 s when near > .2. A 500 ms watchdog hushes the surf when the world stops drawing (menus), and visibilitychange suspends/resumes the AudioContext. Option label: "Sounds (the harbor: bell, surf, gulls)". __F3 exposes AC, AMB, bellRing for probes; run19.sh = run18 + --autoplay-policy=no-user-gesture-required so audio really runs headless.
- v1.57.0 (2026-09-26): FIRST SOUND — the harbor bell clangs audibly. WebAudio synth (bellTone: 392 Hz × partials 1, 2.02, 2.74, 4.1, 5.4, each decaying 2.6→.4 s), volume 1.15 − distance/40 from the bell; AudioContext created/resumed on the first keydown/pointerdown (autoplay rules). OPTS.sound (default on) with a "Sounds (the harbor bell)" switch in the options window. Any future sound should go through bellTone-style helpers + OPTS.sound.
- v1.56.0 (2026-09-26): THE HARBOR BELL (promised on stream). A brass bell (LatheGeometry profile listed bottom→top so faces point out; wBaker + wMat/wInk) on its own arm on the lantern post's water side (local z −.44), three.bell {o,clap,amp}. bellSync(S): wind sway + amp·sin(6.2t), amp decays e^−.9t; the clapper lags and knocks the rim. bellWatch(S) (called from splashTick): a dive landing within 30 of the lantern rings it (run._bspl counts splashes) and Smith grumbles (25 s cooldown); v1.56.1 his lines run out of patience dive by dive ("another one." … "I'm charging for the bell.", per three.bell.sn); swimming out past lantern.x−14 rings it once per trip, Smith: "too far! the sea is not a bathtub." No sound in the app yet (a sound option would be the next step).
- v1.55.0 (2026-09-26): SWIMMING LIES FLAT (fannar22: "when water get deep enough ... swim in horizontal with all race ... back into vertical position when on land"). Page only (animCast): hero group rotation order YXZ, rotation.x = HU.lean eased at 5/s toward 1.45 (1.05 for wolfman, head up) while S.swim && moving, .28 treading water when still, 0 on land/airborne; lifted (PORT.swim-.1)·lean share and shifted back .55 so the body lies at the surface. Strokes by race in rigPoseHum: crawl (human), breaststroke (darkelf), fast doggy paddle (wolfman); tread = sculling arms. The walk gait becomes the flutter kick. The own name tag drops for a flat swimmer (namesTick tp). Probe: jumpprobe.js #auto|swim|<race>[|shore].
- v1.54.0 (2026-09-26): SPACE = JUMP (fannar22: "change space bar into jump action, since 1 key is our attack key"). Strike moves to R (ACTS strike default KeyR, new ACTS jump Space; the keydown handler: KeyR → want.attack, Space → want.jump; Enzo's tutorial lines say R). Engine JUMP {v:5.4,g:15,over:.45,snap:2}: h.y is your feet, h.air/h.vy; jump only when grounded, not swimming, alive (no double jump); walking off anything more than .3 above the ground falls instead of snapping (the pier end, the deck); a move of more than 2 in one step (teleport) snaps h.y down. Airborne above deck+.45 the Enterprise's hull clamp is skipped, so a jump at her rail clears it and you land in the sea (S.splash++, S.landV). S.swim is false while airborne. Page: hero stands at h.y, camera rides half of it, rig pose 'air' (tucked arms, legs still), white spray on a splash (splashFx/splashTick), a round jump button on phones (tJump). Headless note: the rAF loop doesn't tick in the probe browser; use F.freeze(true) + F.step(1/30).
- v1.53.0 (2026-09-26): SMITH'S SHACK (fannar22). PORT.shack {x:-48.2,z:-6.9,w:5.6,d:4.6,y:.62} on the grass line just before the sand, door (local x .3..1.7) on the pier side. Engine: shackIn, SHACK_RECTS (4 walls, front split around the door, L counter so you can't walk into his corner), shackPush in collide, onDeck returns the floor height inside. Smith (grumpy fisherman) sells only SMITH_SHOP: Fried Fish 1s (food: +1 STR for 120 s, NO health — fannar22), Fishing Pole 5c (fits weapon OR secondary, pole:true, never a blade — bladeFits), Tin of Worms 5c (bait, stacks of 20). He buys nothing (keeperRefuses). Trading only from inside the shack. run.eat(i): FOOD_WAIT 4 s between bites, eating again restarts the 2 minutes. Page: shackModel (walls/roof/beams/inside groups that fade like the cave roof: roof + beams + nets vanish when you're inside, camera-side walls fade to .12; .3 when the shack hides you from outside; fade is frame-rate independent), chalkboard of prices, painting of the Enterprise, stove frying with a smoking pipe, his one chair, a fish barrel, nets with cork floats. RIG_LOOKS.smith ('knit' cap style), holds a frying pan; the hero shows a fishing pole in either hand. Fishing itself comes later.
- v1.52.0 (2026-09-26): SWIMMING SKILL (fannar22: "swimming part of player skill, skills up on its own without a point; Human, Dark Elf and Wolfman can swim"). SKILLS.swim {rise:true}: in SKILL_KEYS and SKILLUP_FREE, no trainer (skillUI lists only by/also skills, so it never shows there), old saves get 0 via progNorm. While S.swim && moving, a skill-up roll every SWIM_UP=2.5 s (usual INT×.002 chance, cap by level). swimK(p) = .5 + .3·min(1, swim/50): half walking speed at 0, 80 % at 50. skillText: "swims at N % of walking speed". All three races swim (no race gate).
- v1.51.0 (2026-09-26): THE ENTERPRISE + SWIMMING (fannar22: "a boat and a ramp", "let players swim in the ocean", named her Enterprise). Engine: PORT.boat {name,x:-64.6,z:3.5,len:6.6,beam:2.5,y:.72,deck:2.4} along the pier's north side, bow west; boatHB(dx) = half-beam (pointed bow, fuller stern); PORT.ramp gangplank x -64.2, z .8→2.72, y .3→.72. onDeck = pier ∪ ramp ∪ deck (|dx|≤deck, |dz|≤hb-.3). No more wading wall: deeper than PORT.swim (1.25) you float (standY = sea-swim), S.swim, half speed. collide: in the hull band (|dz|<hb+.3) off-deck you go to whichever is nearer — the deck (bulwark keeps you aboard) or outside the hull (solid from the water). You can climb onto the pier/ramp from the water anywhere. Page: hull = custom BufferGeometry (18 stations × 7-point U profile + transom fan), planks, bulwarks + teal rail, stem, bowsprit, mast, yard, patched square sail, stays + shrouds, pennant, deck barrel, two mooring lines to pier posts (-65.55, -60.95), cleated gangplank; ENTERPRISE painted on the transom (CanvasTexture plane 'boatName'). Hero bobs while swimming.
- v1.50.0 (2026-09-26): WEST DOMINOS PORT (fannar22: a zone to the west, a quarter ocean, the rest flat land for a city). Engine: PORT {x0:-26, west:-72, shore:-58, sea:-.35, wade:1.2, pier, stock}; portH() flattens the land west of x0 into a plain, drops it to a beach and a seabed past the shore; height() ends with portH. onDeck()/W.standY give the pier deck height (y .3, x -54…-68, |z|≤.9). collide: wade to shore-1.2, or walk the pier (can't step off its sides); crate stack + barrels (PORT.stock) are solid; x clamps at west+.5. K.X0 and W.bounds unchanged, so trees, mobs and every rnd sequence east of the old edge are identical (port.js compares against the previous engine). Page: worldPaint adds 3 tile columns west of the old edge + meadow/sand/wet-sand/seabed colours; the skirt spans the port and slopes into a seabed; the old west tree wall is skipped (same dice) and the port gets its own north/south border wood (wRnd 40961). worldPort(): sea plane (toon, shimmer + foam line in the shader, name 'sea'), plank pier on posts, harbor lantern at the far end (glow + halo; nobody admits to lighting it), crates and barrels at the pier foot, meadow tufts (wRnd 51407). Hero, camera, eye and levelFx use W.standY; zone 'West Dominos Port' west of x0 (with hysteresis) and on resume. Next: the boat + a gangplank ramp.
- v1.49.0 (2026-09-26): FOUR BOTTLES (fannar22: "drink up to 4 bottles … 1 minute per drink, up to 4 minutes"). Each bottle adds ALE.dur (60 s) to S.drunk, capped at ALE.max (4) bottles; a fifth gets why:'cut' ("four's the limit"). The stats don't stack (px() adds ALE.fx once). The wait between bottles is 3 s. S.tipsy = S.drunk, so the camera sway lasts the whole bender: amplitude .035+.015 per bottle, a little faster per bottle, fading over the last 6 s. Tests: ale.js covers the stacking.
- v1.48.0 (2026-09-26): LIQUID COURAGE (fannar22: +Strength, −Int/Agi/Wis/Sta/Dex for a minute; "make ale for 1 silver"; "I was expecting a bottle"). One drink only, 1 silver (ALE.price 10). For 60 s (S.drunk): +10 STR, −2 INT/AGI/WIS/STA/DEX. The engine's px() returns P with ALE.fx added while drunk and feeds effects() (retrain, skillUp), manaMax, strikeWith and melee; retrain runs when it starts and ends, so max health drops ~10 and comes back. Unarmed a level-1 Warrior goes 8→19 a punch; with a blade the STR_MULT curve gives only about +1. Balance is fannar22's call. A second bottle refreshes the minute and doesn't stack. The camera sways the whole minute. Two green bottles with corks sit on Chomp's end of the table, and the texts say bottle.
- v1.47.0 (2026-09-26): CHOMP'S ALE (fannar22 asked for a bartender selling booze for 1 gold; it's 1 copper, a gold is a Knight's purse). Chomp is a target NPC now (VILLAGE.chomp, fixed at his wiping spot tl(0,.98) = -18.14,3.59; NPC_HP 34; TARGET_NPCS; pickables add his folk body while FOLK is visible). Double-click him or press 'ale · 1c' on his target frame: run.buyAle() → +10 health, one mug every 20 s (S.aleT), 25 s of camera sway (S.tipsy; ±.05 rad roll after lookAt, fades in 2 s / out 6 s, off with prefers-reduced-motion). First mug gets a Doug's-smoke + cat line. __F3.setTarget added for probes. Tests: scratch gaunt/ale.js (engine) + aleprobe.js/run16.sh (live CDP).
- v1.46.0 (2026-09-26): SLOPPY THE TAVERN CAT (fannar22: named after me, a she, drinks ale not milk). A ginger cat at the far end of the Sleepy Hen's table from Chomp (X.tavern.table.a = ha+π, cat 1.1 m out, saucer of ale at .84). catModel/catPose in the page script, created lazily in folkTick as FOLK.cat; any old 'tavernCat' is removed first because FOLK gets rebuilt (two cats once, the probe read the stale one). Laps 4.5 s of every 9, sits up and turns her head to you within 3.2 m, tail swishes, 'Sloppy' name tag (NAMES.catE, re-added when FOLK.cat changes). Folk avoid her (OB at tl(0,-1)). TAVERN_SAYS has a line about her. Chomp already stands at tl(0,.98) wiping mugs, the bartender spot. Probe: scratch gaunt/catprobe.js + run15.sh (live CDP).
- v1.45.0 (2026-09-26): THE SOUP POT IS SOLID. W.cave.solid = the 20 wall rocks + the pot (r .62 at mouth-frame local CAVE.pot [2.7,-1.1]); caveRocks() returns it for hero and mob bumping. cave.rocks stays the 20 walls (the wall mesh and tests use it). Walk into it head-on and you stop; at a glancing angle you slide round. Test: scratch gaunt/pot.js.
- v1.44.0 (2026-09-26): DOUG'S HAMMER MARK. The Chain Shirt from under Snagtooth's throne sets quests.chain. Bring it (bag or worn) within trade range of Doug and he recognises his mark: the shirt was Tam's, who paid for a helm and never came back. Doug hands over DOUG_MARK (50c = 5s, +25 xp), once per hero (quests.mark). If you read the note (quests.note) he adds "Wrote that my mark held, did he?". A shirt bought from Doug gets no story. All three flags are saved in quests. Test: scratch gaunt/mark.js.
- v1.43.0 (2026-09-26): THE TORN NOTE. A scrap of parchment with a torn corner and three ink lines lies in the last traveler's reach, left of the throne (CAVE.note, mouth-frame local [-2.75,-3]). Walking within 1.3 m reads it once in chat (a diary line: the goblin naps after soup, Doug's mail turned his knife, "tell Doug his hammer mark held"); it re-arms after you're 4 m away (S.noteNear). Sets up the Doug hammer-mark idea. Test: scratch gaunt/note.js.
- v1.42.0 (2026-09-26): HOVER NAMES (mouse only). Hovering anything you can pick (mobs, the Knight, villagers, guards) shows a small tag at the pointer: name · level. It is in the consider colour once you have considered or fought that foe, otherwise in its reaction colour. The cursor becomes a pointer. Direct ray hits only (pickAt slop 0), throttled to ~9 Hz; the tag hides on leave, while dragging the camera, or with a button held.
- v1.41.0 (2026-09-26): SNAGTOOTH'S ROAR WAKES THE BATS. When he wakes, every living bat within 10 m of him (and within 10.5 m of you, so they keep you in mind) wakes too, with its own line ("His roar shakes the cave roof…"). Bats still wake on their own at 5.5 m. Test: scratch gaunt/roar.js.
- v1.40.1 (2026-09-26): starting a new adventure (after you fall) switches auto-attack off quietly, so the old "auto-attack off" line no longer pops up after a restart.
- v1.40.0 (2026-09-26): CAVE BATS (jjj_nnn_hh's idea; the voice's story: they tolerate Snagtooth for his scraps, hate the wolves) — MOBS.bat 'cave bat' lv4 12hp dmg 2–4 hit .8, DROPS 1–5c; three spawn after the goblin guards (so older mob ids stay put; tests t.js/cave.js updated) at the back of the cave. Engine: waking sets circT 1.5 s — the bat circles you sideways and its attack timer is frozen until then (the chase code clamps atkT to period while closing in, so a longer atkT alone never worked); one wake line per 20 s. Page: batModel() is a plain Group (no rig — animRig returns early), batPose() hangs it upside down under the roof (+2.55) while roosting, flaps at chest height with a flutter when awake, dips when it bites, glows when hit; ring .8. Also: touch AUTO button (#tAuto, left of strike) — the same toggle as the 1 key, lit (aria-pressed) while auto-attack runs, synced in hud(); the "auto-attack on" toast names your bound key. Dark Forest directions for players: east of the village (x>100), past the Cursed Knight's clearing (HOME x92); Duskhollow ~x131–137.
- v1.39.0 (2026-09-26, for fannar22): NAMES OVER HEADS — canvas-text sprites (group 'nameTags') over the villagers (gold), guards (steel) and your hero (pale green), placed each frame from a once-measured head height, hidden past ~26 m (fade from 20), grown up to 2.1× with camera distance; your own hides when the camera is within 2.2 m. Options → Names: villagers & guards / your own name (fr3d-opts names, myName). TRADE RANGE: VILLAGE.tradeR 4 for every near*() and shopKeep (quest triggers still use talkR 1.6). CLASS TRAINERS: trainNo(who) — a trainer of the wrong class (or Grudge for a non-dark-elf) opens no window, says "I only train … Go see …" in chat, and the "other trainer" button hides. AUTO-CONSIDER: conSeen{} — foes that hit you or that you swing at keep their consider colour on the ring and the target name (computed live, so it changes as you level); conKey/conHex are gone.
- v1.38.0 (2026-09-26, for fannar22): OPTIONS window behind a gear button on the rail. Keys: every action can be rebound (click, press a key; a taken key swaps; Esc cancels; saved in localStorage fr3d-keys) — done by a capture-phase remapper that re-sends each key event under its default code (KM map), so the input code is untouched; arrows + T stay fixed extras; the help card's <kbd data-act> labels follow your keys. Mouse: double-click opening a trader's stall / a trainer's yard can each be switched off (fr3d-opts, dblOk() in clickPick). Windows: the options window, the TARGET frame (drag its name: it's reparented from the HUD column into the stage as .floating; a click without a drag docks it back; double-click docks) and the SPELL BAR (drag the dotted strip above the slots, a ::before so sbar.children stay the slots) all move; "put windows back" resets every position. The village list in the character window is hidden (you meet people by walking up; the buttons stay wired underneath). Probe: 37 checks.
- v1.37.0 AGILITY + LEVEL CAP 20 (fannar22). AGILITY is the 6th stat (STAT_KEYS gains 'agi'; bases human 55, dark elf 60, wolfman 60; old saves get the base via progNorm). Past 50 each point = +1 % walking speed (effects().moveK → HERO_SPEED × moveK) and +.2 % to slip a blow (added to dodgeChance, 25 % cap). Sheet row sAgi/eAgi; the race picker lists Agility. MAX_LEVEL 20: gainXp stops levelling at 20, a hero already past 20 keeps their level, xp still counts; the xp bar shows full and "N xp · max level".
- v1.36.0 KEYS, CONSIDER, AUTO-ATTACK (all fannar22, 04:53–05:07). C = CONSIDER: E.conOf(heroLv, lv, info) → same level white, 1–3 under blue, further under green, 1–4 over yellow, 5+ over red; own lines for Snagtooth ("eat your soup, then your bones"), the trainers ("you would lose, and he would still offer you tea"), the Knight (no level: red, "the curse hides his strength"). The line lands in chat in its colour and the target ring takes that colour until the target changes (conKey). With nothing targeted, C picks the nearest (E.targetNext). BAG = I, or Tab+C (Tab held). TAB = next target, fired on key-up so Tab+C doesn't also cycle; only captured when the game view has focus (windows keep normal Tab; Shift+Tab always leaves — no keyboard trap). 1 = AUTO-ATTACK toggle: sets inp.attack every tick while the target is a living foe; off on 1 again, the foe dying, or the target changing; with no foe it picks the nearest. inp.foe = your mob/knight target → engine target() hits it first when in reach (S.pref). SPELLS are click-only now ("only click on the spells icon instead of a hot key") — the slot key labels are blank. Bob and Enzo are level 20 (NPC_LV, shown in the target frame).
- v1.35.0 AUTO-TARGET (fannar22: "when you get attacked you should automatically target the Monster"). Engine: every mob swing (hit, miss or dodge) sets S.foe={t:'mob',i}, the Knight's sets {t:'knight'} (S.mobs.forEach now passes mi). Page tgtTick() consumes S.foe: it becomes your target unless your current target is a living hostile (a foe you picked stays picked). Snagtooth's wake line says "throne of bones" (was still spoons).
- v1.34.0 BOB ⇄ ENZO (fannar22: "rename Bob as enzo and enzo as bob", "make bob a young person"). Only player-visible names swapped: every capitalised Bob/Enzo in strings, HTML and comments, plus the three chat regexes that listen for the names. The code keeps its identifiers: BOB, bobOffer, nearBob, VILLAGE.trainer, RIG_LOOKS.bob = the SWORD trainer by the well, now called ENZO; ENZO, q.enzo, nearEnzo, VILLAGE.ranger, RIG_LOOKS.enzo = the ARCHER, now called BOB (see the ENZO-NAMES comment above var BOB). Save keys unchanged, so old quests carry over. Young Enzo: RIG_LOOKS.bob w 1.1, short dark hair, no beard, blush.
- v1.33.0 STATS PAST FIFTY + DUSKHOLLOW STONE HOUSES (all fannar22, 04:15–04:45). Only points above STAT_AVG 50 count (past50(v), "let count every points after 50"): STAMINA +3 % of the class starting hp per point (STA_PCT .03 × C.hp, floored, added to maxHp); DEXTERITY +1 % damage and +1 % swing speed per point (DEX_PCT; atkDmg and strikeOf dmg ×dk, atkCd / strike cd ÷dk, min .3 s; strikeOf returns dex: the bonus); WISDOM +0.5 % mana per point (WIS_PCT; manaMax(cls,level,wis) — the 3rd arg is optional so old callers keep the table value; makeRun + retrain pass P.wis). A level-1 Wolfman Shaman (wis 75) has 23 mana, not 20. INTELLIGENCE is learning only now ("make int only learning for the moment"): it no longer shortens swings or adds bow damage — just skillUpChance. Sheet lines spell it out ("max health 40 (+15 from stamina past 50)", "+10 % damage & swing speed", "+12.5 % mana", "skill-ups N % a use"). DUSKHOLLOW: the three elf tents are dark mossy stone houses (same spots): plinth, 2.4×1.5×1.94 walls, quoins, mossy slate prism roof, chimney, door with a violet hanging, violet glowing windows, glow-moss by the door. Vesh's line mentions old stone and moss.
- v1.32.0 SNAGTOOTH'S CAVE + THE DEFENCE CURVE (all fannar22, 03:25–04:07). ENGINE: CAVE={wall 7.2, rock 1.35, mouth .46 rad, clear 10, lane 16, flat 9.5, back 180, warn 15}. makeWorld picks a spot in the eastern Good Forest (x 56–86, 19.5–27.5 m off the road, pathDist ≥19, camps ≥14 away; rng mulberry32(seed^0xc4fe); fallback x70 ±23), flattens the floor (height() → cave.y within 9.5 + 3 m ramp), rings it with 20 collider rocks (first/last are the bigger mouth rocks, m:1), appends Snagtooth + 2 goblin guards AFTER every older mob (older ids and spots unchanged — tested on 200 seeds), THEN clears trees inside + along a mouth lane. collide()/mobSlide() push out of caveRocks(). MOBS.snagtooth lv10 hp140 dmg15 reach1.9 period2 leash5.5 xp1500 back 180 s (per-kind respawn: MOBS[kind].back||RESPAWN); MOBS.goblin_g 'goblin guard' lv5 hp42 dmg7 leash6.5 xp320 ("let them be level 5"). DROPS snagtooth [1000,1000] = exactly 1 platinum; SNAG_CHAIN .25 → a Chain Shirt via invAdd (the only chain piece in the game); a Bone Crown (crown_s, loot, Mira pays 75c) every kill in gear mode. caveTick says 'a cold draft … muttering about soup' once per approach; Snagtooth's wake line differs for Wolfmen ("Chomp's COUSIN"). DEFENCE: DEF_K 200 — a landed hit is round(dmg×200/(200+defence)) then flat armor, min 1 (the .25 %/pt miss chance kept); the sheet shows "hits that land do N %". RACES.wolfman def 60 ("starting with 60 defence"). Wolfman start health stays 25 (fannar: "actually 25 is good").
  PAGE: caveModel (built once in caveSync, rebuilt if W changes) — wBaker walls (2 stacked boulders per collider rock + moss caps), a lintel over the mouth, a rock shelf + stalactites over the back half on its own transparent materials (fades to .22 while the hero or camera is inside), throne of bones (seat, back slab, fan of long bones, big skull, skull armrests, bone litter), soup pot on logs with steam, torches with flicker, a canvas-texture sign "KEEP OWT / soup is MINE — S.", glowing teal/violet mushrooms, a dark floor disc. Human remains per fannar ("human bones rotting", "a severed arm", "I like gore"): a slumped traveler skeleton with a rusty sword, a severed arm with a stump + puddle by the pot, 4 skulls with bones by the walls — storybook-grim. Page code must use E.CAVE (CAVE is engine-scoped; the first probe died on it). snagtoothModel = goblinModel + pose scale 1.55 + stride×1.5 + R.boss (two-handed overhead swing) + bone-spike crown on the head bone + a shovel-sized ladle in handR (cleaver removed). goblinGuardModel = goblinModel ×1.16 + a pot helmet. fannar vetoed the spoon theme ("instead of spoons make it bones") and the bib.
- v1.31.0 THE WOLFMEN + SHAMAN + MINOR FIRE BLAST (all fannar22, 02:50–03:19). RACES.wolfman {name 'Wolfman', plural 'Wolfmen', str 65 · sta 70 · int 60 · wis 75 · dex 60 (fannar's numbers), def 35, cls ['shaman','warrior']} → RACE_KEYS gains 'wolfman'; they start in the village like humans. CLASSES.shaman {mana, race:['wolfman'] (new: a class can name its races; raceOk checks both sides), hp 25, hpLv [25,40,52,60,72], +12/level, atk 7, skill 'block' (Shift blocks), trainer Bob (TRAINER_OF.shaman)}. classSpells(cls): a new Shaman has SHAMAN_KIT ['heal1','shield1','fire1'] already written in the book AND memorized on bar slots 1–3 at level 1 ("Minor Healing and Minor Shielding at level 1", "spell book and spell slot just like ranger"). SPELLS.fire1 Minor Fire Blast {mana 8, cast 2, dmg 7, range 14, foe:true, lv:{ranger:5}} — spells now use `foe:true` (not `.snare`) for "needs a targeted mob"; SP.dmg lands on the mob (killMob(…,'burn') at 0) and sets m.burnT .7 (decays in the mob tick) → page fireMesh(): additive cones, orange by day, moon-blue at night (cosmetic only, my addition). SPELL_SHOP gains scroll_fire1 (40c = 4 silver). SPELLS[id].lv[cls] = the level a class may buy/scribe it: Rangers from 5 (run.spellLv; buyScroll/scribe → why 'level'); Rooster's row says 'you can learn it at level 5' (roostSig includes P.level). ITEMS.club_s Training Club {slot weapon ONLY (fannar flipped from both hands to Primary only), dmg 4, delay 24, club:1} is the Shaman's class weapon (invState/invNorm/corpseKeep); page rigTrainClub() (ash haft, red grip, bone beads) in handR, P.club shown instead of P.sword when the weapon item has .club. Shamans NEVER learn Dual Wield (DUAL.lv has no shaman → learnDual why 'class'; Bob's window says so). Looks: RIG_LOOKS.wolfman/wolfmanF = Chomp's manokit anthro in grey fur + L.snout (a longer muzzle and a second nose, new in anthroPieces), teal tunic, red scarf, bone cuffs; female has a braid + lashes. Picker: third class pill; note 'Shamans are Wolfmen only' / 'Wolfmen are Shamans and Warriors only' (RACES.plural). Cast bar names a mob target (it printed the mob index before — Ensnare had the same bug). Tests: scratch gaunt/wolf.js (23), club.js (13), wprobe.js page probe (19).
- v1.30.0 THE LOST LAMP (the voice's idea: "the east is all threat and no treasure"; named Lost not Last because the v1.17 west lamp is "the last lamp post"). An old iron lamp out by the east wall among the snatchers (W.lamp: fixed spots near x≈152 + grid fallback, clear of trees 1.5 m / mobs 2.2 m — 200/200 worlds). Stand within LOST_LAMP.r=1.8 m for kindle=5 s (leaving resets: "the wick goes cold again") → +400 xp, +60c (6s), once per hero (quests.lamp). Lit, it WARDS: lurkers + the Gaunt are pushed out to 5.5 m every tick and de-aggro when it catches (a hero standing at the lamp is safe; step out and they come). Falsity: by day hints "There is an old lamp out by the wall…" (x>115, unlit); at night after lighting: "Why did you light the lamp? Nobody asked you to light the lamp. :)". Page: lostLampModel (crooked rusted post, arm, hanging lantern whose glass goes dark → warm; additive glow + a warm ring/pool on the ground at the ward radius, no PointLight), lampSync after falsitySync. GOTCHA hit here: LOST_LAMP was first declared inside makeRun while the api object (top level) referenced it → ReferenceError at load, the WHOLE game failed to start for a few minutes. Engine constants that go into `api` must live at top level; re-extract engine.js after touching the api line, and run the page probe before calling it done. Tests: scratch gaunt/lamp.js (engine 11 ok) + lprobe.js page probe (lit in page, model in scene, lines in chat, no errors).
- v1.29.0 FALSITY (KaiTheTiger's friend: "add verity" → "don't name it verity, name it falsity and make it blue"; Verity = the 2026 Minecraft ARG's yellow smiley AI helper — we made our own take instead of borrowing it, same call as Ticci Toby). A small glowing blue orb with a smile that meets you the first time you walk past x>6 (FALSITY.meetX) — "a little blue light bobs up out of the grass…" + 'Falsity: "Oh good. You are finally here. I have been waiting so long. I will help you!"' — and floats behind your right shoulder from then on (exp follow rate 3; snaps back if >12 m away: "it is simply there again"). Met once per hero: quests.falsity (questState/questNorm), a returning hero has it at once with no hello. Talks every 40–65 s (own rng orr = seed^0xfa15): DAY lines are true tips (dodge the wind-up, Doug buys loot, shadow wolves growl, rest heals, guards defend, snatchers-faster-than-you + Rooster's roots for rangers, low-hp advice); NIGHT (dayState elevation < -.15) lines are LIES/creepy ("The Gaunt loves hugs", "The village moved. It is east now", "Snatchers are slow…"); dusk/dawn transition lines ("smile gets a little wider" / "Morning! Did you sleep? I did not."). chatSpeaker includes Falsity → npc channel. Page: falsityModel(T) (toon sphere 0x5ec8ff with emissive, soft glow shell, ink eyes + torus-arc smile), falsitySync each frame (bob, faces the camera, night lerps a wider smile + slit eyes). Tests: scratch gaunt/fal.js (engine 11 ok) + fprobe.js page probe day/night (met, orb in scene, hello in chat, no errors) + renders fal-day/fal-night. IDEAS (voice): Falsity secretly works for the Gaunt; the Gaunt as the village's lost first lamplighter, Falsity as "the last lamp" — a real lamp worth finding out east would give a reason to go there.
- v1.28.0 ENSNARE (3rd Rooster scroll, fannar22's "more Rooster scrolls" + the snatcher chase): SPELLS.snare1 {name 'Ensnare', mana 10, cast 2, snare .5, dur 30, range 18, glyph 'snare'}; ITEMS scroll_snare1 (40c = "4 silver apiece" like the others); SPELL_SHOP now 3. cast(ri,who): for a snare, `who` is the targeted MOB INDEX (page passes tgt.i when tgt.t==='mob'); refuses {why:'foe'} with no living foe, {why:'far'} beyond 18 m (spellMsg strings added). On landing: re-checks the foe (gone/dead/>20 m → fizzles, mana kept), then m.snareT=30, m.aware=true, "roots burst from the ground and grip the X — half speed for 30 s" (recast: "the roots tighten"). snf(m) halves T.speed in both chase lines (hero + guard fight) and the unwedge threshold; snareT ticks in the mob loop ("the roots let go of the X" when near). 3D: rootsMesh(T) (7 tilted bark/moss cones + a bark torus) added lazily to the mob object, visible while snareT>0. Also: kill/strike/shoot/coin-drop lines now use MOBS[kind].name (no more "the darkelf drops…"). Tests: scratch gaunt/snare.js (engine, 14 ok) + sprobe.js in the page (10 ok: Rooster lists 3, glyph on bar, cast → snared, roots mesh, no page errors). Open for fannar: Ensnare numbers (mana 10 / 30 s / half speed / 18 m) and which scrolls come next.
- v1.27.0 THE GAUNT (pushedbutton liked the name the Snatcher beat): the snatchers' elder, one per world at the middle of the east wall (spot list [158.6,±7.5] … then a grid further in, first free of trees/mobs; appended LAST so older mob ids stay put; 200/200 seeds spawn one). kind 'gaunt', name 'Gaunt' (→ "the Gaunt hits you"): lv6, hp70, dmg [7,11], hit .85, speed 5.4 (< hero 6: you CAN outrun it), aware 14, reach 2.4 (long arms), period 1.7, leash 24, xp 520, drop 40–80c. Model = lurkerModel(1): key 'gaunt', darker rags, ember eyes 0xff7a3a, crown of five bone spikes on the hood, long cloth sleeves, scaled 1.35, stride 2; target ring 1.4. Own wake line ("the Gaunt straightens to its full height…", S.gauntT 20 s throttle). Also fixed: hit/dodge/guard messages used the internal kind ("the darkelf hits you", "the lurker…") → now T.name. NEW for all chasing mobs: UNWEDGE — a chaser that moves < 30 % of its speed for 0.5 s detours at ±1.15 rad for 1.1 s (alternating side), so a hero standing behind a tree pair is no longer safe (Gaunt reached a still hero in 37/39 seeds vs 34/39 before; the rest are 4-tree thickets). Engine tests: node on the extracted engine script (scratch gaunt/t.js).
- v1.26.0 THE SNATCHER (KaiTheTiger's run-to-the-edge fix): a new mob that closes the "I can just run to the end and nobody can hit me" exploit. `lurker` kind (display name 'snatcher'): lv4, hp30, dmg6, speed 6.6 (> HERO_SPEED 6 — it OUTRUNS you), aware 11 (senses from far), leash 20 (chases far), xp 220, drop 6–18c. Spawns: 5 strung along the far east wall (x≈155.5–158, z=-30..30 stepped by 15, jittered, off-trees/off-mobs), Math.PI/2 yaw. AI reuses the generic loop; added a one-shot atmospheric wake line the first time one notices you ("something at the edge of the world unfolds and turns toward you…", throttled 10s via S.lurkT, resets when d>aware*1.5). Model `lurkerModel()`: tall gaunt pale humanoid on the skeleton bone plan, stretched + hunched (hunch .5, full 1.7), dark ragged robe + hood cone + hood drape, cold blue eyes (0xbfe6ff), long clawed hands, a cleaver in the right hand; twitchy asymmetric rest pose. Rendered front+side via the __DBG scratch hook — reads clearly as a hooded lurker with a big blade. Boot+pronoun regression probe still PRON OK 23, page errors none. Name chosen by Kai over pushedbutton's "Gaunt"; internal kind key stays 'lurker'.
- v1.25.1 (KaiTheTiger): Kai the tiger NPC now wears little round gold specs (glasses:0xd8c060 on his FOLK_LOOKS; anthroPieces gained an L.glasses block — two TorusGeometry rims + a bridge + temple arms on the head bone). Old Pell got a he/they line for Chomp ("Chomp caught me sneaking a second mug. They don't miss a thing…") — Chomp is he/they, and was already called 'he' as Kai's partner. Rendered the tiger figure via a scratch debug hook to confirm the specs sit on the snout; pronoun probe still 23/23.
- v1.25.0 pronouns (KaiTheTiger): he/him · she/her · they/them on the hero-creation screen, a new `pron` field on the hero, independent of body (sex). Two labelled rows (BODY, PRONOUNS) in step 1. Body pulls pron along until you pick a pronoun yourself (pronTouched); locked at creation like body/race. Shown in the arrival line and the character-select sub. Old saves default pron from their body via pronOf(). Engine: PRONS/PRON_KEYS/pronOf exported on E; heroState 5th param; heroNorm passes h.pron. Probe: 23/23 (engine model, follow, sticky, old-save default, full submit round-trip they/them+male body, edit lock).
- v1.24.0 (2026-09-26): DOUG THE BLACKSMITH (fannar22's backlog). A rigged burly smith (red headband, brown beard, leather apron, hammer) at the west end of the village, in the gap between the tavern and hut0 at (-22.1,.35), facing the square; forge (-23.15,-.25) with a smoking chimney, glowing coal bed + ember halo, anvil on a stump (-21.42,.5) with a glowing billet, quench barrel (-23.0,.75). Idle: three anvil taps + a rest every 3.2 s; greets within 3.2 m by looking up and raising the hammer. DOUG_SHOP: Iron Sword (dmg 6/delay 26, 400c), Iron Shield (def 7, 300c), Chain Shirt (armor 5, 450c), Iron Greaves (armor 3, 250c) — Iron Sword strike 14 vs Short Sword 10. Keepers stay in their lane: Doug/Mira/Vesh don't stock each other's goods (buy() {why:'stock',who} + a refusal line each way); Doug buys gear back at half, refuses bones/pelts/scrolls with pointers (Mira/Vesh). Stall window "Doug's stall" (aria "Doug's stock"), 4 rows, refused rows disabled with a reason, far note "walk up to Doug's forge at the west end". Target→trade opens HIS stall even where his and Mira's reach circles overlap (new shopTk/shopKp: the target owns the window — fixes a bug where targeting Doug inside Mira's reach opened Mira's). Full talk personality + cross-talk (Bob/Mira/Vesh each got a Doug line; help tip / say box / "no one hears" name him). Side: item rows show "· damage N"/"· defence N", Iron Shield tints steel on the hero, no double refusal line. +4 draw calls (Doug body+hammer+outlines, ~2850 tris; forge/anvil/barrel merge into existing batches, 0 extra calls ~1856 tris). Built by a delegated subagent (S/doug/apply_doug.py rebuilds byte-identically; probedoug 31/31; regressions 1230/1210/1200/1190/1180 all pass; engine node test passes). KNOWN: "dark" is a Doug→Vesh keyword so "it's dark" gets the Vesh line; from some angles Mira's stall hides Doug's legs.
- v1.23.0 (2026-09-25): SHADOW PELTS (my pick, pitched on stream by the voice: 'Vesh pays double, Mira won't touch them'). ITEMS.pelt {Shadow Pelt, loot, stack 10, buyer:'Vesh'}, ITEM_VALUE 40; a shadow-wolf kill drops one with PELT_DROP .35 (gearOn, br() RNG, right after the skeleton's bone-chip roll). sellValue(id,n,who) (engine, exported) is now the one price rule: loot with a buyer fetches double there and 0 elsewhere; everything else unchanged. sell(): Mira refuses (why 'buyer', 'it still smells of the dark trees — take it to Vesh in Duskhollow'), Vesh's sale line 'double what anyone else would pay'. Page: shop row 'Vesh only' + disabled at Mira, tooltip 'Vesh pays … · Mira won't touch it', bag hints name Vesh; Mira and Vesh answer 'pelt/fur/hide' in chat. Pelts ride saves and corpses like any bag item. Probe b12/probe1230.js 19/19 (30 forced kills → ~a third drop).
- v1.22.0 (2026-09-25): THE LEVEL CURVE per fannar22 (17:00: "level 2 800, level 3 1600, level 4 3000, level 5 5500, level 6 9000, Level 7 18000"): XP_LEVELS=[800,1600,3000,5500,9000,18000], then +9000 a level. Kill XP scaled ×8 so level 2 still takes about ten fights (goblin/wolf/skeleton/orc 80, shadow wolf 120, dark elf 160, Knight 800). That was MY call (option B) while fannar was away; if they want the true grind (option A, kills stay at 10), rerun scratch big/apply_v1220.py with mode A. Saves: xpv 3; xpv-2 heroes keep the same fraction of their level (XP_OLD2 table).
- v1.21.1 (2026-09-25): a shadow wolf GROWLS before it comes for you: an unaware shadow wolf between aware and 1.9×aware (6.5–12.4 m) says 'a low growl rolls out of the dark trees…' once (m.growl, reset past 3×aware; S.growlT keeps a pack to one line per 8 s).
- v1.21.0 (2026-09-25): CORPSE RUNS (fannar22: "where you die you lose all your items and have to run back for your corpse"). On death (gear mode only) corpseStrip() moves EVERYTHING except the class sword (worn, bag, scrolls, arrows) onto a corpse {x,z,items:[{id,n,sl}]} at the death spot; the class sword goes to the weapon hand. Purse, quests, skills and the spell book stay. H.corpses is saved (saveP via S.invDirty; heroNorm/corpsesNorm clean it, max 3; a 4th death folds the oldest corpse into the newest). Respawn = the usual start (village / Duskhollow) in a NEW forest seed, so makeRun nudges corpses out of trees with collide(). Walking within CORPSE.r 1.8 m loots (edge-triggered: step 2.4 m away to retry): worn things back to their slots (swapping the respawn sword to the bag), the rest to the bag; leftovers stay ("your bag is full: N things still on your corpse"). World: cloak mound + skull + bones + an additive ghost-light pillar (fog:false, visible from afar); HUD: #corpseHint pill under the quest line with distance and an arrow (deg = camYaw − atan2(dx,dz); positive = clockwise = screen right, verified by projection). Probes: art/b12/probe1210.js 27/27, probe1210b (arrow), 1180/1190/1200 green.
- v1.20.1 (2026-09-25): Duskhollow's lanterns and fire glow VIOLET, not pink. Cause: every glow part shares X.warm, whose colour is set each frame to a warm night tint (≈ 1.32, 1.04, .6 at full night — right for village windows). Duskhollow pre-compensates: VW [.42,.38,1.67], firepit [.6,.54,1.67] (vertex colours above 1 are fine). Any future cool-coloured glow part needs the same trick (or its own material).
- v1.20.0 (2026-09-25): MOVABLE WINDOWS per fannar22 ("Chat window to be moveable, same with inventory and spell book and they can show up at the same time on screen"). Wide screens only (min-width 40.01rem; phones keep the docked bottom sheets and one-at-a-time): winDrag(el,handle) on #inv/#shopWin/#bookWin/#roostWin/#skillWin (their h2) and #chat (.chead; buttons/inputs never start a drag, 4 px threshold, only when the element is absolute/fixed). Position = CSS vars --wx/--wy + class .moved (left/top clamped in CSS so a window can't leave the box), saved in localStorage 'fr3d-wins'; double-click the title resets. .wfront (z 5) on the last touched window. The bag and the spell book now stay open together (toggleInv/toggleBook only close each other when !wideWins()); besideBook() puts the book left of the bag (.beside) when neither was moved and the box is wide enough. Shop/skill/Rooster windows still take over. GOTCHA: don't put animation:none on .dragging — removing it replays winIn (translateX 10px) after every drag. probe1200 16/16; probe1150u's old 'C closes the book' check now fails by design.
- v1.19.0 (2026-09-25): FEMALE HEROES per fannar22 ("add female to Human Race same with female dark elf"). Engine: heroState(race,name,cls,sex) → hero.sex 'm'|'f' (old saves and bad values → 'm'), heroNorm passes it through. Creator: #crSex male/female pill toggle under the race (radio name 'sex', locked for existing heroes like race). Page: rigHero(ranger,race,fem) picks RIG_LOOKS warriorF (auburn BRAID + gold ribbon), rangerF (loose copper 'long' hair), darkelfF (tall white PONYTAIL, violet tie); all get lash:1 + lip colour. Braid/ponytail pieces sit on a 16th bone 'tail' (rigHumDefs(w,L.tail) appends it ONLY for looks with L.tail=[y,z], so every other rig keeps 15 bones and its indexes); animRig swings b.tail back with speed (.12+w*.55, stride sway, idle sway, counters head pitch). probe1190 16/16; 1180/1170/1150u pass. Note: a getBoundingClientRect height in the creator reads ×.985 during its entrance animation — measure offsetHeight.
- v1.18.1 (2026-09-25): orc pawns hit 70 % of their swings (fannar22 19:41 "give orc pawns 70 percent chance to hit the player instead"; was 20 %). MOBS.orc.hit .7.
- v1.18.0 (2026-09-25): DUSKHOLLOW per fannar22 (18:42 "Make sure Dark Elf starts in Dark Forest"; 18:45 "shady merchant named Vesh were the town will start at"; 18:47 "warrior trainer for dark elf named grudge"; 18:54 "orc pawn … 10 health … attack between 1 to 4 with 20 percent chance"; 18:55 "first warrior quest to be to kill 10 orcs"). ENGINE: K.TOWN (134, road z 2.43), TOWN_R 7 — ground flattened, no trees/mobs inside; new Dark Elf heroes spawn there facing west (yaw -π/2, camYaw follows hero.yaw in start()); zone picked from hero.x>DARK_X at start + resume. VILLAGE.vesh (131.2, 6.1) and VILLAGE.grudge (136.6, -1.3); NPC_HP Vesh 28 / Grudge 64; TARGET_NPCS + chatSpeaker + npcNear include both. MOBS.orc {name 'orc pawn', lv1, hp10, dmg [1,4], hit .2} (mdmg/hr hit rolls), DROPS orc [1,4], GUARD foes + orc, ORC_CAMPS in the dark forest. orcQuest locked→hunt (stand by Grudge)→return (10 orc pawns, QUEST_ORCS)→done; Grudge trains Dark Elf warriors only (skNo(): humans 'Grudge trains Dark Elves only'; darkelf rangers get Enzo's quest text + chat points them west). Vesh = Mira's stock under his own name: run.shopKeep() picks the nearest keeper, shopWho drives title/notes/failure lines. PAGE: rigVesh (stall, violet drape, the STOLEN ORANGE LANTERN on a pole), rigGrudge (greatsword), orcModel (grey-green, tusks, red eyes, topknot, studded club); Duskhollow IIFE: 3 hide tents, 4 violet lantern posts, Grudge's stone ring + practice post, violet firepit. probe1180 29/29; 1170/1160/1150e/1150u pass.
- v1.17.0 (2026-09-25): THE DARK FOREST per fannar22 (09:57 "second zone to be called Dark Forest"; 10:12 "Dark Elf home"; 11:01 + 18:27 wolves; 16:55 "Good forest have a path leading east to head to Dark forest"; 16:58 "Human and Dark Elf be enemy, so they attack each other on sight"; 17:03 "light pole right before Dark Forest"). ENGINE: K.XE=160 is the new east edge and K.DARK_X=100 the zone line; K.X1 stays 100 so the Good Forest generates exactly as before (trees/mobs identical, checked on seeds 1/7/42/12345). Dark trees use their own stream (seed^0xda4c, 420 trees, flagged t.d), dark mobs their own (seed^0xde1f) and go on the END of S.mobs (page models match by index). New MOBS darkelf (lv3, 24 hp, 5 dmg, aware 7, xp 20, drops 8-16c) in 4 road bands of 2-3, shadowwolf (lv2, 17 hp, 4 dmg, fast 5.2, xp 15, drops 10-14c) in 2 packs. A dark elf ignores a Dark Elf-race hero (aggro line checks S.race); shadow wolves hunt everyone. Hero clamp and W.bounds.x1 now XE. PAGE: wTree tints trees past the line (ink-blue pines, violet broadleaf crowns, pale dead poplars); ground/skirt/grass shade to blue-black; grass and rocks scale with the wider world (GR) so page decorations reshuffle (cosmetic); the last lamp post at x=98.8 (road north side) with a little sign; worldDay darkens fog/hemi/sun/sky by hero x (dk, softened at night via dn so night is not pitch black); zoneTick(S.hero) before worldDay fires "You have entered Dark Forest/Good Forest" when crossing x 100±.4; darkElfModel (hooded, slate skin, violet eyes, violet-hilted blade) and wolfModel(dark) (cache key wolf-shadow, violet eyes). Draw calls deep in the forest ~111. GUARD FIX: GUARD.near=3, guards only take a foe that is aware (hunting someone) or within 3 m of the post: ~15% of worlds spawned a wolf pack resting at the village edge and the guards killed it in the first second (Garrick shouted at 0:00). Probe art/b12/probe1170.js 22/22; 1160 23/23, 1150e 36/36, 1150u 43/43.
- v1.16.0 (2026-09-25): TOWN GUARDS per fannar22 (17:30 "2 guards to protect the town, both of them are level 20 with 400 health … 25 attack each, and they defend town from wolf, goblin, skeleton, and dark elf"; 17:33 "along town edge near the well but each one is on separate side of the path"; 18:22 "guards should just stick in their spawn points and not do patrols"). Engine GUARD {lv 20, hp 400, atk 25, period 1.6, first swing .45 s, reach 1.45, speed 3.8, aggro 5.5 from the post, leash 9, back 60 s, regen 4 hp/s at the post, foes wolf/goblin/skeleton/darkelf}, posts Garrick (-11.9,.15) north / Wynn (-11.9,-3.75) south of the road, facing east (yaw π/2), just east of the lamp posts. makeRun opts {guards:true} (page only, older suites unchanged). guardTick runs right after spread() in step(); hit lands .12 s into the .35 s swing (g.pend) so the blade and the kill line up. Every current foe dies to one blow. A foe within 3 m of the guard that picked it sets m.foe and fights the guard (guardHurt; a fallen guard is back at the post in 60 s). Guard kills: no XP, no loot, no quest credit (S.guardKills counts them). No patrols: they stand at the post and only step out for a foe within 5.5 of it; 4 seeds × 10 idle minutes = 0 kills at the wolf spawns. Targeting: guards ride the 'npc' ref by name (targetInfo lv 20, react friendly, guard:true; targetList adds them), target button says "talk" → their line in chat; Minor Healing on a targeted guard really heals him/her. Voice lore: Garrick = old soldier, lost a boot to a wolf (one odd boot on the rig, boot2), line "Keep your hands off the well."; Wynn = young, cheerful, ginger braid + a pink flower on her helm, line "Lovely evening for a stabbing!". Rig: RIG_LOOKS.garrick/wynn (guard:1 → steel shoulder caps, blue tabard front+back with a gold hem, gold diamond badge; new 'kettle' helm style: dome, wide brim, ridge), rigHalberd along hand +z (shaft, spike, axe blade on -y, back spike, blue tassel). Greeting wave for an armed rig = a raised-halberd salute. Probe probe1160.js 23/23; v1.15 probes 36/36 + 43/43.
- v1.15.0 (2026-09-25): RANGER SPELL KIT per fannar22 (17:07-17:54: mana L1 20 · L2 25 · L3 32 · L4 39 · L5 45 · L6 60 · L7 70; "Once you purchase spell from Rooster the spell go to inventory … drag it to a slot that is open to scribe … about 15 seconds"; "8 slot spell bar … 20 seconds to memorize"; "2 seconds for minor spells"; "moving to break the cast"; Minor Healing +10 hp to you or a selected player, 6 mana; Minor Shielding +10 defence for 1 minute, 8 mana; 4 silver each; Rooster: "feather cloak and Human look" + a red hat feather). ENGINE (eng 36/36): MANA_LV/manaMax (+10 a level past 7, 0 for non-mana classes), MANA_REGEN 1 every 2 s, SPELLS heal1/shield1, SCRIBE_T 15, MEMO_T 20, BOOK_SLOTS 12 (BOOK_PAGE 6), BAR_SLOTS 8; S.spells {book,bar,scr,mem} saved per hero (heroNorm→spellNorm whitelist); buyScroll/scribe/memorize/forget/cast + spellTick (mana is paid when the cast LANDS, so a fizzle keeps it; h.moving breaks it); buffDef() adds to hurt()'s defence; VILLAGE.rooster (-13.9,1.5) between the well and the east lamp post, facing the road. PAGE (ui 43/43): mana row in the hero frame (.herobar.caster), buff chip with a countdown, spell book button (key B) + an 8-slot spell bar under the rail (2 columns below 50rem tall, 8 in a row at the bottom on landscape phones), keys 1-8 cast, right-click / 650 ms hold forgets, cast bar; #bookWin: scrolls in the bag, a parchment page of 6 slots with ‹ › (ink rises from the bottom while scribing, countdown on the page); drag (own pointer drag, .dragghost) OR tap-then-tap (armed state; Esc puts it away); #roostWin shop (Warriors get "Spells aren't for sword-swingers"); one window at a time; a scroll can't be worn. Heal a friend = a targeted villager (tgt.t==='npc'); anything else heals you. ROOSTER rig: RIG_LOOKS.rooster style 'hat' (brim, crown, red band, big red plume) + goatee; L.feathers adds two rings of flattened feather cones over the shoulders and rows down the cape (one baked mesh); rigRooster adds a scribe's desk (scroll rolls, a page, ink pot, red quill).
- v1.14.4 (2026-09-25): SEE-THROUGH CAMERA (pushedbutton 16:09: "occlusion culling on the player camera", "It happens when amongst the trees too"). ROOT CAUSE of the dark purple void: three r128 only uploads the built-in `cameraPosition` uniform for Phong/Toon/Standard/Shader materials (or with an envMap). The ink shells (wInk) are MeshBasic, so their per-object 'crowding the camera' fade measured from the WORLD ORIGIN: the tree/cottage faded, its BackSide shell stayed, and from inside you saw the shell's dark inside. Fix: own WU.uCam (set from three.cam.matrixWorld each frame in the fog/uniform block) replaces cameraPosition in wHook (vertex fade + fragment). Also new: a SCREEN-SPACE see-through circle round the hero (WU.uEyeS = hero chest on screen px, cam distance, radius .95*uPx/dist; ellipse y*.78), applied only to fragments nearer than the hero (fr ramp uEyeS.z-.9..-.4) on every o.fade material, solid and ink cut together; ink additionally dithers away within 1.6-2.6 m of the lens. Cache key wv12. Verified: behind-cottage, hutA, two forest views clean (magenta-ink debug copy first), tavern probe all ok, draw calls 80 at the tavern.
- v1.14.3 (2026-09-25): OLD PELL'S NIGHTCAP. At dusk Pell doesn't go straight home: he walks to the Sleepy Hen (FOLK.pub.a = tl(-1.25,-.25)), slides onto the road-side bench across from Kai (FOLK.pub.s = tl(-.62,-.25), facing tA+π/2, hips .085 over the wood, same sitH/sitA maths as Kai), sits 22–36 s, slides back out and weaves home at 0.8× speed with a yaw wobble and a .07 roll (o.rotation.z). State f.pub: 0 fancies one → 1 walking there → 2 seated (f.seated: no separation, no folkPush) → 3 heading home; a new day resets to 0. If he gets stuck on the way (6 s no progress) he gives up and goes home. New FOLK_SAYS.pellPub lines while he's at the table. Probe probe1143 14/15: the one FAIL ('sits a good while') is a probe bug; F.step already runs folkTick through drawFrame, so the probe's extra folkTick doubled the clock, and 13 s of probe time = ~26 s in game. Walk there ~15 s, home ~16 s, min distance to any cottage centre 2.31, draw calls 88 at the tavern. PROBE NOTE: any probe that calls F.step AND F.folkTick runs folk at 2× speed.
- v1.14.2 (2026-09-25): TALLER COTTAGES, SMALLER FOLK (xyzfela 15:42-15:51: "people are still much too big for that world, like half the size of a house", "the world is too cramped", "make all creatures and hero smaller, that will solve both"). Measured first: doors were 1.08 tall vs people 1.52 (PS .74), roof apex 2.9 = 1.9 people. Cottages grow UP on the same footprint: the hut loop builds through MH = M0·scaleY(HUT_Y=1.45) (plinth, walls, gable, roof boards shear along, beams, door, windows, attic disc, chimney + smoke point, porch lantern + winTag porch, bunting apex anchors); barrel, crate and the tavern's sign/keg/table keep M0 (people-scale props). Footprints, routes, OB and colliders unchanged. PS .74 → .66 (everyone: hero, folk, mobs). Door now 1.57 vs people ~1.35; apex 4.2 ≈ 3 people. Camera left as is (the world reads bigger). Sits follow the scale: Kai's bench sit is computed from PS (hips .53 world = bench .445 + .085; sitA from thigh .41/shin .49 so shins reach the ground); the default sit height is .592/PS so the camp lounger's hips stay where they were on the world-sized log (top .27). ATK_R 2.2 unchanged (a small hero with a long swing; trim if it reads as hitting from afar). probe1141 18/18 (Kai hips +.085 over the bench), probe1131b 16/16, probe1140b 8/8. liveshot.sh moves mobs within 9 of the shot's hero away, so camp shots show no goblins (by design).
- v1.14.1 (2026-09-25): CHOMP AND KAI AT THE SLEEPY HEN (chompthemanokit: "add in my fursona" = CHOMP, a RED MANOKIT, the barkeep; KaiTheTiger: a BLUE ANTHRO TIGER, the regular; they're partners). anthroPieces(L) adds to the human rig when look.anthro is set: fur2 snout + dark nose on the head bone; 'manokit' = two tall flattened cones for ears with cream inner cones, big dark eyes, a tuft, a three-sphere fluffy tail on the hips bone; 'tiger' = round ears, face/cheek/forehead stripes (look.stripe), a drooping tail with two rings and a dark tip. look.towel = bar towel on the shoulder. FOLK_LOOKS.chomp/.kai, FOLK_SAYS.chomp/.kai (partner jokes, Kai's tab). folkMug prop in handR (names chompProp/kaiProp). Placement in folkBuild via tl(u,v) = the tavern table frame (tvn + hut angle tA): CHOMP tl(0,.98) by the keg at the front end, facing the road (yaw tA) — first face you see; KAI on the hut-side bench tl(.62,.15) facing the table (tA-π/2). Plan entries with stay:'wipe'|'sit' → f.stay: folkTick's stay branch comes first (no walking, never goes indoors at night, skipped by the night init, the separation pass and folkPush); Chomp gets an OB .3 so folk and hens go round him, Kai sits inside the table's OB. Poses: st.wipe (mug at the chest, free hand circling), st.sip (hand on the table; every 5-10 s the mug goes to the mouth for 1.6 s, head tips back). The sit legs now read R.sitH (default .8) and R.sitA (default .97); Kai uses .73 / 1.4 so his hips sit ~.08 above the bench top (world .445) instead of hovering. One voice at a time round the table: the tavern line, Chomp and Kai share FOLK.barCd (6 s). The folk count is now 5 (probe1131 expected 3 → probe1131b). 77 draw calls at the tavern. probe1141 18/18, probe1131b 16/16, probe1140b 8/8. Known: probe1131's Pip-wall / hen-scatter checks flake now and then (2 more folk shift the rnd stream; seen once each, both pass on rerun). probe1134's 'empty cottage stays dark' checks are superseded since v1.14.0 (the tavern glows).
- v1.14.0 (2026-09-25): THE SLEEPY HEN TAVERN (chompthemanokit 15:12 "the bar?", KaiTheTiger "i agree with chomp, add a bar"). TAVERN_HUT=1 (the old empty cottage; its front door is jammed against Mira's stall, so the beer garden is on its EAST side, local (-2.45,.5) -> world ~(-18.64,4.43)). tavern(M0,ha) in worldVillage: iron bracket + green/gold sign with a foaming mug on both faces at the front-left corner (faces the road/START), a keg with a tap by the wall, and on its own ground-height matrix TM a trestle table, two benches, three ale mugs and a glowing table lantern (WIN/GL, outside winTag's 2 m so it keeps its glow). X.tavern={x,z,sign,hut}. winTick goal=1 for TAVERN_HUT (glows all night). FOLK.OB gets [table, .9]. folkTick: walking up (<2.6, moving) gives a TAVERN_SAYS line, 90 s cooldown.
