- v1.13.4 (2026-09-25): LIT WINDOWS FOLLOW WHO IS HOME. worldVillage records X.huts[hi]={x,z,porch}; winTag(geo) tags every vertex of the merged 'windows' glow mesh with its cottage (within 2 of the hut centre, porch lantern within .32 excluded -> -1) and copies the base colours; winTick(dt,w) (from worldTick, after the warm colour) eases X.winK[h] at 1.6/s to the share of that home's FOLK people with f.inside (f.hut = cottage nearest f.home, cached), multiplier m=1-(1-k)*.7*ww with ww=(uWarm-.15)/.85, and rewrites the colour attribute only when some m moves >.004. Hut 0 = Hilde, hut 2 = Pell+Pip (half-lit with Pip in), hut 1 has nobody -> dark at night. Porch lanterns, lamp posts, well/stall glows untouched. No new draw calls (85 in the square). window.__F3.WX exported for probes. probe1134 14/14.
- v1.13.3 (2026-09-25): THE VILLAGE GOES TO BED. FOLK.night with hysteresis on E.dayState(S.t).daylight (home below .3, out above .45; set on the first folkTick, so a run loaded at night starts with everyone indoors). At dusk each person walks to f.home (Hilde: west cottage door; Pell AND Pip: the east cottage — Pip is Pell's grandson) and goes inside (f.inside, hidden, no greeting, skipped by the shove pass); at dawn they come out after f.outR seconds (Pip .2–.6, Hilde .8–2.5, Pell 2.5–5) and resume their loop. Hens roost at night (no new walks, no pecking, head tucked) but still scatter. Steering fixes found by the night run: pressed against a blocker (along < its radius + r + .35) folkSteer turns 1.45 rad (slide round it) instead of .95 (stayed pinned); walking home only slips indoors when stuck within 1.5 of the door (Pip vanished 3.7 from it); a scared hen picks the first free direction away from the threat inside the village (0, ±.8, ±1.5, ±2.1) instead of having its flee point clamped back TOWARD the hero (it froze at .6). Tests: probe1133 11/11 (day out, night noticed, all indoors through their own door, hens roost, no talk through a door, frozen stays in, dawn order Pip→Hilde→Pell, back to their day, night load), probe1131 16/16 ×3 (probe now resets S.t mid-run to stay in daylight).
- v1.13.2 (2026-09-25): CAMP LIFE (continues xyzfela's NPC ask). Engine: only two home spots move — the COOK stands at COOK_R 1.0 from the fire (same angle), the IDLE lounger takes the log seat at tent.r+(2.5/3)·360°, LOG_R 1.45, facing the fire; same random draws, so every other mob/tree is identical (eng1130 18/18, eng1120 15/15, eng1110 37/37, 1103 27/27, 1102 39/39, 1101 30/30 and the known-stale 1104 8/12, 1100 48/49, 199 14/15, 198 40/54 — all identical to v1.13.1). Page: rigPoseHum gets st.stir (right arm circles over the pot at 2.4 rad/s with the blade in, left hand on the hip, lean in, head down) and st.sit (R.sitK eases down at 2.5/s and springs up at 8/s: hips to R.hy·.8, thighs −.97, knees +.97 → butt on the log top, arms on the knees). The mob call passes stir/sit only while calm AND within .3 of home. Fix: camp log seats were laid at the FIRE's ground height and sank into / floated over slopes — each log now sits on W.height at its own spot. Tests: probe1132 13/13 via art/live.sh (spots, sit height = log top +.03, knees, the cook's hand .7–.8 from the pot centre circling .08, the cook never sits, on alert the lounger is up within .5 s, 92–141 calls at a camp depending on the seed).
- v1.13.1 (2026-09-25): VILLAGE LIFE (xyzfela 12:10, same ask). Page only, not targetable, no engine change. Three townsfolk and four hens in the square: HILDE (bucket in hand) walks her loop west cottage door → well → Mira's stall; OLD PELL (cane, slow .62) east cottage door → the road → a chat spot by Bob; PIP (small, k .72) chases a hen, rests, picks another; hens Clucky (fastest)/Rusty/Soot/Honey idle, peck, wander to free spots in a 5.6 disc round the square and SCATTER from the hero or Pip within 1.5. Obstacles are circles in FOLK.OB (cottages r1.8 + barrel/crate corner, well, stall, Mira/Bob/Enzo/dummy/target, lantern posts); folkSteer() looks ≤1.6 ahead and turns .95 rad AWAY from the first blocker (the hero counts too), folkPush() resolves overlaps, a shove between people is followed by an obstacle push, stuck > 3 s skips to the next errand after a short pause. A walk-up greeting (FOLK_SAYS, 60 s cooldown, only while you're moving, after 5 s) lands in chat as npc talk. Everything is driven by game dt (frozen = still) and hidden beyond 45 of the square. First cut: steering turned INTO obstacles (sign of the perp test was backwards) and Hilde's first door (middle cottage) sits behind Mira's stall — she now lives in the west cottage. Tests: probe1131 16/16 (2 simulated minutes: nobody inside anything, both errand loops complete, Pip ran 211, hens stay ≤ 6.3 from centre, a hen scatters, folk keep ≥ .7 off the hero, one greeting, frozen, hidden far, 89 calls / 137k tris in the square), real-time run 0 errors, same software fps as v1.13.0. HARNESS: probes with long synchronous step loops HANG under --virtual-time-budget dump-dom (the GPU process spins at 300 %, renderer idle) even though they finish in ~20 s in a real headless browser — art/live.sh runs the page live with --remote-debugging-port and polls the title over /json.
- v1.13.0 (2026-09-25): FOREST FOLK THAT LIVE SOMEWHERE (xyzfela 12:10: "behaviour/movement of npcs can be improved"). Engine only (the rig already reads gait from position deltas). Roles set at world build (scalar fields — makeRun shallow-copies mobs): each camp's crew is a COOK (stays at the pot, small glances), a LOOKOUT/guard (paces to the camp rim at CAMP_CLEAR 3.4, faces outward, walks back) and an IDLE lounger (faces the fire, bigger glances); wolves are PACKS (role lead/pack, pk id) — the leader picks a spot within 3.5 of the den every 2.5–6 s and the pack follows keeping its offsets; skeletons HAUNT (shamble within 2.4). m.hyaw = the spot's facing; m.cx/cz = camp fire / den. Movement: turnTo() eases yaw (aware 14 rad/s, calm per role), walkTo() walks only once roughly facing the way (no moonwalking home after a chase), mobSlide() pushes mobs round tree trunks (TREE_R·s + MOB_R .38), spread() keeps any two mobs ≥ 2·MOB_R apart so a chase ends in a ring, not a pile; spotOk() keeps wander spots off trunks, the pad, the clearing and (for non-goblins) camps. Wander rolls use their OWN stream wr (seed^0x3a1d), so combat/drop/skill-up sequences are unchanged — older suites eng1110/1104/1103/1102/1101/1100/199/198 give identical scores on live and new. Respawned mobs come back at their spot facing hyaw. Tests: eng1130 18/18 (roles in 40 worlds, a simulated minute: wolves roam ≥1.2, packs stay together (mean gap 1.6), lookouts reach the rim, cooks stay put, 94 % of steps face travel, no one inside a trunk, deterministic, crew chase spreads ≥ .76 apart, turn ≤ 14/60 a frame, walk home facing forward, 173 ms per simulated minute), eng1120 15/15, probe1111 9/9, probe1110 19/19, probe1105 10/10 at 1200 and 390. HARNESS: bigshot2.sh adds sim=<s> (steps before the shot) and noaggro=1 (E.MOBS aware 0); sim ≥ 4 at 1200 wide overran the 130 s shot timeout under load.
- v1.12.0 (2026-09-25): THE BIG FOREST + SMALLER FOLK (xyzfela 12:10/12:25: "people and creatures should be smaller", "the world in general can be generated much bigger", "people are almost as big as houses"). Engine: bounds X0 −24 → X1 100, ±ZW 40 (was ±24 square); the village keeps every spot and the path west of x=0 is unchanged to the inch; east of it a long meander (±9) settles into the rune clearing at HOME (92,0) — the walk village→clearing is ~19.5 s (was ~6). makeWorld places six camps FIRST (CAMP_XS 4…75, alternating sides, pushed until pathDist ≥ 5.2, tent 2.1 away from the path side), then ~700 trees on a 4-pace spatial grid (treesNear(x,z,r)) with fbm glades, then mobs: 3 goblins round each fire facing it (18), 3 wolf packs of 3 on the path (9), 7 skeletons in the east. goblinCamps(W) now copies W.camps; collide() only looks at nearby trees and clamps to the new bounds. Page: people and creatures scaled by PS .74 (psFit also scales the walk stride so feet don't skate), terrain/grass/props/forest baked in 16-pace CHUNKS that hide beyond the fog (chunkTick), mobs beyond the fog hidden, border wood up to 620 trees, camera look height lowered with the hero. BUG FIX: loop() clamps dt at 0 — closeMenus() sets last=performance.now() and the next rAF stamp can be earlier, so time ran BACKWARDS for a frame (in headless it built cam.hold up to 1.8 s and the camera stopped following turns; probe1105's D check caught it). Budget: busiest view 107 draw calls / 195k tris (village, camp, mid-path and east all inside the phone budget). Tests: eng1120 15/15 (60 worlds validated, deterministic, ~10 ms a world), probe1111 9/9, probe1110 19/19, probe1105 10/10 at 1200 and 390 (probe1111's camp-count check loosened to ≤6).
- v1.11.1 (2026-09-25): GOBLIN CAMPS WITH CAMPFIRES (xyzfela's idea, fannar22 picked it next). Engine (pure, moves nothing, so older suites play as before): goblinCamps(W) finds up to CAMPS=3 camps where goblin home spots bunch up within CAMP_R 5.5 (densest first), pushed off the path (|z−corridorZ| ≥ CORR+1.2), clear of trees (≥1.3) and goblins (≥1), ≥7 apart, never on the pad or in the clearing; each camp lists its goblin ids and a tent spot (clearest of 8 directions, 2.1 away). campLit(camp,mobs) = any of its goblins alive. Page: worldCamps() bakes stones, an ash disc, fire logs, seat logs, a tripod with the stolen pot of green stew and a hide tent (dark doorway, flap, pole) into the world batch; per camp a 'campfire' group of 3 flame cones, an additive ground glow plane, embers (spark kind 3), a halo and smoke; ONE shared PointLight 'campLight' that follows the nearest camp (off beyond 22). campTick(S) after worldTick: k.lit eases toward campLit, so a cleared camp sinks to embers and relights when its goblins respawn; flames flicker (steady under reduced motion). Budget: a camp view ~88 draw calls, village 54 (was 48), +20k tris. Tests: probe1111 (camps) 9/9, probe1110 19/19 ×2, probe1105 10/10 (104 s vs live 97 s; two earlier 'NO TITLE' timeouts were machine load, not the camps — traced with console markers). Node: 300 seeds avg 2.99 camps, 0 violations.
- v1.11.0 (2026-09-25): THE ART PASS + DARK ELVES. Art per xyzfela ("make clean artistic and original", "world gen can be improved … keep the basic elements"), built by three helpers on seams and merged as patches in order rig → engine → world → ux → page (scratchpad art/*/apply_*.py, exact-string rep() with count asserts). RIG: one skinned toon mesh + ink hull per character (humanoid(look)); figure/goblin/skeleton/wolf models replaced; one per-frame animCast(S,W,h,k); named parts kept. WORLD: painted sky dome (dayState-driven, sun, clouds, stars, cratered moon), vertex-painted toon ground (path, square, tree shade), 3 tree styles at engine positions with sway + dither fade near the camera, timber cottages with flickering windows/smoke, stone well, lantern posts, bunting, knight's clearing with rune stones, glowing mushrooms, rune circle, god-rays; fireflies/pollen in one draw call; ~31 draw calls in the village. UX: dark-glass floating HUD, xp bar, collapsible chat (folded on phones), a controls card that fades (? brings it back), IM Fell English + Alegreya. GAME per fannar22: 'You have entered Good Forest.' (chat + fading zone banner #zone), Dark Elf race (Warriors only: str 45 dex 60 int 70 sta 65 wis 65, Defence 30; Human Defence 40), Defence = miss chance def×0.25 % (own RNG), Wooden Shield +4 Defence, Short Sword (4 dmg, delay 25, primary or secondary, Mira 2g), Bone Chips (25 % off skeletons, stacks of 20, Mira pays 5c each; loot can't be worn). Page: the class picker locks Ranger for Dark Elves (#crClsNote), an existing hero's race is locked, Defence on the sheet (#defSum), loot priced via E.itemValue. Tests: probe1105 10/10 ×2, probe1110 19/19 ×2, eng1110 37/37; eng1100 'Human is the only race' and eng196's shop list fail by design.
- v1.10.5 (xyzfela: "q and e go in opposing ways?"): a screenshot playtest showed the steering was mirrored — D/→ turned LEFT, E stepped LEFT, and the touch stick walked left when pushed right. In three.js a bigger yaw turns you left and (cos yaw, −sin yaw) is the screen's LEFT. Fixed in the engine (yaw −= turn, side uses (−cos, sin)) and camRelative. The game now opens full page by default (localStorage realm3.full remembers the button). probe1105 10/10 ×2; eng1104 and probe1104 fail 4–5 checks by design (they asserted the mirrored directions).
- v1.10.4 (2026-09-25): KEYBOARD STEERING + FULLSCREEN per xyzfela ("controls are a bit confusing … when pressing arrow keys things move too much"; "a button to full screen the main part of the game, now it's in a small box"). CAUSE: keys were camera-relative AND the camera eased behind the hero's yaw, so A/D sent you in circles and S spun the hero round and flipped the camera. NOW (EverQuest-style): W/S ↑/↓ forward / back (back at TANK.back .6), A/D ←/→ turn (TANK.turn 2.4 rad/s, turn in place), Q/E side-step (.85), facing kept. ENGINE: inp.tank + fwd/turn/side (step turns h.yaw, builds dx/dz from it, and skips the face-where-you-walk rule); the touch stick keeps camera-relative dx/dz. PAGE: keyTank(); camRelative() is stick-only; camera follow .18 while steering (camTight) else .06. FULLSCREEN: #fsBtn (top-right of the stage, 44px, SVG corners, aria-pressed) or F; .arena.full = fixed full-window layer (view + chat) + requestFullscreen where the browser has it (fsNative; fullscreenchange drops the class), Esc / F / the button leave; the renderer now resizes on height too. Tests: eng1104 12/12, probe1104 19/19 ×2; eng1103/1102 unchanged, probe1103 11/11, probe1102 12/12. probe199 7/9 on base AND new (old drift).
- v1.10.3 (2026-09-25): THE BALANCE PASS per fannar22 ("level 1 need 100, level 2 need 700, level 3 need 1200, level 4 need 2000, and level 5 need 3000"; "Skeleton, Goblin and Wolf to give out 10 experience points"; "level 4 ranger 48, level 5 Ranger 60 … Warrior level 4 70, level 5 84"; "Skeleton 10 Health, Wolf 12, goblin 13"). READ AS TOTALS: XP_LEVELS [100,700,1200,2000,3000] = the total xp at which level L ends (the sheet's 'next level at N xp'), then +XP_PAST_5 1000 a level (this app's number). xpNext(L)/xpStart(L); gainXp loops on xpNext. MOBS hp 13/12/10, xp 10 each. KNIGHT_XP 10 → 100 (was five goblins, now ten; this app's number). hpLv Warrior [30,45,60,70,84] +15 after, Ranger [20,35,45,48,60] +10 after. SAVES: progState has xpv:2; progNorm converts a sheet without xpv from the OLD scale (XP_PER_POINT 3 a level, kept only for this) — same level, same share of the way to the next (old L5 with 13 xp → 2333), and lifts a new-scale xp below its level start. Tests: eng1103 27/27, probe1103 11/11 ×2, probe1102 12/12 ×2. BY DESIGN (old numbers): eng 111/138 (was 116), eng19 27/28, eng192 32/44, eng193 15/21, eng194 26/32 — all 3-xp levels, 8-hp skeletons, 9-hp wolves, L4 55 health.
- v1.10.2 (2026-09-25, feature 123cd37ec, stamped 'build v1.10.2 · 2026-09-25 · 123cd37ec'): ENZO'S WOLF HUNT per fannar22 ("I want Ranger Trainer to give a quest to kill 10 wolf"; "let reward be a bow"). QUESTS: questState gains wolfQuest 'locked'|'hunt'|'return'|'done' + wolves 0..QUEST_WOLVES (10); questNorm clamps; older saves → locked/0. FLOW (Rangers only, in questTick's Enzo chain): once enzo==='done' and the test reward moment has passed (enzoFx 0), standing by Enzo starts the hunt (ENZO.wolves); his Pinning Arrow offer follows a tick later as before. killMob counts any wolf that falls while hunting ('wolves n/10'); at 10 → 'return' (ENZO.wolvesDone). By Enzo: the Ranger's Short Bow (bow_r — in ITEMS since v1.9.6, never handed out before) goes on if Range is empty, SWAPS in for a plain Bow (old Bow to the bag), else into the bag; no room → the quest waits and ENZO.bowFull is said once a visit (S.enzoFull resets when you walk away). S.enzoFx=1.2 saves + refreshes the bag. REWARD NUMBER (this app's choice, fannar22 may change it): bow_r bonus:1 = +1 damage over Mira's Bow; run.bowOf(id) = class bow (Warrior: WAR_BOW) + item bonus, used by bowNow and the gear window (itemLines adds a '+1 damage over a plain Bow' line). questText: hunt 'n/10', return 'return … for your bow', locked-after-test 'has a hunt for you'. npcReply Enzo: wolf/hunt/quest/reward words after the test answer with the count. hud saves during hunt/return. Tests: eng1102 39/39, probe1102 12/12 (1200 + 390), eng1101 30/30 + probe1101 26/26 unchanged; eng199 14/15, eng198 40/54, eng196 74/76 fail identically on the base (baseline drift from earlier versions).
- v1.10.1 (2026-09-25, feature 4d74d14c8, stamped 'build v1.10.1 · 2026-09-25 · 4d74d14c8'): SKILL-UPS per fannar22 ("I want int to work on this formula Int/0.20 … skill have potential to increase on it own ever 12 percent or depending on player int stats" → yup = Int × 0.2 %; "Offense, Defence, Archary … learnt on it own without going to the trainers"; "For Duel Wield you have to put at least 1 point for it to skill up"; "at least 1 point into Dodge same with Block"; "every skill up in Duel Wield will use second blade every so often" = option A). ENGINE: SKILLUP_PER_INT .002 (60 Int → 12 %), skillUpChance(p), canSkillUp(p,k) (SKILLUP_FREE offense/defense/archery from 0; block/dodge/dual need ≥1; stop at skillCap(level)). run.skillUp(k) rolls a NEW stream sr = mulberry32(seed^0x5e11) (old seeds' drops/dodges/stuns unchanged), bumps P.sk[k], recomputes M (+maxHp for Defense), pushes S.ups {k,v}, says 'You have become better at Offense! (7)'. Hooks: Offense + Dual on a melee blow that lands on a mob/Knight (not the tutorial dummy); Archery on every arrow (butts too); Defense when a blow lands and you live; Block in hurt()'s shield branch; Dodge on iframe slips + Dodge sidesteps (mobs and Knight). DUAL WIELD IS A NUMBER: 'dual' in SKILL_KEYS, SKILLS.dual {learn:true, no by → no standard trainer row}; learnDual sets p.sk.dual ≥1; trainSkill('dual') refuses ('learn') until learned; heroNorm/rosterNorm/makeRun give an old learned hero 1. DUAL_JOIN {base .1, per .01, max .6, dmg 1}: gear-mode melee() returns {two, off, join}; a landing two-blade blow rolls sr() < dualChance(v) → adds the second blade's own strike (full, dmg share 1 — the voice asked fannar22 full vs half, no answer yet; change DUAL_JOIN.dmg) and says 'your second blade joins in (+10)'; S.joins counts. The flat +2 (OFFHAND_DMG) now only applies to the no-gear test path. PAGE: hud syncs S.ups into P.sk → saveP, sheet, skillUI; the Dual Wield row, once learned, shows v/cap with +1/+5 (focus k:dual:1) and 'second blade joins N % of swings · +1 % a point · rises with use'; learnDualUI now run.retrain(P)s; skill rows add 'rises with use' / 'rises with use once trained'; skNote adds 'each use: 12 % to rise (Intelligence 60)'; the Intelligence line adds 'skill-ups 12 %'; Secondary slot/item info say 'joins N %'; skSum hides Dual Wield until learned. Tests: eng1101 30/30, probe1101 26/26 ×3 (1200/390/320), eng1100 49/49, probe1100 58/58; old suites unchanged except by design: eng192 40/44 (six skills now), eng198 40/54 (+2 → join, Int line text), eng199 14/15 (+2 → join), probe199c 20/22 (learn button → +1/+5, 13 → 11 + joins).
- v1.10 (2026-09-25, feature 6302daa39, stamped 'build v1.10 · 2026-09-25 · 6302daa39'): HEROES per fannar22 ("next release to be 1.10"; "a race selection before you choose your class, and … name their character"; "I only want Human as 1 possible race"; "the race, then name and character class"; 16-letter names; "character select section you can either play or make new character with 8 character save slot"; "human starting stats … Strength 50, Dex 55, Int 60, stamina 55, and Wisdom 60" / "It set the base"; "damage … base damage x (Strength x 0.028) + base"; "delay / 10"; "move Duel Wield … into skills instead of ability"). ENGINE: RACES {human: base str 50 sta 55 wis 60 int 60 dex 55}, both CLASSES bases = the Human base (a 2nd race needs the base looked up by race too); OLD_BASE + progNorm keep every point spent above the old class bases (generalises OLD_WAR_STR). Effects count from the base, so only the blade changes: STR_MULT .028, bladeDmg(base,str)=round(base×(1+.028×str)) — Training Sword 4 @ 50 → 10 for BOTH classes (Ranger melee 4 → 10). ITEMS delay 24 (tenths), strikeOf uses delay/10. SAVE v5 = ROSTER {v:5, cur, heroes:[8 × hero|null]}, hero = {race,name,cls,prog,inv,quests,wallet,skills}; heroState/heroNorm/heroReady/heroName (letters of any script, space - ', 2–16)/rosterState/rosterNorm/rosterFree. rosterNorm migrates v1–v4: the last-played class → slot 1 with the (shared) purse and quests, the other class → slot 2 only if it was ever played, both UNNAMED (named on first play). PAGE: the class row is gone → hero bar (#hbName, #hbSub 'Human Warrior · level N', 'characters' button). #heroes select: 8 slots (play/resume · ✕ delete asks twice: 'delete for good?' delete/keep, focus on keep), '+ new hero' on empty slots, 'back to NAME'; #create creator: 1 Race (Human card lists its stats) · 2 Name (#crName maxlength 16, errors 'give your hero a name — 2 to 16 letters' / 'you already have a hero called X') · 3 Class; an unnamed old hero → 'Name your hero', class fixed. Boot: select if any hero, else the creator. menu blocks tick + game keys; Escape: creator → select → forest (only with a named hero); Tab trapped; menus close bag/shop/trainer. H = current hero (or an unsaved SCRATCH hero behind the menus — saveP stores only when HI points at a roster hero). start(seed, cls) still works: a class picks that class's first hero (the suites rely on it). Start over resets only the current hero (name kept). Names show on the health bar, target frame and your chat lines. Dual Wield row moved from Ability to the skills list (val 'level 13' / 'learned'). Sword card: 'every 2.40 s (delay 24)'. Tests: eng1100 49/49, probe1100 58/58 ×3; eng199 15/15, eng19/193/194/196/192/191/194b unchanged; probe199 with the list move 22/22; probe192/195/196/197 unchanged. By design: eng198 42/54, eng17 56/61, eng 116/138 (old small stat numbers, delay 2.4, 1 per 8 Strength), probe193 12/15, probe194_196 27/30, probe198 27/30 (Ranger 10 a hit), probe17_w2 (no class row), compat (class row + v4 save reads).
- v1.9.9 (2026-09-25, feature 27498e8da, stamped 'build v1.9.9 · 2026-09-25 · 27498e8da'): TRAINERS BY CLASS + DUAL WIELD per fannar22 ("I want Ranger Trainer to train Ranger only Class and Warrior Trainer only train Warrior"; "add Duel Wield to be trained from Bob for Warrior For Ranger … by enzo"; "Warrior to learn Duel Wield at level 13 and for Ranger Level 10"; "nothing once you reach the level, just … skill points" → "1 skill point"). ENGINE: TRAINER_OF {warrior:'Bob', ranger:'Enzo'}; DUAL {sp:1, lv:{warrior:13, ranger:10}}; learnDual(p,cls,skills,who) → why learned|class|level(+lv)|points, spends p.sp and sets skills.dual (saved with SAVE.skills[cls]; wieldOk/dualGate from v1.9.8 then let the second blade stay). Every shared skill already has by/also, so Bob offers a Warrior all five skills (Archery and Dodge included) and Enzo offers a Ranger Offense, Defense, Archery and Dodge. PAGE: the other class's trainer still shows the list, but #skNote reads 'Enzo trains Rangers only — your trainer is Bob, by the well' (and the mirror), every +1/+5 is disabled with that reason, and trainSk refuses it too; a Dual Wield row under each trainer's own ability (Warriors only / Rangers only, level, 1 skill point, 'reach level 13 first (you are level 4)', learned), learnDualUI with Bob's "two blades, then. keep the second one low." / Enzo's "light feet, two edges. don't trip over either."; dualMsg() names your own trainer and level. Stun Punch and Pinning Arrow keep their old rules (asked: should Stun Punch be Warrior-only?). Tests: eng199 15/15, probe199 22/22 ×3; eng198 54/54, eng196/192/eng unchanged. By design: probe198 29/30 (the Secondary message now names Bob and level 13), probe192 34/43 (a Warrior training Archery at Enzo's).
- v1.9.8 (2026-09-25, feature 1079def10, stamped 'build v1.9.8 · 2026-09-25 · 1079def10'): WEAPON DAMAGE + DELAY, 50 STRENGTH, DUAL WIELD GATE per fannar22 ("Training Sword should have 4 damage", "let do 24 delay for every 2.4 seconds", "I want warrior to have 50 Strength stats … try to figure out how 4 damage get up to 10 damage per hit" → liked "every 8 Strength = +1"; "you need duel wield before putting anything in secondary slot" / "but it can hold a shield"). ENGINE: ITEMS sword_w/sword_r {dmg:4, delay:2.4}; STR_PER_DMG 8; strikeOf(id,p,cls) = blade dmg + floor(str/8) + floor(Offense/5), delay − .05 s per Intelligence over the class base (floor .3) → Warrior 4+6 = 10 every 2.4 s, Ranger 4 every 2.4 s. Only with makeRun opts.gear (the page); without it the class strike (8/6 every .6 s) stays so old suites keep their numbers. Warrior base str 4 → 50; statCap(k,cls) = max(10, base+6) (a flat cap of 10 would pin a Warrior at 50 forever); progNorm lifts an old Warrior save to 50 + whatever it had spent over 4 (6 → 52). effectText/skillText take (p,cls,gear,bladeId): sheet 'strike 10 (4 blade + 6)' / 'strike every 2.40 s'. DUAL WIELD: skills.dual (skillNorm, off, nothing teaches it yet); wieldOk(id,sl,sk) = fits && not (blade in Secondary without dual); dualGate(inv,sk) at makeRun (gear only) moves a Secondary blade to an empty Primary, else to the bag (full bag: it stays, but the +2 two-blade bonus also needs dual) → S.gated 'primary'|'bag'|null, the page says so at start. invMove(inv,from,to) moves worn gear slot to slot (the target's item swaps back if it fits, else to the bag; refused if the bag is full). PAGE: slotOk() gates click (a bag sword picks Primary, never Secondary), drag (Secondary does not light up, a drop says 'a blade in Secondary needs Dual Wield — Secondary holds a shield for now'), worn→worn drag (new) and the panel's 'wear in … / move to …' buttons (shown only when there is a real choice). Panel blade line 'damage 10 a strike (4 blade + 6 from Strength[ + N from Offense]) · every 2.40 s', '+2 … (needs Dual Wield)'. Strike button (#tAtk) gets a conic ring (--cd = atkCd / melee().cd, 1/40 steps) that clears clockwise as the blade recovers. Warrior card '50 Strength: 10 a hit'. Tests: eng198 54/54, probe198 30/30 ×3. By design: eng.test 117/138 (baseline, v1 save str 4), eng17 60/61, eng192 42/44 (str 6 kept → 52), eng196 74/76 (sword in Secondary without dual), probe196 31/38 (second sword into Secondary), probe197 26/28 (strike 8/.6 s), compat 91/107 (the Knight fixture chains off 'strike 8'); probe19/191/192/193/194_196/195/17_w2_196 unchanged.
- v1.9.7 (2026-09-25, feature 6d2f3b689, stamped 'build v1.9.7 · 2026-09-25 · 6d2f3b689'): item stats panel per fannar22 (B: a panel under the bag, for bag items AND worn slots; A: show what Mira pays). #itemInfo (aria-live polite) sits under #slots with a quiet idle hint; hover (mouse pointerenter) or keyboard focus shows name · slots it fits · in the bag / worn, then lines: blades 'damage <S.mods.atkDmg> a strike · every <atkCd> s' + '+2 with a blade in each hand' (blades have NO damage of their own yet — it's the hero's strike), bows the hero's bow numbers (class bow or WAR_BOW), armor 'defence +N — every blow hits N less', shield 'Shift raises it for blockT s' (Ranger: can't block), stacks 'N in this stack (up to 100) · 4c each', and 'Mira pays X' / 'class kit — Mira won't buy it'. Touch/pen: first tap shows stats + 'tap again to wear it / take it off' (tapFirst via el.dataset.pt from pointerdown; tapKey separate from infoKey because focus fires on tap); mouse click and keyboard Enter still act at once. After wear/takeOff the panel follows the item (infoKey 's'+slot / 'b'+index). Suites: probe197 28/28 ×3; probe196 38/38, probe195 28/28, probe193 15/15, probe194_196 29/30, probe19 45/47, probe191 35/38, probe192 38/43 (all = v1.9.6).
- v1.9.6 (2026-09-25, feature b0034e670, stamped 'build v1.9.6 · 2026-09-25 · b0034e670'): gear slots per fannar22. Six slots: primary (internal key still 'weapon' for saves; SLOT_NAMES maps it) · secondary · range · ammo · chest · legs. The Training Sword fits Primary or Secondary (ITEMS.fits); Secondary holds a shield (Shift blocks) or a second blade (+OFFHAND_DMG 2 a strike when both hold blades); without a shield Shift ROLLS for a Warrior too. A bow in Range needs arrows in Ammo (run.bowNow(); a Warrior without Archery shoots WAR_BOW stats); each shot spends one arrow (useArrow: '5 arrows left', refills from a bag stack, 'out of arrows — Mira sells them at 4c each'). Arrows stack to 100 (inv v:3 adds cnt[8] + ammoN; invStack is all-or-nothing: Ammo slot, bag stacks, empty Ammo, empty bag). Mira's shelf: leggings, breastplate, studded, Wooden Shield 50c, Bow 1g, 1 arrow 4c (bought one at a time). STARTER KIT (fannar22: "Warrior and Ranger only start with Training Sword"): both classes start with ONLY the Training Sword — no shield, bow or arrows; old saves keep what they carry and get nothing new (an old Ranger keeps Ranger's Short Sword; invNorm keeps sword_r for a Ranger who holds one so no Training Sword sneaks in on reload). Enzo's archery test waits for a bow (fannar22 chose A): a Ranger with no usable bow at Enzo stays 'locked' and hears ENZO.nobow once per visit; questText(q,cls,knightOn,noBow) says 'get a Bow (Mira, 1 gold) and arrows, then walk to Enzo…'. Gear rules are opt-in (makeRun opts.gear, page passes it): without gear bowNow/shieldOn stay class-based so old suites keep their numbers. Hero meshes shieldL / sword2 / bowB follow the slots. Suites: eng196 76/76, probe196 38/38 ×3 (starts bare, buys the shield in Mira's window, drags a 2nd sword into Secondary, buys the Bow + 3 arrows, shoots dry, Ranger ammo take-off/wear). BY DESIGN: eng.test 119/138 (same as v1.9.5), eng18 31/32 (Bob's shield line), eng192 43/44 (archery text), eng194b 31/35 (inv v3, Ranger's Training Sword, shelf of 6); probe17_w2 20/32 (fresh Ranger has no bow → Enzo waits; probe17_w2_196 with a bow = 28/32 like v1.9.5); probe_194compat 25/31 (?knight: no bow/shield → the Knight never falls → the probe throws on F.fx; probe_194compat_196 with the kit = 95/107, only the 3-button shelf check differs); probe194_196 29/30 (shelf count).
- v1.9.5 (2026-09-25, feature 437ba619c, stamped 'build v1.9.5 · 2026-09-25 · 437ba619c'): per fannar22 "change right click to left click" (fannar22 picked A: quick click selects, drag turns the camera). clickPick(x,y,r,ts,clear) is the one pick routine for right-click, left click and tap; a left pointerdown→pointerup under 6 px of travel (any point of the path — lc.far) and .4 s is a click (mouse slop 36 px, touch/pen tap 56 px); a second click on the same character within .5 s opens Bob's/Enzo's skill window or Mira's stall (shares rcLast with right-click). Left click / tap on empty ground KEEPS the target (Esc or right-click on nothing clears it) so turning the camera mid-fight never drops it. Right-click and touch long-press unchanged. Words: canvas label, help line (click · touch: tap · double-click Mira…), Enzo's two lines ("double-click me"). Suites: probe195 28/28 ×3 (click/double-click Bob and Mira, slow clicks, sky keeps target, drag turns camera, drag out-and-back is not a click, 3 px wobble is, .5 s hold is not, right-click still targets/clears, right-button press alone does nothing, Skeleton lv 1, monster double-click opens nothing, tap slop vs mouse slop at a searched spot 46 px off Enzo, touch long-press, words, no errors). All engine suites + probes at their baselines; BY DESIGN probe19 45/47 (a 100 ms touch on Bob is now a tap that targets him; help line text).
- v1.9.4 (2026-09-25, feature 06ed17fd4, stamped 'build v1.9.4 · 2026-09-25 · 06ed17fd4'): per fannar22. SKELETON ("a new Monster name Skeleton", "make the skeleton a level 1"): MOBS.skeleton lv 1 (8 hp, dmg 2, speed 2.6, period 1.5, 1 xp), DROPS 1–3c, SKELETONS=4 placed AFTER goblins/wolves (world identical for them: 200/200 seeds); skeletonModel() bone-white; every MOBS entry has lv (goblin 2, wolf 1, skeleton 1 — wolf level asked to chat, A 1 / B 2, no answer yet) shown as '· lv N' via .tname[data-lv]::after + the target aria-label. RESPAWN ("all Monster to respawn every 30 seconds"): killMob sets m.back=30; runs with opts.respawn (page passes it; engine suites don't) call rise(m) → home spot, full hp, calm; waits while the hero is within RESPAWN_CLEAR=6 of the spot; the Knight never respawns; respawned goblins count for Bob's quest. TRAINING SWORD: ITEMS.sword_w renamed (Ranger's Short Sword unchanged). REAL BAG ("when item is equipped it remove the item from the bag… removed… dragged to be in character inventory"): inv.v=2; slots = carried only, equip = worn; invEquip(inv,id,i) swaps the worn item into bag slot i; invUnequip(inv,slot,i) → slot i or first free, refuses on a full bag; invNorm migrates pre-v2 bags once (removes the worn copy), never two class swords, class sword never lost; buy() into an empty slot = worn at once (works with a full bag); Bob's breastplate worn if chest empty; sell() no longer has the 'equipped' case. Page: equip slots are role=button (click/Enter/drag to bag = take off; tabIndex only when worn; .worn solid border); bag items click/drag = wear; wear()/takeOff() helpers + messages. START OVER ("reset all player back to level 1"): the old 'forget progression' button → 'start over', two clicks (arms 5 s), wipes the save and starts a fresh forest at once (per-browser; I can't reset other players). MIRA: SHOP leggings, breastplate (price 60 = 6s, fannar22 confirmed), studded. BUG caught in testing: ring-scale ternary read S.mobs[undefined].kind for NPC targets — fixed with the ti.ref.t==='mob' guard. Suites: eng194 32/32, eng194b 35/35, probe194 30/30 ×3. BY DESIGN: eng.test 119/138 (+10 bag/rename/shop lines 71/88/93/96/100/124/141/143/145/146), eng191 27/31 (sword not in the bag / worn leggings not listed), probe191 35/38, probe192 38/43 (row index), old ?knight probe: use probe_194compat.js (two-click wipe + worn-not-in-bag adaptations) → 96/107, all 11 by design.
- v1.9.3 (2026-09-25, feature 8a592c72a, stamped 'build v1.9.3 · 2026-09-25 · 8a592c72a'): HEALTH BY LEVEL per fannar22 ("Level 1 warrior have 30 Health, level 2 have 45 Health and level 3 60 Health"; "level 1 Ranger Health be 20, Level 2 35, and level 3 for 45"). CLASSES gain hpLv tables + hpPerLevel (Warrior [30,45,60] +15 after, Ranger [20,35,45] +10 after, the last step asked for); CLASSES.hp is now the level-1 value (30 / 20). effects.maxHp = levelHp(cls,level) + 4×Stamina + Defense/2. The page calls run.retrain(P) on a level-up so the new health lands at once (damage taken stays taken, a fallen hero isn't revived) and the level-up line says '+15 max health (45)'. TRAINERS: "Ranger Trainer to train Defense, and Offense" + "Warrior to teach Dodge and Archery" → SKILLS.offense/defense also:'Enzo', archery/dodge also:'Bob'; the skill window lists by===who||also===who (Bob 5 rows, Enzo 4), same price. Block stays Bob's alone. Suites: eng193 21/21, probe193 15/15 ×3 widths. - v1.9.2 (2026-09-25, feature f2614ab71, stamped 'build v1.9.2 · 2026-09-25 · f2614ab71'): SKILL WINDOW per fannar22 ("when you double right click on Trainer I want it to bring up a skill window you can train in and you have like 10 allocation points given every level"; "in place of them" = instead of stat points; "I want to warrior to train Dodge, and Archery, along with Ranger Trainer"). ENGINE (eng192 44/44): `SP_PER_LEVEL` 10 → `p.sp`; `p.sk` {offense, defense, block, archery, dodge}; `SKILLS` (Bob: Offense +1 strike / 5, Defense +1 max hp / 2, Block +.01 s shield / pt, Warriors only; Enzo: Archery +1 bow dmg / 5 and a Warrior's first point buys `WAR_BOW` {range 5, dmg 2, cd 1.3}, Dodge .5 % / pt to slip any blow, max 25 %) — cross-class at the SAME price (poll A/B open, default A); `skillCap(level)` = min(50, 5 + 5·level); `trainSkill(p,cls,key,n)` → why skill/class/points/cap; `skillText`. effects() adds the skill terms + dodgeChance; step's bow branches use `M.bowRange>0` instead of `C.bow`; Dodge rolls on its own seeded stream `mulberry32(seed^0x0d06e)` (the world is unchanged) in the goblin/wolf AND Knight blow branches ("you sidestep the … (Dodge)", counts in S.dodged); `run.retrain(p)` recomputes M at once (Defense also adds the gained health). MIGRATION: progNorm — a sheet without `sk` (saved before v1.9.2) gets sp = (level−1)·10; stats already spent are kept; a Ranger never keeps Block; save stays v4. gainXp still counts the old p.points (so old saves/suites read the same) but the page no longer spends them. PAGE: `#skillWin` (class "inv skillwin", labelled dialog) — double right-click Bob/Enzo or 'train' on their target frame; title Bob's yard / Enzo's range, note near/far · points · cap, one row per skill (value/cap, what it does now + per-point rule, +1 / +5, disabled with the reason: walk up / no points — every level brings 10 / at the level-N cap / Warriors only), the trainer's ability row (Stun Punch / Pinning Arrow, the same rules as the character window's village row), a switch to the other trainer's list (look anywhere, train only beside the trainer), close. Refreshes itself from hud (skSig), keeps focus on the button used, Esc closes it first, one window at a time with the character window and Mira's stall, a new run closes it. The character sheet: the stat + buttons and keys 5–9 are gone (CSS `.stat .raise{display:none}`, all disabled), `#skSum` lists the skills + cap, the header counts skill points; a level-up says '+N skill points — train with Bob or Enzo'. Enzo's lines: the Pinning Arrow stays a Ranger lesson but "Archery and Dodge I teach anyone" (kept the 'I train Rangers' prefix + 'Rangers only' in the button title). Tests: probe192 43/43 ×3 widths; probe19 / probe191 now also close the skill window after their quick right-clicks on Bob → 47/47, 38/38; eng 135/138 (same 3), eng17 61/61, eng18 32/32, eng19 28/28, eng191 31/31, probe18 39/39. BY DESIGN (stat points removed): probe17 28/32 (key 9 / + button / Dex-save checks) and old ?knight 101/107 (the 2 declared + key 6, + button, 'three points' text, the levelled 48 hp hero). Screens skill-1200/390 reviewed.
- v1.9.1 (2026-09-25, feature ddea61593, stamped 'build v1.9.1 · 2026-09-25 · ddea61593'): MIRA'S STALL + FULL-TIME REGEN per fannar22 ("double right click on a Merchant it bring up what merchant sell, and it can also buy items"; "Mira shouldn't buy item back at full price, make it 50 percent"; "regain health every 2 second per 1 health tick" / "keep it full time regen"; "all that to go into version 1.9" → numbered 1.9.x). ENGINE (eng191 31/31): `SELL_RATE` .5, `itemValue(id)` = price or `ITEM_VALUE` (the quest breastplate never had a price → 60, she pays 30), `sellPrice` floors; `run.sell(i)` refuses in order empty → class (class swords never sell) → far (nearMira, talkR 1.6) → equipped (the ONLY worn copy; a spare of the same item sells) → worthless; a sale clears the slot, pays, and says 'Mira: "… half what it fetches new." (purse … · sold)', which lands in the LOOT tab. REGEN {every:2,hp:1}: in step after `var h=S.hero,k=S.knight;`, only when makeRun opts.regen (the page passes {knight:KNIGHT,regen:true}; every older suite runs without it), only while 0 < hp < max, the clock resets at full or at 0 (no banked regen, the fallen stay fallen); step clamps dt to .1, so tests step in tenths. PAGE: `#shopWin` (class "inv shopwin", a labelled dialog) shares the character window's corner (opening one closes the other): note near/far + purse, For sale (buy · price, disabled with the reason: walk up / you need … / bag full), Sell to Mira (every bag item; class sword 'not for sale', the worn only copy 'take it off first'), close. It refreshes itself from hud when nearMira / purse / bag / equip change (shopSig) and keeps focus on the button you used. Opens by DOUBLE RIGHT-CLICK on the same character within 500 ms, timed by e.timeStamp (a heavy desktop frame between two clicks ate a 450 ms Date.now window in headless), or the new `.tgtact` button in the target frame: 'trade' on Mira, 'train' on Bob/Enzo (opens the character window until the v1.9.2 skill window); none on mobs/self. Esc closes the stall first (target kept); a new run closes it. The frame now shrinks on 320 px phones (max-width 100%, the name ellipsises, the bar min 2 rem) — the trade button had pushed it 21 px past the view. Tests: probe191 38/38 ×3 runs ×3 widths; probe19 needed `F.toggleInv(false)` after its rapid near-miss right-clicks on Bob (they are now a double right-click = intended) → 47/47 on v1.9 and v1.9.1; unchanged eng 135/138, eng17 61/61, eng18 32/32, eng19 28/28, probe17 32/32, probe18 39/39, old ?knight 105/107. Screens shop-1200/390 reviewed.
- v1.9 (2026-09-25): TARGET WINDOW per fannar22 ("right click on like a player or a NPC it comes up in target window"; "Just show their Health, no Level"). ENGINE (pure, eng19 28/28): `targetInfo(S,ref)` with ref {t:'self'} · {t:'npc',n:'Bob'|'Mira'|'Enzo'} · {t:'mob',i} · {t:'knight'} → {name, react self/friendly/hostile, hp, maxHp, dead, x, z, dist}; villagers can't be hurt, so `NPC_HP` Bob 50 / Mira 30 / Enzo 36 is a fixed display value (always full), not a rule; mobs capitalised (Goblin 12, Wolf 9), overkill reads 0; the Knight only with ?knight. `targetList` = living within 20 (`TARGET_RANGE`), foes first then villagers, nearest first, never self; `targetNext` cycles and wraps; `targetKey`; reads only (asserted). PAGE: `.bar.tgt` right after your own health bar (name, bar, hp text, a 44 px ✕), edge red/green/gold by reaction, a foe's bar red; "(you)" on self; aria-label "Target: Bob · friend · health 50/50" + the status line announces it. Picking = three.js Raycaster through the pointer against the character groups (hero, trainer, merchant, enzo, visible mobs, Knight), walking up parents (Mira's stall counts as Mira); a near miss takes the character whose middle is nearest on screen within 36 px (56 px for touch). Right-click (contextmenu, always preventDefault) picks; right-click on nothing clears; touch long-press 500 ms (moves >10 px or an early lift cancel; the browser's own long-press contextmenu within 900 ms is swallowed); T cycles, Esc clears (both before the focused-button return, and the INPUT return keeps the chat box safe); ✕ clears. A ring (RingGeometry .74–1.04, +.12 above the ground, red/green/gold, 1.25× under a wolf, 1.4× under the Knight) follows the target; a fallen target shows 'defeated' struck through for 2 s of game time, no ring, then clears. A new forest / class switch clears it. PHONES: `#view` is 3:4 (max 80vh) under 40 rem, bars 4.5 rem, tighter HUD gaps — the HUD rows and the touch stick/buttons no longer overlap (the v1.3–v1.8 crowding TODO). Tests: probe19 47/47 ×3 widths; unchanged: eng 135/138, eng17 61/61, eng18 32/32, probe17 32/32, probe18 39/39, old probe ?knight 105/107. Screenshots tgt-1200/390 reviewed (the first ring was too thin to see behind the goblin → bolder + higher).
- v1.8 (2026-09-24): CHAT WINDOW per fannar22 ("NPC talks, player talks and damages from enemy", "scrollable", "also put in what loot you get"). Every engine line of a tick now reaches a scrollable log (the HUD used to show only the LAST line of a tick — shield lines vanished), game-clock stamps, 200-line cap, a 'new lines' button when you have scrolled back, fold button. Pure engine half: chatChannel (by WORDS — kind 'hit' is both your blows and theirs): npc / you / dmg (hits you for, shield line, boss warnings) / hit (your blows, dodges, training) / loot (coin drops, Bob/Enzo rewards, Mira receipts — still bolded as the speaker) / sys; npcNear (hearing range 3.2) + npcReply keyword answers for Bob (goblins count, dummy, Stun Punch, shield), Mira (real prices, your purse, no haggling), Enzo (test count, Pinning Arrow, Dexterity; Warriors sent to Bob) — they only TALK, asserted to change nothing. Player talk = LOCAL typing (no network; other players are not in this game) — chose this without an answer from chat and said so. Tabs all · talk · combat · loot. Enter opens the input, Esc/send returns to the canvas; the keydown handler now returns for INPUT first (typing c/i/v/wasd used to fire game keys). Desktop (52rem+ fine pointer): translucent overlay bottom-left, the big caption visually hidden (still the aria-live line); phones: panel under the view. LESSON (+): headless_shell reports NO fine pointer, so a 1200px probe silently tests the phone layout — test desktop-only CSS with a copy whose `and (pointer:fine)` is swapped for `and (min-width:0)`, and let the probe detect the layout from computed style. eng18 32/32, probe18 39/39 ×3 + 41/41 desktop-forced; old suites unchanged (eng 135/138, eng17 61/61, probe17 32/32, old probe 105/107).
- v1.7 (2026-09-24): DEXTERITY · ENZO THE RANGER TRAINER + PINNING ARROW · CURSED KNIGHT BEHIND A SWITCH, per fannar22 ("Dex, adding Enzo, and removing cursed Knight will be version 1.7"; "Enzo is a Ranger Trainer"; "cursed knight removed from the game before chat window"). DEX: fifth stat, 3D only (the turn-based game keeps four), baselines Ranger 4 / Warrior 2 (fannar22 asked mid-build that Warriors get it too — they do): +1 bow damage and −0.05 s off the Shift cooldown (dodge OR shield, floor 0.7) per point over the baseline; INT keeps its bow bonus so no existing Ranger gets weaker; key 9; save stays v4 (progNorm fills a missing dex with the baseline, so an old save's effects are byte-identical — tested per class). ENZO: stands at (−19.2, −4.6), his archery target (straw butt with white/red/gold rings) 2.9 units off; a Ranger who walks up starts his test (three arrows into the target from beyond sword reach, 2.2–6 units; the target only takes the arrow when nothing hostile is nearer; inside sword reach Enzo says why it does not count), then two silver, then a one-time offer of the PINNING ARROW (three silver; every third arrow that LANDS pins its target 1 s, the Knight 0.6 s). Shooting the third arrow from beside him pays on the spot. Warriors who walk up are sent to Bob once per visit and cannot learn it. quests gain enzo (locked/aim/return/done) + arrows; skills gain pinArrow. KNIGHT: `makeRun(..., opts)` — `opts.knight===false` = a Knight at 0 hp flagged absent (never targeted, drawn or ticked); no opts keeps him, so every older suite runs verbatim; the page passes `{knight:KNIGHT}` where KNIGHT = `?knight` in the address (the _bar wrapper forwards the query string, so the switch works on sloppy.live too). Start / rest / lose texts, the last quest line, meta + OG description + OG image prompt, header and help line no longer point at him. BUG FIXED ON THE WAY: the Knight HP bar's own `.bar` display rule beat `[hidden]` since v1.3 — it showed permanently (0/60 with him gone); the screenshot caught it, the attribute-only probe never did → `.hud .bar[hidden]{display:none}` + computed-style asserts. TESTS: old eng 135/138 (3 declared migrations: STAT_KEYS, progNorm shape, class baselines — all 'dex added'), new eng17 61/61 (Dex effects/cooldowns/old-save equality, Knight off vs on, Enzo flow both paths, range rules, warrior redirect, trainPin refusals, Pinning Arrow cadence on mobs and Knight), old probe run WITH ?knight 105/107 ×3 (2 declared: 'four stat rows' → five), new probe17 32/32 ×3 (no Knight drawn/bar/text, head texts, Dex row + key 9 + save, Enzo + target in the scene facing each other, full archery test through the real keys, button gating/titles, Warrior redirect, switch on brings him back and the bar really hides/shows). Suites: scratchpad f3 (eng.test.js, eng17.test.js, probe.js, probe17.js, run17.sh [PROBE=… Q=…], shot17.sh). Screenshots 1200 + 390 reviewed; the 390 HUD crowding is the same as v1.6 (not a regression; Ranger quest line trimmed anyway).
- v1.6 (2026-09-22): OVER-THE-SHOULDER CAMERA · MOUSE / FINGER LOOK · DAY-NIGHT LIGHTING per two chat asks merged ("over-the-shoulder third-person camera for the low-poly forest village, keyboard movement, preserved NPC chat interactions" + "low-poly forest village with over-shoulder camera, keyboard and mouse movement, day-night lighting"). CAMERA: `cam={dist 5.5, pitch .3, side ±1, hold, drag}` — the camera sits `dist·cos(pitch)` behind and `dist·sin(pitch)` above the hero, offset 1.1 units to one shoulder, looking 2.5 units past them; terrain-clamped. `camYaw` is unchanged as the yaw camera-relative walking uses, so a mouse/finger DRAG on the canvas (0.006 rad/px yaw, 0.004 rad/px pitch, clamped −0.15…0.9) steers where W goes; a drag holds the view 1.5 s, then it eases back behind the hero as before (the old auto-follow, untouched). Wheel zooms 3.5…9, V swaps the shoulder (status line says which). The touch stick and the strike/dodge buttons keep their own handlers (they stop propagation before the canvas). NPC chat preserved: questTick's Bob lines, the village window (train / Mira's stall) and the character window are untouched — the existing 94 probe checks run unchanged. DAY / NIGHT: pure `dayState(t)` in the engine (`DAY_LEN` 240 s from dawn; sun elevation = sin, daylight smooth-clamped; sky/fog colour lerped night→dusk→day; sun colour warms through dawn; `sunI`, `hemiI`, village `lamp` brightening at night; labels dawn/day/dusk/night by elevation + rising/setting half) — the renderer applies it every frame to background, fog, hemisphere, sun (colour, intensity, position) and the well lamp; the HUD gets a `sky` chip. Engine byte-identical apart from that pure block (apply asserts it). TESTS: eng 138/138 (+7: period, dawn at 0, noon/midnight extremes, low-sun bands, day/night ordering of every light, ranges, continuity ≤0.06 per half second), probe 107/107 ×3 (+13: camera ≈5 units behind + above + right-shoulder offset, a 100 px drag turns 0.6 rad and holds, W walks along the turned view, vertical drag tilts, wheel zoom + both stops, V swaps sides with a message, the view eases back, noon vs midnight: HUD label, sun/lamp/sky/fog numbers AND a rendered ground-band pixel sum darker at night). Screenshots 1200 + 390 reviewed. Night sky deepened once (.05/.07/.14 → .03/.045/.10) because the first cut was barely darker than the forest-green day.
- v1.5 (2026-09-20): BOB · FOUR-TIER PURSE · MIRA THE GEAR MERCHANT · LEGS SLOT · STUN PUNCH · CONTINUOUS PLAY + STOP ADVENTURE — one batch for six chat requests. HONESTY FIRST: chat called the currency "confirmed" and asked for Bob's "exact quest dialogue" — neither existed anywhere (no design note, no script; the trainer was unnamed). Everything below is THIS app's own design, stated as such in the engine comment. Economy: `COIN` c/s/g/p at 10:1, purse = ONE integer in copper (`coinText` → "1p 2g 3s 4c"), shared by both classes in the v4 save; drops seeded per run (`DROPS` goblin 2–4c, wolf 1s, Knight 1g). Bob (named; `BOB` lines) pays the Leather Breastplate + 5 silver (`QUEST_SILVER`), then offers the Stun Punch ONCE after the reward moment (`rewardFx===0`) and sells it for 3s (`train()`: gated on quest done, distance, coin; never twice). Stun Punch: unarmed only, every 3rd landed punch (`S.punches`, `TRAIN.every`) staggers the target 1.2 s (Knight 0.8 s): `stun` timer, no movement, no bite/blow, visual wobble. Mira (`VILLAGE.merchant`, moved off the spawn radius after the first fixture spawned inside her talk circle) sells Leather Leggings (NEW `legs` slot, armor 1, 4s) and a Studded Breastplate (chest, armor 3, 1g 5s) via `buy()` (stock, distance, coin, bag space; auto-equips an empty slot). `EQUIP_SLOTS` + `isEquipped` + summed `armorOf` replace the weapon/chest special-casing; `invNorm` drops an item saved in the wrong slot. Continuous play: `knightFalls()` sets `knightDown`, banks 10 xp + 1g and returns to explore — NO overlay; xp now lands on EVERY kill in hud (same gainXp rules, glow per level); `stop()` (the HUD "Stop Adventure" button) ends the run as won / rested (never a fake win) with a summary (kills, dodges, coin earned, "everything is kept"); a fall still ends it. Saves: v4 = v3 + `wallet` + `skills{warrior,ranger}`; v1→v4 migrations verified; the 2D key still read once, never written. Character window gains a Village row (train button + Mira's stock, live-gated with title reasons) and the legs drop. TESTS: eng 131/131 (was 89; FIVE declared migrations: "the last strike wins"/"a won run is frozen"/"killing strike wins" → knightDown + continuous + stop(), and v3 → v4), probe 94/94 ×3 widths (was 75; migrations: win overlay → no overlay until Stop Adventure, glow/xp on knightDown, v4 save, stamp regex). Screenshots 1200 + 390 reviewed (Stop Adventure + purse in the HUD, Mira with her stall by the huts).
- v1.4 (2026-09-19): PUNCH — the unarmed basic attack per chat. With no weapon in the slot every melee strike is a punch: 3 damage + the Strength bonus (floor 1), 0.45 s cooldown (faster than the 0.6 s sword), same 2.2 reach, "you punch …" / "your fist meets air"; the hero's sword mesh hides while unarmed and returns on re-equip; the Ranger's bow never needed the sword. Unequipping keeps the sword in its bag slot; equipping mid-run switches the very next strike back to the sword numbers. The equipment line reads "— (unarmed: Punch, N dmg)". Tests: eng 89/89 (armed/unarmed switch, punch damage + shorter cooldown, re-equip mid-run, Strength adds to the punch, fist-not-blade miss outside the leash with the cooldown over, bow independent), probe 75/75 ×3 (click-unequip in the character window → punch 3 + hidden sword, drag the sword back onto the weapon slot → strike 8 with the sword drawn). Also answered in the v1.3 note: there is no action bar or copper in this app — nothing to verify.
- v1.3 (2026-09-19): VILLAGE · TRAINER · TUTORIAL · MOBS · TEN-GOBLIN QUEST · CHARACTER WINDOW — one batch for seven chat requests (village spawn, warrior trainer + movement tutorial, glowing straw dummy, low-poly wolf/goblin models, ten-goblin quest with live progress, eight-slot inventory + chest slot + Leather Breastplate, one character window with C + touch drag-and-drop; Mage deferred). WORLD: the start pad is a sunlit hamlet (pad flattened to 6.5, three huts with cone roofs, a well, a warm point light), the Warrior trainer (figure + shield) and a straw dummy stand inside it; ten goblins (12 hp, bite 4, 2 xp like the 2D bandit) and four wolves (9 hp, bite 3, 1 xp) are seeded in the mid-forest with notice/chase/leash/return AI and 1.2 s / 1.0 s bite timers; the hero's strike and the Ranger's arrow hit the NEAREST target in reach/range. MODELS (verified by scene-graph + bounding boxes in the probe): goblin = squat cylinder body, big head, two cone ears, dagger — shorter than the hero and wider than tall; wolf = long low box body on four leg cylinders, cone head, tail — long axis ≥1.6× its height. QUESTS (pure state machine, persisted): walk to the trainer → walk to the dummy → strike it three times (the straw glows softly while it is the target and flares on each hit) → back to the trainer → "ten goblins" with LIVE `x/10` on the HUD quest line → return → Leather Breastplate (armor 2, auto-equipped into the empty chest slot, never twice; if the bag is full the trainer asks you to make room). Armor subtracts from every blow (floor 1) before the shield halves it. INVENTORY: eight slots, weapon + chest equipment; each class starts with its sword equipped (Iron Sword / Ranger's Short Sword — invNorm re-adds a missing class sword and drops bogus equips); click toggles equip/unequip; touch-friendly DRAG-AND-DROP with pointer capture: a ghost follows the finger, the matching slot highlights, dropping equips, dragging an equipped item back to the bag unequips, a wrong-slot drop is refused; motion is judged by pointer distance (synthetic events have no movementX). ONE CHARACTER WINDOW (`C`, or `I`, or the "character" button): stats rows (moved in from the old sheet, same ids/keys 5–8) + equipment + bag + "forget progression"; on phones (<40 rem) it floats over the viewport at ≥50 % height instead of squeezing into the short stage. SAVE v3 `{v:3, cls, prog:{…}, inv:{warrior, ranger}, quests}` with v1/v2 migration (class swords, fresh quests); the 2D key still read once, never written. Tests: eng 79/79 (village on the flat pad, no trees on it, 10+4 mobs seeded off pad/clearing, full tutorial → hunt → 3/10 → 10/10 → reward once, armor 8→6, inv cap/equip/unequip, wolf bite + leash return, v2→v3 + v3 round-trip), probe 71/71 ×3 widths (village objects + warm light, spawn + quest line, model counts + silhouettes, tutorial via seam incl. glow, live 5/10, dead goblins vanish, reward equipped, C window with four stat rows/equipment/8 slots ≥44 px, click unequip, drag ghost + highlight + drop equips, wrong slot refused, v3 save, new forest keeps gear + quests, 2D key untouched), screenshots 1200/390. NOT IN THIS APP (chat asked to verify them): there is no eight-slot ACTION BAR, no ability cooldown bar and no copper/currency — nothing to verify; say so rather than invent.
- v1.2 (2026-09-19): CLASSES + LEVEL-UP GLOW per chat ("move class selection into the 3D prototype, mirror existing classes and saves, keep the 2D version intact" + "brief golden level-up glow with outward particles, preserving progression"). Warrior (44 hp, strike 8, baselines 4/3/1/1, Shift = shield window 1.2 s that halves the next blow) and Ranger (30 hp, strike 6, bow 6 dmg up to 6 units with its own 0.9 s cooldown, baselines 2/3/3/3, Shift = dodge) — the same names, hit points and stat baselines as the turn-based game; the skills became windows/ranges. Picker above the stage mirrors the 2D pick (aria-pressed, ≥44 px). SAVES: `fantasy-realm-3d-v1` is now `{v:2, cls, prog:{warrior, ranger}}` — a v1 blob migrates onto the Warrior sheet (stats clamped up to the Warrior baseline), both sheets persist, "forget" clears both; the turn-based key `fantasy-realm-v1` is READ ONCE to preselect the class when this app has no save and is never written (asserted in the apply script + probe). Stat effects per class: STR strike +1, STA +4 hp, WIS +0.05 s dodge safety / shield window, INT bow +1 (Ranger) and strike cooldown −0.05 s. LEVEL-UP GLOW: on a level gain, 40 golden points burst outward from the hero (radial velocity + gravity, fading over 1.2 s) with a gold point light, and the character sheet pulses (`.glow` keyframe); reduced-motion keeps only the sheet highlight; seam `__F3.fx`. Hero figure is gold as Warrior, green as Ranger; the bow pose shows on a shot, the sword tints gold while the shield is up. Tests: eng 58/58 (class constants, Warrior block halves to 4 and is spent, Ranger bow range/cooldown/out-of-range, per-class effects text, v1→v2 migration, fallbacks), probe 48/48 ×3 (picker, initialClass precedence 3D save → 2D save → Warrior, class switch = new forest with class hp/baselines, shooting pose, shield tint, glow: 40 particles move outward and fade, gone after 1.2 s, v2 save shape, 2D key never written). SUITE MIGRATIONS DECLARED: the v1.0 encounter tests now run as a Ranger (dodge) and read the class strike from `mods`; the v1.1 baseline numbers moved from a classless 40-hp hero to the Warrior (44 hp, 4/3/1/1).
- v1.1 (2026-09-19): PROGRESSION MIRRORING per chat ("wire XP, level, and progression mirroring into the 3D prototype, preserving saves and the 2D fallback"). Same rules as the turn-based game, copied into this app's pure engine (apps stay self-contained): level L at 3·(L−1) cumulative xp, ONE free point per level, surplus xp + unspent points carry over, four stats capped at 10; the Cursed Knight is worth 10 xp like the 2D boss (so a first win lands level 4 with three points). 3D effects: STR +1 strike damage per point over base, STA +4 max health, WIS +0.05 s of dodge safety, INT −0.05 s strike cooldown (floor 0.3 s); baseline effects equal the v1.0 numbers exactly. Points apply to the NEXT forest (a run snapshots `mods` at makeRun). Persisted under this app's OWN key `fantasy-realm-3d-v1` = `{v:1, prog}` with a clamping loader; the turn-based key `fantasy-realm-v1` is never read or written (asserted by the apply script and the probe), and the 2D game is untouched — it remains the fallback. Character sheet under the stage (level · xp · next threshold · points, four rows with + buttons, keys 5–8, effect text, a "forget progression" button). Tests: eng 44/44 (constants + threshold table identical to 2D, multi-level jump + carryover, cap/refusals, effects, progNorm clamps, makeRun applies mods, killing strike carries 10 xp, no-prog run == v1.0), probe 37/37 ×3 (sheet, win banks 10 xp → level 4/3 points, 3D key written + 2D key untouched, keys + buttons raise, next forest uses the levelled hero, forget clears). FIXTURE LESSONS: pin the world seed in the probe (tree placement decided a walk test once); a probe that wins a run earlier has already banked xp — reset before sheet checks; a seam getter that returns a REPLACED object must be re-read, not captured.
- v1.0 (2026-09-19): built per chat ("a separate 3D Fantasy Realm prototype with an explorable overworld, hero, and one enemy encounter"). Separate app, no code shared with the turn-based Fantasy Realm. Pure `<script id="eng">` (dual-exported): seeded 48×48 world — fbm value-noise terrain flattened on the start pad and in the Knight's clearing, ~150 trees scattered off the pad/clearing/a winding corridor (z = 2·sin(x/6)) and ≥1.6 apart; hero moves 6 u/s (dodge burst 12 u/s for 0.25 s, 0.5 s i-frames, 1.2 s cooldown), circle collision with trunks + bounds, dt clamped to 100 ms; ONE encounter — the Cursed Knight (60 hp) notices you within 9 units, closes at 4.2 u/s, winds up for 0.4 s then hits for 8 every 1.5 s in reach 1.9, leashed 7 units from his clearing, drops aggro past 11 and walks home; your strike does 8 in reach 2.2 with a 0.6 s cooldown; won/lost states freeze the run. Rendering = three.js r128 from cdnjs (the only network use): vertex-coloured flat terrain with a lighter corridor, instanced cone trees, dodecahedron stones ringing the clearing, primitive hero/knight figures (sword swing, halo flashes on hit, wind-up raises the sword), third-person camera behind the hero with yaw smoothing, hemisphere + directional light, fog. Controls: WASD/arrows camera-relative, Space strike, Shift dodge; touch stick + two buttons (hidden on fine-pointer desktops); HUD bars, aria-live state line, won/lost overlay with focus on "new forest". Seam `__F3` (freeze/step/setHero/draw). Tests: eng 31/31 node (seeded world, tree-free pad/clearing/corridor, corridor walk reaches the clearing, speed, tree push-out, bounds, dt clamp, aware/close/wind-up/blow timing, dodge i-frames, strike + cooldown + out-of-reach, leash + return, win, frozen after win, lose), probe 27/27 ×3 widths (WebGL frame painted — mid-height band sampled, camera follows, keys + arrows, touch controls ≥44 px on narrow screens, encounter via seam, strike/cooldown/wind-up sword pose/dodge/touch strike, win overlay + focus + restart, lose overlay, computed-style overlay check, stamp gutter), screenshots 1200/390.

## issues
- + three r128 `cameraPosition` is NOT set for MeshBasicMaterial/Lambert (only Phong/Toon/Standard/Shader or envMap): any onBeforeCompile hook on a basic material that reads it gets (0,0,0). Use an own uniform (WU.uCam). Debug trick that found it: recolour wInk to 0xff00ff in a scratch copy — dark voids that turn pink are ink shells.
- PROBE (2026-09-28): headless swiftshader here often loses the WebGL context ~0.1 s after Start at 900 px+ windows (live build too): the canvas stays black (restore does not bring three r128 back) and some runs hang at the P0 title. Shot probes should listen for webglcontextlost and retry; 800x500 functional probes were fine.
- PROBE: readPixels after F.draw() returns zeros here (no preserveDrawingBuffer), and the world seed is random per page load — pixel-count probes don't work; use screenshots.
- v1.10.1: the run keeps its OWN copy of the sheet (P); a skill-up changes run P and reports through S.ups, which hud() copies into the page's P and saves. Anything else that changes run P mid-fight must report the same way, or the next run.retrain(page P) quietly undoes it. Skill-up rolls use their own stream (sr) — keep it separate so seeded suites stay stable.
- v1.10: the save is a ROSTER (v5) and old probes that read localStorage .prog/.v===4 or click #pick break by design; heroes created by F.start(…,cls) with no roster hero are SCRATCH (never saved) — seed ROSTER.heroes with E.heroState(...) when a test needs persistence.
- The dusk/dawn label must use the rising/setting HALF of the cycle (phase <.25 or >.75 = rising), not `phase<.5` — the first cut called 0.48 'dawn'.
- Mob AI drains `stun` inside the same step that set it, so a fixture reading `k.stun` right after the punch sees 0.75, not 0.8 — assert a range. hud() shows the LAST event of a tick, and a level-up line overrides even that — chat-facing text asserts must accept any of the tick's lines.
- Bob's offer is deliberately NOT in the reward tick (rewardFx must hit 0 first) or it hides the reward line; tests step 1.3 s.
- The `[hidden]` vs own-display trap struck again: `.overlay{display:grid}` kept the end overlay visible (and dimmed the whole scene) while every attribute check passed — the screenshot caught it; the probe now asserts computed display. Rule: any element with its own display rule gets an explicit `[hidden]{display:none}`.
- Fixture lessons: a walk test from the start pad runs into trees — test speed in the tree-free clearing; a dodge ROLLS the hero out of the Knight's reach, so an i-frame test must hold position; the 100 ms dt clamp means "wait 0.7 s" is seven steps, not one; read WebGL pixels from a mid-height band, a corner is often one flat colour.
